# 第 3 册：测试与配置

> 测试是这套系统的**可执行规格**：`test_action_contract.py` 钉返回值契约，`test_sign_evidence.py` 钉「什么算签到成功」，`test_unified_steps.py` 钉 type 层等价性。
> 配置是这套系统的**程序**：运行期没有任何 App 特判代码，34 份 YAML 就是全部差异。

## 2. 配置模板

### `config.example.yaml`（3262 行）

真实 `config.yaml` 不入包（含 VLM api_key）。注意：模板内嵌了一份**陈旧的 apps 列表**（只有 1 个 App），派生可运行配置时要砍掉它并改用 `apps_dir: config/apps`，否则 34 份 App 配置会被 `load_config` 全部忽略。

```yaml
# ============================================================
# config.example.yaml — 配置模板（可安全提交）
#
# 用法：复制成 config.yaml 再填自己的值。config.yaml 已在 .gitignore 里，
#       因为 vision.vlm.api_key 是真实密钥。
#       Key 也可以用环境变量 VLM_KEY 提供（优先级高于本文件）。
# 提交前自查：python devtools/check_secrets.py --staged
# ============================================================

device:
  serial: '192.168.1.100:5555'   # 改成你的设备
  screen_w: 1080
  screen_h: 2340
human:
  enabled: true
  wake_window:
    start: 07:30
    end: '23:00'
  run_jitter_seconds:
  - 0
  - 600
  delay_between_actions:
  - 0.6
  - 2.2
  delay_after_open:
  - 3.0
  - 6.0
  think_delay:
  - 1.5
  - 4.0
  swipe_duration:
  - 0.5
  - 0.9
  tap_jitter_px: 12
  wander_probability: 0.06
  scroll_probability: 0.5
  use_swipe_home: false
trajectory:
  enabled: true
  px_per_step: 15
  steps_min: 20
  steps_max: 60
  duration_per_px: 0.0007
  duration_min: 0.3
  duration_max: 1.5
  perpendicular_ratio: 0.12
  use_ease_in_out: true
  jitter_sigma: 1.2
  jitter_tail_fraction: 0.25
  overshoot_prob: 0.25
  overshoot_ratio: 0.06
vision:
  enabled: true
  template_dir: templates
  template_threshold: 0.8
  template_scales:
  - 0.6
  - 0.8
  - 1.0
  - 1.2
  - 1.4
  min_score: 0.5
  vlm:
    enabled: true
    base_url: http://localhost:20128/v1
    api_key: YOUR_VLM_API_KEY_HERE
    model: auto/best-vision
    timeout: 90
    # 坐标空间：norm1000 = 模型返回 0-1000 归一化坐标（本网关实测为默认行为，
    # 明确要求并换算后误差可到 ~1px）；pixel = 模型直接返回像素坐标。
    coord_space: norm1000
    # 每个后端重试次数。网关偶发返回内容安全串（"User Safety: safe"）而非 JSON，
    # 重试可显著提升可用率。
    attempts: 3
    fallback:
      base_url: http://localhost:20128/v1
      api_key: YOUR_VLM_API_KEY_HERE
      model: auto/vision
      timeout: 90
knowledge:
  file: element_knowledge.yaml
  fail_threshold: 3
diversify:
  variant_prob: 0.8
captcha:
  enabled: false
  slider:
    detect_texts:
    - 按住滑块
    - 拖动滑块
    - 向右滑动
    - 拖动到最右边
    - 向右拖动滑块
    success_texts:
    - 验证成功
    - 验证通过
    - 验证完成
    - 拼图完成
    retry_times: 3
    handle_y_ratio: 0.85
    handle_x_ratio: 0.08
    handle_templates: []
    band_h_ratio: 0.08
    scale: 1.0
    gap:
      mode: edge
      diff_threshold: 60
      background_template: ''
      vlm_prompt: 图片中有滑块验证码，缺口（暗色凹槽）中心的 x 坐标是多少？只返回数字。
    drag:
      duration:
      - 0.9
      - 1.5
apps:
- name: 趣头条
  package: com.jifen.qukan
  aliases:
  - 趣头条
  - 趣看天下
  - 趣闻
  - 新闻赚金币
  intent_map:
    sign: sign
    watch: watch
    withdraw: withdraw
  behaviors:
    sign:
    - type: click_first
      candidates:
      - type: text
        value: 直接签到
      - type: text
        value: 每日签到
      - type: text
        value: 签到
      - type: text
        value: 立即签到
      timeout: 8
      optional: true
    - type: think
    - type: reward
      pattern: 已连续签到(?P<amount>[0-9]+)\s*(?P<unit>天)
      unit: 天
      name: 连续签到天数
    - type: reward
      pattern: 明日签到可领(?P<amount>[0-9]+)\s*(?P<unit>金币)
      unit: 金币
      name: 明日签到奖励
    - type: reward
      pattern: 看视频再领(?P<amount>[0-9]+)\s*(?P<unit>金币)
      unit: 金币
      name: 视频额外奖励
    - type: click_id
      res_id: com.jifen.qukan:id/iv_close
      optional: true
    watch:
    - type: click_first
      candidates:
      - type: text
        value: 看视频领金币
      - type: text
        value: 体验领金币
      - type: text
        value: 领50金币
      - type: text
        value: 视频
      timeout: 8
      optional: true
    - type: think
    - type: reward
      pattern: 看视频[^0-9]*(?P<amount>[0-9]+)\s*(?P<unit>金币)
      unit: 金币
      name: 看视频
    - type: reward
      pattern: \+\s*(?P<amount>[0-9]+)\s*(?P<unit>金币)
      unit: 金币
      name: 看视频加奖
    - type: swipe
      from:
      - 0.5
      - 0.7
      to:
      - 0.5
      - 0.3
      duration: 0.8
      natural: true
    - type: wait
      seconds: 3
    withdraw:
    - type: click_text
      text: 我的
      timeout: 8
    - type: think
    - type: click_text
      text: 提现兑换
      timeout: 6
    - type: ensure
      text: 我要提现
      exists: 'yes'
      timeout: 5
    - type: reward
      pattern: 约(?P<amount>[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*(?P<unit>元)
      unit: 元
      name: 可提现余额
    - type: click_text
      text: 立即提现
      timeout: 6
      optional: true

      pattern: 可提现(?P<amount>[0-9]+(?:\\.[0-9]+)?)\\s*(?P<unit>元)
      unit: 元
      name: 可提现余额
    - type: click_text
      text: 立即提现
      timeout: 6
      optional: true
```

### `tests/test_action_contract.py`（261 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_action_contract.py - 2026-10-29 「type 按返回值契约分 9 类」回归钉

背景：19 个 type 平铺，问题不是"没分类"，而是**同类动作返回值语义不一致**，
`_run_kind` 只能统一 `None -> True` 兜底，于是"没做任何判断"与"判断通过"同义。
这个文件钉住四件事：

  ① `ACTION_CATEGORY` 与 `_run_kind` 的分派表严格一一对应（新增 type 漏登记会红）；
  ② 每个类别都有返回值契约；
  ③ 三个被 `None -> True` 掩盖的真 bug 已按契约修好：
       - `_step_click_point` 丢 `_verify_landing` 返回值（落地失败也算成功）
       - `_step_any_of` 的 optional 全 miss 分支日志写"放行"、却 return False
       - `_step_random` 不汇总子步骤（子步骤全失败，整步仍算成功）
  ④ 只有 point / timing / read 允许返回 None；其余类别返回 None 记 WARN 判失败。

运行：
    python tests/test_action_contract.py
"""
import os
import re
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

import behavior_runner as br  # noqa: E402
from executor import Executor, LocateResult  # noqa: E402
from config_loader import load_config  # noqa: E402
import nlu  # noqa: E402


class _Rec:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = []

    def log(self, event, app="", level="INFO"):
        self.lines.append(f"[{level}] {event}")


class _Human:
    def tap(self, *a, **k):
        pass

    def jitter_point(self, x, y):
        return x, y

    def delay(self, *a):
        pass

    def maybe_wander(self, *a):
        pass

    def think(self, *a):
        pass


class _Dev:
    pass


def _mk():
    ex = Executor("fake-serial", _Human(), _Rec(), dry_run=False, config={})
    ex.d = _Dev()
    ex._locate = lambda k, v, timeout=2.0: LocateResult(False, None, "测试未命中")
    ex._kb_candidates = lambda app, primary: ([primary], False)
    ex._kb_mark_fail = lambda app, prims: None
    return ex


# ---------- ① 分类表与分派表一一对应 ----------

def t_category_matches_dispatch_table():
    """分派表里的每个 type 都必须在 ACTION_CATEGORY 里登记，且不得多登记。

    漏登记 → `_run_kind` 记「动作类型未登记分类」并按失败处理，行为静默失效。
    直接从源码里抽出 `_run_kind` 的 dict 键做比对，避免测试里再抄一份名单。
    """
    src = open(br.__file__, encoding="utf-8").read()
    body = src.split("def _run_kind")[1].split("}.get(t)")[0]
    dispatched = set(re.findall(r'"([a-z_]+)":\s*self\._step_', body))
    assert dispatched, "没能从 _run_kind 抽出分派表，正则需更新"
    missing = dispatched - set(br.ACTION_CATEGORY)
    extra = set(br.ACTION_CATEGORY) - dispatched
    assert not missing, f"分派表有、分类表没有: {sorted(missing)}"
    assert not extra, f"分类表有、分派表没有: {sorted(extra)}"
    print(f"OK  分类表与分派表一一对应（{len(dispatched)} 个 type）")


def t_every_category_has_contract():
    """每个类别都必须有返回值契约，否则 `_check_return` 会退回 {bool} 兜底。"""
    cats = set(br.ACTION_CATEGORY.values())
    assert cats <= set(br._RETURN_CONTRACT), \
        f"缺契约的类别: {sorted(cats - set(br._RETURN_CONTRACT))}"
    for c, allowed in br._RETURN_CONTRACT.items():
        assert allowed and allowed <= {"bool", "none"}, (c, allowed)
    print(f"OK  {len(cats)} 个类别都有返回值契约")


def t_none_allowed_only_for_point_timing_read():
    """只有 point / timing / read 允许 None —— 其余类别返回 None 即失败。"""
    allow_none = {c for c, a in br._RETURN_CONTRACT.items() if "none" in a}
    assert allow_none == {"point", "timing", "read"}, allow_none
    print("OK  允许 None 的类别恰为 point/timing/read")


# ---------- ② bug 1：click_point 必须把落地校验结果返回上去 ----------

def t_click_point_landing_false_propagates():
    """落地校验失败 → `_step_click_point` 与 `_run_kind` 都必须返回 False。

    改前：`self._verify_landing(...)` 的返回值被丢弃，函数末尾无 return（None），
    `_run_kind` 把 None 当 True —— 落地失败也算成功。
    """
    ex = _mk()
    ex._verify_landing = lambda act, app, x=None, y=None, no_retry=False: False
    act = {"type": "click_point", "x": 0.5, "y": 0.5,
           "expect": {"text_any": ["签到"]}}
    assert ex._step_click_point(act, "T") is False
    assert ex._run_kind(act, "T") is False
    print("OK  click_point 落地失败 -> False（不再被 None 兜成成功）")


def t_click_point_landing_true_and_no_expect():
    """落地通过 → True；没写 expect → True（点了就是成功，无可判信号不算失败）。"""
    ex = _mk()
    ex._verify_landing = lambda act, app, x=None, y=None, no_retry=False: True
    assert ex._run_kind({"type": "click_point", "x": 0.5, "y": 0.5,
                         "expect": {"text_any": ["签到"]}}, "T") is True
    ex2 = _mk()
    assert ex2._run_kind({"type": "click_point", "x": 0.5, "y": 0.5}, "T") is True
    print("OK  click_point 落地通过/无 expect 均返回 True")


# ---------- ③ bug 2：any_of optional 放行必须返回 True ----------

def t_any_of_optional_all_miss_is_true():
    """optional 的 any_of 全 miss → True，且日志与返回值同义。"""
    ex = _mk()
    ex._dispatch_probe = lambda act, app, allow_vision=True: False
    act = {"type": "any_of", "optional": True,
           "choices": [[{"type": "click_text", "text": "签到"}]]}
    assert ex._step_any_of(act, "T") is True
    assert any("optional 放行" in l for l in ex.recorder.lines)
    print("OK  any_of optional 全 miss -> True（放行）")


def t_any_of_non_optional_all_miss_is_false():
    """非 optional 的 any_of 全 miss → False（B6 语义不变）。"""
    ex = _mk()
    ex._dispatch_probe = lambda act, app, allow_vision=True: False
    act = {"type": "any_of", "choices": [[{"type": "click_text", "text": "签到"}]]}
    assert ex._step_any_of(act, "T") is False
    print("OK  any_of 非 optional 全 miss -> False")


# ---------- ④ bug 3：random 必须汇总子步骤 ----------

def t_random_aggregates_substeps():
    """随机分支里任一子步骤失败 → 整步 False（改前恒为 None->True）。"""
    ex = _mk()
    ex._dispatch_probe = lambda act, app, allow_vision=True: False
    act = {"type": "random",
           "choices": [[{"type": "click_text", "text": "A"},
                        {"type": "click_text", "text": "B"}]]}
    assert ex._step_random(act, "T") is False
    assert ex._run_kind(act, "T") is False

    ex2 = _mk()
    ex2._dispatch_probe = lambda act, app, allow_vision=True: True
    assert ex2._run_kind(act, "T") is True

    ex3 = _mk()
    assert ex3._step_random({"type": "random", "choices": []}, "T") is False
    print("OK  random 汇总子步骤（全成功 True / 有失败 False / 无 choices False）")


# ---------- ⑤ 契约校验：None 只在允许的类别里算成功 ----------

def t_timing_and_read_none_are_success():
    """wait/think（timing）与 reward（read）返回 None 是合法的，算成功。"""
    ex = _mk()
    ex._step_wait = lambda act, app: None
    assert ex._run_kind({"type": "wait", "seconds": 1}, "T") is True
    ex2 = _mk()
    ex2._step_reward = lambda act, app: None
    assert ex2._run_kind({"type": "reward", "name": "金币"}, "T") is True
    print("OK  timing/read 返回 None 视为成功（合法契约）")


def t_click_none_violates_contract():
    """元素点击返回 None 属契约违反：记 WARN 并按失败处理。"""
    ex = _mk()
    ex._step_click_text = lambda act, app, allow_vision=True: None
    assert ex._run_kind({"type": "click_text", "text": "签到"}, "T") is False
    assert any("返回值契约违反" in l for l in ex.recorder.lines), ex.recorder.lines
    print("OK  click 返回 None -> WARN + False（不再兜成成功）")


def t_unregistered_type_is_failure():
    """分派表有、分类表没有的 type（人为制造）→ WARN + False。"""
    ex = _mk()
    br.ACTION_CATEGORY.pop("back", None)
    try:
        assert ex._run_kind({"type": "back"}, "T") is False
        assert any("未登记分类" in l for l in ex.recorder.lines)
    finally:
        br.ACTION_CATEGORY["back"] = "key"
    print("OK  未登记分类的 type -> WARN + False")


# ---------- ⑥ behavior 层：删 growth / 降级 open ----------

def t_growth_intent_removed():
    """growth 已从意图表删除：相关文案不再解析出 growth。"""
    for text in ("去做任务", "浇花施肥", "升级"):
        intents = [i for i, _ in nlu.parse_intents(text)]
        assert "growth" not in intents, (text, intents)
    assert "growth" not in nlu.DEFAULT_INTENT_MAP
    print("OK  growth 意图已删除（做任务/浇花/升级 不再产出 growth）")


def t_open_intent_removed():
    """open 已从意图表删除（系统动作，不是用户目标）。"""
    for text in ("打开", "启动", "开启应用"):
        intents = [i for i, _ in nlu.parse_intents(text)]
        assert "open" not in intents, (text, intents)
    assert "open" not in nlu.DEFAULT_INTENT_MAP
    print("OK  open 意图已删除")


def t_baidu_lite_redirects_sign_to_open():
    """百度极速版只有 open 一条链，必须靠 intent_map 重定向，否则彻底不可执行。"""
    apps = load_config(os.path.join(ROOT, "config.yaml"))["apps"]
    app = next(a for a in apps if a.get("name") == "百度极速版")
    assert (app.get("intent_map") or {}).get("sign") == "open", app.get("intent_map")
    assert "open" in (app.get("behaviors") or {}), list(app.get("behaviors") or {})
    print("OK  百度极速版 intent_map: sign -> open")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [t_category_matches_dispatch_table, t_every_category_has_contract,
             t_none_allowed_only_for_point_timing_read,
             t_click_point_landing_false_propagates,
             t_click_point_landing_true_and_no_expect,
             t_any_of_optional_all_miss_is_true,
             t_any_of_non_optional_all_miss_is_false,
             t_random_aggregates_substeps,
             t_timing_and_read_none_are_success,
             t_click_none_violates_contract,
             t_unregistered_type_is_failure,
             t_growth_intent_removed, t_open_intent_removed,
             t_baidu_lite_redirects_sign_to_open]
    failed = 0
    for t in tests:
        try:
            t()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {t.__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_anchor_anyof.py`（352 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_anchor_anyof.py - any_of 锚点校验语义（无需真机）

背景（真实测量缺陷）：
    三级导航的 any_of 分支形如
        [click_point(我的), think, click_first(福利中心), think, click_first(签到)]
    中间那步「福利中心」也是锚点类动作。早期 `check_anchor` 遍历分支时
    **把分支里任何一个锚点类动作命中都当成整个分支命中**，
    于是「福利中心」在落地页被找到时，就报成
        `首屏命中 any_of 分支#2: text='福利'`
    —— 看起来像命中，实际上签到锚点根本没出现过。

    实测影响：天天快讯极速版.sign / 河马剧场.sign / 喜番免费短剧.sign
    三个三级行为在 3 轮统计里全被记成「首屏命中」，全是假命中。

正确语义：
    一个分支是否命中，取决于该分支的**最后一个**锚点动作（真正的目标），
    而不是中途的入口。执行器本身就是按顺序执行、最后一步才落到目标锚点。

运行：
    python tests/test_anchor_anyof.py
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(ROOT, "devtools"))

from verify_live import (  # noqa: E402
    check_anchor, mid_entry_of, target_anchor_of, find_anchor_node,
    at_top_level,
)

H = 2340
NAV = {"type": "click_point",
       "vlm_prompt": "定位屏幕底部导航栏最右侧的『我的』标签中心",
       "x": 0.9, "y": 0.915, "vision": False, "optional": True}


def _n(text="", cx=500, cy=1200, clickable=True):
    return {"text": text, "desc": "", "rid": "", "center": (cx, cy),
            "size": (100, 100), "clickable": clickable,
            "bounds": (cx - 50, cy - 50, cx + 50, cy + 50)}


def _three_level_anyof(anchor_values=("签到", "每日签到")):
    return {"type": "any_of", "optional": True, "choices": [
        [{"type": "click_first",
          "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": v} for v in anchor_values],
          "optional": False}],
        [dict(NAV), {"type": "think"},
         {"type": "click_first",
          "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "福利中心"},
                         {"type": "text", "value": "福利"}],
          "optional": False},
         {"type": "think"},
         {"type": "click_first",
          "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": v} for v in anchor_values],
          "optional": False}],
    ]}


def t_mid_entry_alone_is_not_a_hit():
    """落地页只有「福利中心」（中间入口）-> 不应判为命中。"""
    nodes = [_n("福利中心", 540, 2187)]
    hit, det = check_anchor(nodes, _three_level_anyof(), H)
    assert not hit, f"中间入口命中被误判为整体命中: {det}"
    print("OK  中间入口「福利中心」单独出现不算命中")


def t_real_anchor_is_a_hit():
    """落地页有真正的签到锚点 -> 命中（走分支1）。"""
    nodes = [_n("签到", 720, 2093)]
    hit, det = check_anchor(nodes, _three_level_anyof(), H)
    assert hit, det
    assert "分支#1" in det, det
    print("OK  真正的签到锚点出现即命中（分支#1）")


def t_second_branch_anchor_also_counts():
    """分支2 的最终锚点也要被检查到（不是只看分支1）。

    `convert_deep` 生成的两条分支**共用同一个目标锚点**，所以单份 dump 上
    分支1 先命中 —— 这里断言的是「命中被正确识别」，并说明分支2 的价值在
    『换页面再找』（由 导航后 复校体现）。
    """
    nodes = [_n("每日签到", 300, 800)]
    hit, det = check_anchor(nodes, _three_level_anyof(), H)
    assert hit, det
    assert "分支#" in det, det
    # 分支2 的末步（目标锚点）确实被纳入检查范围
    act = _three_level_anyof()
    b2_anchors = [a for a in act["choices"][1]
                  if a.get("type") == "click_first"]
    assert b2_anchors, "分支2 应包含锚点动作"
    assert check_anchor([_n("签到")], {"type": "any_of", "optional": True,
                                      "choices": [act["choices"][1]]}, H)[0]
    print("OK  分支2 的最终锚点被纳入检查（命中识别正确）")


def t_branch2_alone_still_needs_real_anchor():
    """只给分支2（导航+中间入口+锚点）时，中间入口不算命中、锚点才算。"""
    act = _three_level_anyof()
    only_b2 = {"type": "any_of", "optional": True,
               "choices": [act["choices"][1]]}
    assert check_anchor([_n("福利中心", 540, 2187)], only_b2, H)[0] is False
    assert check_anchor([_n("签到", 720, 2093)], only_b2, H)[0] is True
    print("OK  仅分支2 时：中间入口不算命中，目标锚点才算")


def t_two_level_anyof_unchanged():
    """二级 any_of（无中间入口）行为不变：末步就是锚点。"""
    act = {"type": "any_of", "optional": True, "choices": [
        [{"type": "click_first", "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"}],
          "optional": False}],
        [dict(NAV), {"type": "think"},
         {"type": "click_first", "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"}],
          "optional": False}],
    ]}
    assert check_anchor([_n("签到")], act, H)[0] is True
    assert check_anchor([_n("福利")], act, H)[0] is False
    print("OK  二级 any_of 语义不变")


def t_nav_point_is_not_treated_as_anchor():
    """导航 click_point 不是锚点，不能靠它"命中"。"""
    act = {"type": "any_of", "optional": True, "choices": [[dict(NAV)]]}
    hit, det = check_anchor([_n("我的", 918, 2130)], act, H)
    assert not hit, f"导航步不应算锚点命中: {det}"
    print("OK  导航 click_point 不算锚点命中")


def t_all_branches_miss():
    nodes = [_n("首页"), _n("推荐")]
    hit, det = check_anchor(nodes, _three_level_anyof(), H)
    assert not hit, det
    assert "未命中" in det, det
    print("OK  全部分支未命中")


def t_mid_entry_detected_for_three_level():
    act = _three_level_anyof()
    mid = mid_entry_of(act)
    assert mid is not None, "三级 any_of 应能识别出中间入口"
    vals = [c["value"] for c in mid["candidates"]]
    assert vals[0] == "福利中心", vals
    tgt = target_anchor_of(act)
    assert tgt is not None
    tvals = [c["value"] for c in tgt["candidates"]]
    assert "签到" in tvals, tvals
    assert mid is not tgt, "中间入口不能等于目标锚点"
    print("OK  三级 any_of：中间入口与目标锚点被正确区分")


def t_no_mid_entry_for_two_level():
    act = {"type": "any_of", "optional": True, "choices": [
        [{"type": "click_first", "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"}],
          "optional": False}],
        [dict(NAV), {"type": "think"},
         {"type": "click_first", "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"}],
          "optional": False}],
    ]}
    assert mid_entry_of(act) is None, "二级 any_of 不应有中间入口"
    assert target_anchor_of(act) is not None
    print("OK  二级 any_of 无中间入口")


def t_mid_entry_none_for_plain_anchor():
    assert mid_entry_of({"type": "click_first",
                         "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"}]}) is None
    print("OK  非 any_of 动作无中间入口")


def t_target_anchor_of_plain_anchor_is_itself():
    plain = {"type": "click_text", "text": "提现"}
    assert target_anchor_of(plain) is plain
    print("OK  普通锚点的目标就是它自己")


def t_find_anchor_node_returns_clickable_node():
    nodes = [_n("福利中心", 540, 2187)]
    mid = mid_entry_of(_three_level_anyof())
    node, det = find_anchor_node(nodes, mid, H)
    assert node is not None, det
    assert node["center"] == (540, 2187)
    print("OK  find_anchor_node 返回可点击节点（供真机点中间入口）")


def t_find_anchor_node_none_when_absent():
    node, det = find_anchor_node([_n("首页")], mid_entry_of(_three_level_anyof()), H)
    assert node is None, det
    print("OK  中间入口不存在时返回 None")


def t_find_anchor_node_respects_nav_band():
    """中间入口若是导航标签，也要过底部带校验。"""
    act = {"type": "click_first", "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "我的"}]}
    node, _ = find_anchor_node([_n("我的", 575, 627)], act, H)
    assert node is None, "页面中部的『我的』不应作为入口"
    node2, _ = find_anchor_node([_n("我的", 918, 2130)], act, H)
    assert node2 is not None
    print("OK  find_anchor_node 继承底部导航带校验")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 行为间复位
def t_at_top_level_true_when_nav_in_bottom_band():
    """底部带有导航标签 -> 顶层页。"""
    nodes = [_n("首页", 100, 2140), _n("我的", 960, 2145)]
    assert at_top_level(nodes, H) is True
    print("OK  底部带导航标签 -> 判为顶层页")


def t_at_top_level_false_when_label_in_middle():
    """页面中部的『我的』不算顶层页（假命中防线）。"""
    nodes = [_n("我的", 575, 627)]
    assert at_top_level(nodes, H) is False
    print("OK  页面中部同名文字 -> 不判为顶层页")


def t_at_top_level_false_for_sub_page():
    """二级页（签到列表）无底部导航 -> 不是顶层页，必须继续 BACK。"""
    nodes = [_n("签到", 188, 1993), _n("每日签到", 400, 1200),
             _n("立即领取", 900, 1500)]
    assert at_top_level(nodes, H) is False
    print("OK  二级页无导航栏 -> 不判为顶层页")


def t_at_top_level_infers_height_when_unknown():
    """未传屏高时用节点最大 y 兜底推断，仍能识别底部带。"""
    nodes = [_n("首页", 100, 2200), _n("我的", 960, 2230)]
    assert at_top_level(nodes, None) is True
    print("OK  屏高未知时按最大 y 推断")


def t_at_top_level_single_non_home_label_is_not_enough():
    """底部只有一个非首页类标签（如『福利』）-> 不足以判为顶层页。

    这条防的是「二级页底部恰有个『福利』字样就被当成顶层页、于是不复位」。
    """
    nodes = [_n("福利", 500, 2140), _n("立即领取", 900, 1500)]
    assert at_top_level(nodes, H) is False
    print("OK  底部单个非首页类标签不足以判为顶层页")


def t_at_top_level_home_tab_alone_is_enough():
    """底部出现『我的』这类首页标签，单独一个即可判为顶层页。"""
    nodes = [_n("我的", 960, 2145), _n("签到", 400, 1200)]
    assert at_top_level(nodes, H) is True
    print("OK  底部出现首页类标签即判为顶层页")


def t_at_top_level_real_tab_bar():
    """典型 5 个 tab 的顶层页。"""
    nodes = [_n("首页", 100, 2140), _n("视频", 400, 2140),
             _n("任务", 700, 2140), _n("福利", 900, 2140),
             _n("我的", 1000, 2145)]
    assert at_top_level(nodes, H) is True
    print("OK  典型多 tab 顶层页识别正确")


def t_void_status_is_excluded_from_denominator():
    """新增的作废状态必须能被统计脚本识别（前缀契约）。"""
    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(ROOT, "devtools"))
    from stability_run import is_void, is_hit
    st = "数据作废(导航后页面非目标App)"
    assert is_void(st) is True, "作废状态必须排除出分母"
    assert is_hit(st) is False
    assert is_void("导航后仍未命中") is False
    assert is_hit("导航后命中") is True
    print("OK  作废状态前缀契约成立（不进分母、不算命中）")


# ---------------------------------------------------------- reset_to_landing
class _FakeDev:
    """只记录 press 调用次数的最小假设备。"""

    def __init__(self, pages=None):
        self.presses = []
        self.pages = list(pages or [])

    def dump_hierarchy(self):
        if len(self.pages) > 1:
            return self.pages.pop(0)
        return self.pages[0] if self.pages else "<x/>"

    def press(self, key):
        self.presses.append(key)


def _with_stubs(pages, fn):
    """临时替换 page_belongs_to / parse_nodes，避免测试依赖真机 dump。"""
    import verify_live as V
    orig_pb, orig_pn = V.page_belongs_to, V.parse_nodes
    V.page_belongs_to = lambda xml, pkg: (True, pkg, 1, 1)
    V.parse_nodes = lambda xml: pages.pop(0)
    try:
        return fn(V)
    finally:
        V.page_belongs_to, V.parse_nodes = orig_pb, orig_pn


def t_reset_skips_back_when_already_top_level():
    """已在顶层页 -> 一个 BACK 都不按。

    实测教训：多数 App 在「我的」tab 按 BACK 会**直接退出 App**。
    早期实现「先按 BACK 再判」导致一按就退到桌面，白吃一次冷启动。
    """
    dev = _FakeDev()

    def run(V):
        log = []
        V.reset_to_landing(dev, "com.x", log, H)
        return log

    log = _with_stubs([[_n("首页", 100, 2140), _n("我的", 960, 2145)]], run)
    assert dev.presses == [], f"已在顶层页不该按 BACK，实际 {dev.presses}"
    assert any("已在顶层页" in x for x in log), log
    print("OK  已在顶层页时不按 BACK（先判断后动作）")


def t_reset_backs_until_top_level():
    """二级页 -> 按 BACK，退到顶层页即停（不多按）。"""
    dev = _FakeDev()

    def run(V):
        log = []
        V.reset_to_landing(dev, "com.x", log, H)
        return log

    # 第 1 次 dump（判前）= 签到二级页；BACK 后第 2 次 dump = 顶层页
    pages = [[_n("签到", 188, 1993)],
             [_n("首页", 100, 2140), _n("我的", 960, 2145)]]
    log = _with_stubs(pages, run)
    assert dev.presses == ["back"], dev.presses
    assert any("回到顶层页" in x for x in log), log
    print("OK  二级页按一次 BACK 回到顶层页后即停")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_anti_detection.py`（193 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_anti_detection.py - F2 反检测增强单测（AUTOP-FEAT-2026-10-20）

覆盖卡规 F2.3：
  1. seed 固定 -> 误触发生序列可复现（含误触点坐标）；
  2. 限流长停触发条件命中/不命中；
  3. 验证码冷却生效（含未启用/零值时不冷却）；
  4. 不破坏既有 human 测试（test_human_seed 基线先跑过，全绿）。

运行：
    python -m pytest tests/test_anti_detection.py -q
    python tests/test_anti_detection.py
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

import human as human_mod  # noqa: E402
from human import Human  # noqa: E402


class _D:
    """假设备：window_size 回 plain tuple（真机实测形态），click 全记录。"""

    def __init__(self, ws=(1080, 2340)):
        self._ws = ws
        self.clicks = []

    def window_size(self):
        return self._ws

    def click(self, x, y):
        self.clicks.append((x, y))


def _human(anti=None, seed=42):
    return Human({"human": {}, "anti_detection": anti or {}}, seed=seed)


class _SleepSpy:
    """把 human 模块内的 time.sleep 换成记录器（用完还原）。"""

    def __init__(self):
        self.durations = []
        self._orig = None

    def __enter__(self):
        self._orig = human_mod.time.sleep
        human_mod.time.sleep = lambda s: self.durations.append(s)
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *a):
        human_mod.time.sleep = self._orig
        return False


def t_seed_reproducible_mistakes():
    """同 seed 的两个 Human：误触序列（含错点坐标）逐帧一致。"""
    a, b = _human({"mistake_prob": 0.5}, seed=20261021), \
           _human({"mistake_prob": 0.5}, seed=20261021)
    da, db = _D(), _D()
    # tap 内含限流长停（默认每 12 次睡 14-26s）——测试必须 spy sleep，
    # 否则 40 次循环要跑几分钟（2026-10-21 首版即因此挂死被 timeout 杀）。
    with _SleepSpy():
        for i in range(40):
            a.tap(da, 500 + i, 1200)
            b.tap(db, 500 + i, 1200)
    assert da.clicks == db.clicks, "同 seed 点击序列必须一致（含误触）"
    # 至少发生过误触（双跳：错点+正点），否则本用例没测到东西
    doubles = sum(1 for i in range(0, len(da.clicks) - 1, 2)
                  if da.clicks[i] != da.clicks[i + 1])
    assert doubles > 0, "mistake_prob=0.5 下 40 次应出现误触"
    print("t_seed_reproducible_mistakes OK（误触 %d 次）" % doubles)


def t_mistake_prob_zero_no_mistake():
    """mistake_prob=0：永不误触，每次 tap 恰好一次 click。"""
    h = _human({"mistake_prob": 0.0}, seed=7)
    d = _D()
    with _SleepSpy():
        for i in range(30):
            h.tap(d, 300, 900)
    assert len(d.clicks) == 30, d.clicks
    assert all(c == (300, 900) for c in d.clicks)
    print("t_mistake_prob_zero_no_mistake OK")


def t_mistake_offset_bounded():
    """误触点约束：±160px 内且不出屏（防真点歪改页面）。"""
    h = _human({"mistake_prob": 1.0}, seed=9)
    d = _D()
    with _SleepSpy():
        for i in range(30):
            h.tap(d, 540, 1170)
    assert len(d.clicks) == 60          # 每次都误触+正点
    for j in range(0, 60, 2):
        mx, my = d.clicks[j]
        assert 8 <= mx <= 1080 - 8 and 8 <= my <= 2340 - 8, (mx, my)
        assert abs(mx - 540) <= 160 and abs(my - 1170) <= 160, (mx, my)
    print("t_mistake_offset_bounded OK")


def t_cooldown_triggers_at_threshold():
    """限流：每 cooldown_every 次点击触发一次长停，时长在 0.7~1.3 倍区间。"""
    h = _human({"cooldown_every": 3, "cooldown_seconds": 10}, seed=11)
    d = _D()
    with _SleepSpy() as spy:
        for i in range(9):
            h.tap(d, 100, 100)
    long_sleeps = [s for s in spy.durations if s >= 7.0]
    assert len(long_sleeps) == 3, (spy.durations, "9 次点击/每3次=3 次长停")
    assert all(7.0 <= s <= 13.0 for s in long_sleeps), long_sleeps
    print("t_cooldown_triggers_at_threshold OK")


def t_cooldown_not_before_threshold():
    """未到阈值不长停（短延迟不算长停）。"""
    h = _human({"cooldown_every": 5, "cooldown_seconds": 20}, seed=13)
    d = _D()
    with _SleepSpy() as spy:
        for i in range(4):
            h.tap(d, 100, 100)
    assert not [s for s in spy.durations if s >= 14.0], spy.durations
    print("t_cooldown_not_before_threshold OK")


def t_captcha_cooldown_effective():
    """验证码冷却：默认 300s（0.8~1.2 抖动）；禁用/零值返回 0 不冷却。"""
    h = _human({}, seed=17)                     # 缺省即 300
    with _SleepSpy() as spy:
        sec = h.captcha_cooldown()
    assert 240.0 <= sec <= 360.0, sec
    assert spy.durations and spy.durations[-1] == sec
    off = _human({"enabled": False}, seed=17)
    assert off.captcha_cooldown() == 0.0
    zero = _human({"cooldown_on_captcha": 0}, seed=17)
    assert zero.captcha_cooldown() == 0.0
    print("t_captcha_cooldown_effective OK")


def t_anti_disabled_no_effects():
    """anti_detection.enabled=false：无误触、无限流、无冷却（老行为零影响）。"""
    h = _human({"enabled": False, "mistake_prob": 1.0,
                "cooldown_every": 1, "cooldown_seconds": 50}, seed=19)
    d = _D()
    with _SleepSpy() as spy:
        for i in range(6):
            h.tap(d, 100, 100)
        assert h.captcha_cooldown() == 0.0
    assert len(d.clicks) == 6, d.clicks       # 无误触双跳
    assert not [s for s in spy.durations if s >= 35.0], spy.durations
    print("t_anti_disabled_no_effects OK")


def t_wander_probability_override():
    """anti_detection.wander_probability 覆盖 human 段同名值。"""
    h = Human({"human": {"wander_probability": 0.06},
               "anti_detection": {"wander_probability": 0.25}}, seed=23)
    assert abs(h.wander_prob - 0.25) < 1e-9
    # 缺省回落 human 段
    h2 = Human({"human": {"wander_probability": 0.06},
                "anti_detection": {}}, seed=23)
    assert abs(h2.wander_prob - 0.06) < 1e-9
    # 两段都缺省 -> 0.10（F2 默认）
    h3 = Human({"human": {}, "anti_detection": {}}, seed=23)
    assert abs(h3.wander_prob - 0.10) < 1e-9
    print("t_wander_probability_override OK")


def t_defaults_match_card():
    """默认值与派单卡 F2.2 配置段一致。"""
    h = _human({}, seed=29)
    assert abs(h.mistake_prob - 0.05) < 1e-9
    assert h.cooldown_every == 12
    assert abs(h.cooldown_seconds - 20) < 1e-9
    assert abs(h.cooldown_on_captcha - 300) < 1e-9
    assert h._anti_enabled is True
    print("t_defaults_match_card OK")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    t_seed_reproducible_mistakes()
    t_mistake_prob_zero_no_mistake()
    t_mistake_offset_bounded()
    t_cooldown_triggers_at_threshold()
    t_cooldown_not_before_threshold()
    t_captcha_cooldown_effective()
    t_anti_disabled_no_effects()
    t_wander_probability_override()
    t_defaults_match_card()
    print("ALL anti_detection TESTS PASSED")
```

### `tests/test_any_of.py`（310 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_any_of.py - Executor._step_any_of 单元测试（无需真机）

背景：App 落地页不确定（开屏广告/活动弹窗/新手引导/上次退出的 tab/登录态），
同一行为的入口可能在首屏、也可能只在二级页。写死前置步会堵死首屏路径，
只写首屏锚点又会在页面漂移时全灭。`any_of` 让多条路径共存。

这里覆盖它的关键语义：
  1. 分支按顺序尝试，第一组**全部动作成功**即停，后续分支不执行；
  2. `optional: true` 的定位动作「没找到」返回 False（不是 None/异常），
     必须被识别为分支未命中 —— 否则任何分支都会"成功"，any_of 形同虚设；
  3. 分支内某步抛 StepError → 该分支判失败，且**后续步骤不执行**；
  4. 全部分支未命中时：optional 放行返回 False，否则 WARN 返回 False；
  5. 视觉门控是显式参数链：只有最后一个分支 allow_vision=True
     （B7 教训：早期用 `_probe_no_vision` 实例标志位临时改写再还原，
     异常/新增直调点会留下脏状态，测试只能靠反证用例兜底；现改为
     `allow_vision` 参数显式贯穿，反证用例随之取消）。

运行：
    python tests/test_any_of.py
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from executor import Executor, StepError, LocateResult  # noqa: E402


class _Rec:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = []

    def log(self, event, app="", level="INFO"):
        self.lines.append(f"[{level}] {event}")


def _mk(fake_probe):
    ex = Executor.__new__(Executor)
    ex.recorder = _Rec()
    ex._dispatch_probe = fake_probe
    return ex


def _scripted(mapping, calls):
    """构造一个按 (type, tag) 返回 True/False/抛异常的假 _dispatch_probe。

    mapping: {(type, tag): True|False|"raise"}
    calls:   调用记录列表，元素为 (type, tag)
    """
    def fake(act, app_name, allow_vision):
        key = (act.get("type"), act.get("tag"))
        calls.append(key)
        res = mapping.get(key, True)
        if res == "raise":
            raise StepError(f"模拟失败 {key}")
        return bool(res)
    return fake


# ---------- 用例 ----------

def t_first_branch_wins():
    """第一分支命中即停，第二分支不应被执行。"""
    calls = []
    ex = _mk(_scripted({("a", 1): True, ("b", 1): True}, calls))
    act = {"type": "any_of", "choices": [
        [{"type": "a", "tag": 1}],
        [{"type": "b", "tag": 1}],
    ]}
    assert ex._step_any_of(act, "T") is True
    assert calls == [("a", 1)], calls
    print("OK  第一分支命中即停，后续分支不执行")


def t_fallback_to_second_branch():
    """第一分支未命中（定位动作返回 False）→ 继续试第二分支。"""
    calls = []
    ex = _mk(_scripted({("a", 1): False, ("b", 1): True}, calls))
    act = {"type": "any_of", "choices": [
        [{"type": "a", "tag": 1}],
        [{"type": "b", "tag": 1}],
    ]}
    assert ex._step_any_of(act, "T") is True
    assert calls == [("a", 1), ("b", 1)], calls
    print("OK  首分支未命中时回退到第二分支")


def t_optional_miss_is_not_success():
    """optional 定位动作返回 False 必须被当作未命中（关键语义）。"""
    calls = []
    ex = _mk(_scripted({("miss", 1): False, ("hit", 1): True}, calls))
    act = {"type": "any_of", "choices": [
        [{"type": "miss", "tag": 1}],          # 模拟 optional click_first 没找到
        [{"type": "hit", "tag": 1}],
    ]}
    assert ex._step_any_of(act, "T") is True
    assert ("hit", 1) in calls, "optional 未命中必须继续试下一分支"
    print("OK  optional 未命中不被误判为分支成功")


def t_multistep_branch_stops_on_failure():
    """分支内某步失败 → 该分支后续步骤不执行，直接转下一分支。"""
    calls = []
    ex = _mk(_scripted({("nav", 1): True, ("anchor", 1): False,
                        ("anchor", 2): True}, calls))
    act = {"type": "any_of", "choices": [
        [{"type": "nav", "tag": 1}, {"type": "anchor", "tag": 1}],
        [{"type": "anchor", "tag": 2}],
    ]}
    assert ex._step_any_of(act, "T") is True
    assert calls == [("nav", 1), ("anchor", 1), ("anchor", 2)], calls
    print("OK  分支内失败即中断该分支（多步分支）")


def t_step_error_treated_as_miss():
    """分支内抛 StepError → 判该分支失败并继续。"""
    calls = []
    ex = _mk(_scripted({("a", 1): "raise", ("b", 1): True}, calls))
    act = {"type": "any_of", "choices": [
        [{"type": "a", "tag": 1}],
        [{"type": "b", "tag": 1}],
    ]}
    assert ex._step_any_of(act, "T") is True
    assert calls == [("a", 1), ("b", 1)], calls
    print("OK  分支抛 StepError 被当作未命中并继续")


def t_all_miss_optional_pass():
    """全部分支未命中 + optional → 返回 **True**（放行），不抛异常。

    2026-10-29 契约修正：此前这里断言 False，理由是"没命中就是没命中"。
    但日志一直写的是「optional 放行」，而放行在语义上等于"本步不算失败"。
    日志说放行、返回值说失败，外层 `_run_kind` 只认返回值，于是 49 处
    optional 的 any_of 全 miss 都被记成「步骤失败」，体检报告因此长期偏红。
    现在返回值与日志同义：True。
    """
    calls = []
    ex = _mk(_scripted({("a", 1): False}, calls))
    act = {"type": "any_of", "optional": True,
           "choices": [[{"type": "a", "tag": 1}]]}
    assert ex._step_any_of(act, "T") is True
    assert any("optional 放行" in l for l in ex.recorder.lines), ex.recorder.lines
    print("OK  全部分支未命中 + optional 放行（返回 True，与日志同义）")


def t_all_miss_returns_false_warn():
    """全部分支未命中 + 非 optional → WARN 并返回 False（B6 不再抛 StepError）。"""
    calls = []
    ex = _mk(_scripted({("a", 1): False}, calls))
    act = {"type": "any_of", "choices": [[{"type": "a", "tag": 1}]]}
    assert ex._step_any_of(act, "T") is False
    assert any("any_of 全部分支均未命中" in l for l in ex.recorder.lines)
    print("OK  全部分支未命中且非 optional 时 WARN 返回 False")


def t_empty_choices():
    """choices 为空 → 返回 False 且不崩。"""
    ex = _mk(_scripted({}, []))
    assert ex._step_any_of({"type": "any_of", "choices": []}, "T") is False
    assert any("缺少 choices" in l for l in ex.recorder.lines)
    print("OK  choices 为空时安全返回 False")


def t_dict_choice_supported():
    """choice 允许是单个动作 dict（不必包成 list）。"""
    calls = []
    ex = _mk(_scripted({("a", 1): True}, calls))
    act = {"type": "any_of", "choices": [{"type": "a", "tag": 1}]}
    assert ex._step_any_of(act, "T") is True
    assert calls == [("a", 1)]
    print("OK  choice 支持单个动作 dict")


# ---------- 与 _step_click_first 的集成（any_of 成立的前提） ----------

def _mk_real_click_first(locate_result):
    """构造一个真实的 Executor（不打桩 _dispatch_probe），只打桩定位相关依赖。"""
    ex = Executor.__new__(Executor)
    ex.recorder = _Rec()
    ex.dry_run = True
    ex.d = None
    ex._last_fail = ""
    ex._locate = lambda k, v, timeout=6.0: locate_result
    ex._kb_candidates = lambda app, primary: ([primary], True)
    ex._kb_mark_fail = lambda app, prims: None
    ex._vision_click = lambda act, app, desc: False
    ex.knowledge = type("K", (), {"mark_ok": lambda *a: None})()
    return ex


def t_click_first_optional_miss_returns_false():
    """optional 且全候选未命中时，_step_click_first 必须返回 False（而非 None）。

    这是 any_of 正确工作的前提：若返回 None，_dispatch 会映射为 True，
    于是任何含 optional 定位动作的分支都被判成"成功"，any_of 形同虚设。
    """
    ex = _mk_real_click_first(LocateResult(False, None, "测试未命中"))
    act = {"type": "click_first", "optional": True,
           "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"}]}
    res = ex._step_click_first(act, "T")
    assert res is False, f"应返回 False，实际 {res!r}"
    print("OK  click_first(optional) 未命中返回 False（any_of 前提）")


def t_click_first_nonoptional_miss_returns_false():
    """非 optional 且全候选未命中时，返回 False 并记录 detail（B6 不再抛 StepError）。"""
    ex = _mk_real_click_first(LocateResult(False, None, "测试未命中"))
    act = {"type": "click_first",
           "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"}]}
    res = ex._step_click_first(act, "T")
    assert res is False, f"应返回 False，实际 {res!r}"
    assert ex._last_fail and "所有候选均未出现" in ex._last_fail, ex._last_fail
    print("OK  click_first(非 optional) 未命中返回 False 并记录 detail")


def t_three_level_branch_runs_nav_mid_anchor():
    """三级导航分支：我的 → 中间入口 → 目标锚点，逐步执行到位。

    这是 sign 类行为的真实形态（河马剧场：我的 → 福利 → 签到）。
    """
    calls = []
    ex = _mk(_scripted({("anchor", 0): False, ("nav", 1): True,
                        ("mid", 1): True, ("anchor", 1): True}, calls))
    act = {"type": "any_of", "optional": True, "choices": [
        # 分支1：首屏直连（未命中）
        [{"type": "anchor", "tag": 0}],
        # 分支2：我的 → 中间入口 → 锚点
        [{"type": "nav", "tag": 1}, {"type": "think", "tag": 1},
         {"type": "mid", "tag": 1}, {"type": "think", "tag": 2},
         {"type": "anchor", "tag": 1}],
    ]}
    assert ex._step_any_of(act, "T") is True
    assert calls == [("anchor", 0), ("nav", 1), ("think", 1), ("mid", 1),
                     ("think", 2), ("anchor", 1)], calls
    print("OK  三级导航分支逐步执行：首屏 → 我的 → 中间入口 → 锚点")


def t_three_level_mid_miss_stops_branch():
    """三级分支里中间入口没找到 → 该分支失败，不执行后面的锚点。"""
    calls = []
    ex = _mk(_scripted({("anchor", 0): False, ("nav", 1): True,
                        ("mid", 1): False, ("anchor", 1): True}, calls))
    act = {"type": "any_of", "optional": True, "choices": [
        [{"type": "anchor", "tag": 0}],
        [{"type": "nav", "tag": 1}, {"type": "mid", "tag": 1},
         {"type": "anchor", "tag": 1}],
    ]}
    assert ex._step_any_of(act, "T") is True      # 全分支未命中 + optional 放行
    assert ("anchor", 1) not in calls, "中间入口失败后不应继续点锚点"
    print("OK  三级分支中间入口失败即中断，不误点后续锚点")


def t_only_last_branch_allows_vision():
    """只有最后一个分支允许视觉兜底（前面的分支是"便宜探测"）。

    B7：门控是显式参数链，断言 allow_vision 序列 == [False, True]。
    """
    seen = []

    def fake(act, app_name, allow_vision):
        seen.append((act.get("tag"), allow_vision))
        return act.get("tag") == 2      # 只有最后一个分支命中

    ex = _mk(fake)
    act = {"type": "any_of", "optional": True, "choices": [
        [{"type": "x", "tag": 1}],
        [{"type": "x", "tag": 2}],
    ]}
    assert ex._step_any_of(act, "T") is True
    assert seen == [(1, False), (2, True)], seen
    print("OK  仅最后一个分支 allow_vision=True（显式参数链）")


def t_dispatch_maps_none_to_true():
    """_dispatch 兼容薄包装：None 归一化为 True，False 原样传出。"""
    ex = Executor.__new__(Executor)
    ex.recorder = _Rec()
    ex.human = type("H", (), {"delay": lambda *a: None,
                              "maybe_wander": lambda *a: None})()
    ex.d = None
    ex._step_wait = lambda act, app: None
    ex._step_click_first = lambda act, app, allow_vision=True: False
    assert ex._dispatch({"type": "wait"}, "T", ritual=False) is True
    assert ex._dispatch({"type": "click_first"}, "T", ritual=False) is False
    assert ex._dispatch({"type": "不存在"}, "T", ritual=False) is False
    print("OK  _dispatch 归一化：None->True，False->False，未知类型->False")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [t_first_branch_wins, t_fallback_to_second_branch,
             t_optional_miss_is_not_success, t_multistep_branch_stops_on_failure,
             t_step_error_treated_as_miss, t_all_miss_optional_pass,
             t_all_miss_returns_false_warn, t_empty_choices, t_dict_choice_supported,
             t_three_level_branch_runs_nav_mid_anchor,
             t_three_level_mid_miss_stops_branch,
             t_only_last_branch_allows_vision,
             t_click_first_optional_miss_returns_false,
             t_click_first_nonoptional_miss_returns_false,
             t_dispatch_maps_none_to_true]
    failed = 0
    for t in tests:
        try:
            t()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {t.__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_candidates_auto.py`（125 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_candidates_auto.py - 第3项：跨 App 共性词表归纳（AUTOP-NL-2026-10-22）

目标（用户原话）：关键词识别在「所有 App」层面具有共性，归纳后不必每个 App 单独写。
即：某条 sign 只写了
    candidates: [连续签到, 今日签到, 签到]
执行器也应自动并入全局表与跨 App 归纳词。

锁定四点（均可红）：
  1. mine_words 把「≥2 个 App 在同一行为下用过」的文案升级为共性词；
  2. 仅 1 个 App 用过的文案不升级（防把个别 App 的特有文案污染全局）；
  3. candidates_for 的结果包含归纳词（顺序：全局表 → 归纳 → App extra）；
  4. click_first 未写 candidates_from 时按 _current_bid 自动推断类别并入池。

运行：
    python -m pytest tests/test_candidates_auto.py -q
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

import candidates  # noqa: E402
from executor import Executor, LocateResult  # noqa: E402


def _app(name, words, bid="sign"):
    return {
        "name": name,
        "behaviors": {
            bid: [
                {"type": "click_first", "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": w} for w in words]},
            ]
        },
    }


def t_mine_promotes_words_used_by_two_apps():
    # 注意：连续签到/今日签到/签到 本就在全局 SIGN 表里，归纳层只需覆盖「表外新词」
    apps = [_app("A", ["跨App新词甲", "单App独有词"]), _app("B", ["跨App新词甲"])]
    mined = candidates.mine_words(apps)
    words = mined.get("sign", [])
    assert "跨App新词甲" in words, words
    assert "单App独有词" not in words, f"仅 1 个 App 用过，不应升级: {words}"
    print("OK  支持度 >=2 的文案升级为共性词，单 App 文案不升级")


def t_mine_skips_words_already_in_global_table():
    apps = [_app("A", ["签到"]), _app("B", ["签到"])]
    mined = candidates.mine_words(apps)
    assert "签到" not in mined.get("sign", []), "已在全局表的不重复列出"
    print("OK  已在全局表的文案不重复列出")


def t_candidates_for_includes_mined_words():
    apps = [_app("A", ["特有一号词"]), _app("B", ["特有一号词"])]
    candidates.register_apps(apps)
    merged = candidates.candidates_for("sign", None)
    assert "特有一号词" in merged, merged
    assert merged.index("今日签到") < merged.index("特有一号词"), "全局表应排在归纳词之前"
    candidates.register_apps([])  # 复位，避免污染其他用例
    print("OK  candidates_for 并入归纳词且顺序为 全局表→归纳")


def t_click_first_infers_category_from_current_bid():
    class _Rec:
        def log(self, *a, **k):
            pass

    class _Human:
        def delay(self, *a):
            pass

        def maybe_wander(self, *a):
            pass

        def think(self):
            pass

        def natural_swipe(self, d):
            pass

    apps = [_app("A", ["跨App共性词"]), _app("B", ["跨App共性词"])]
    candidates.register_apps(apps)

    seen = []

    def _locate(k, v, timeout=2.0, zone=None, screen_w=0):
        seen.append(v)
        return LocateResult(False, None, "测试未命中")

    ex = Executor("fake-serial", _Human(), _Rec(), dry_run=True, config={})
    ex.dry_run = False
    ex._locate = _locate
    ex._kb_candidates = lambda app, primary: ([primary], False)
    ex._kb_mark_fail = lambda app, prims: None
    ex._click_obj = lambda obj, relocate=None: None
    ex._app_extras = lambda app: {}

    ex._current_bid = "sign"
    ex._step_click_first({"type": "click_first", "timeout": 0.1}, "A", allow_vision=False)

    assert "跨App共性词" in seen, f"未按行为自动并入共性词，实际探测: {seen}"
    candidates.register_apps([])
    print("OK  未写 candidates_from 时按 _current_bid 自动并入共性词")


def t_stopwords_never_promoted():
    """弹窗关闭词/导航词绝不能被升级成 sign 共性词（否则会去点「关闭」）。"""
    apps = [_app("A", ["关闭", "暂不", "以后再说", "我", "我的", "签到页"]),
            _app("B", ["关闭", "暂不", "以后再说", "我", "我的", "签到页"])]
    mined = candidates.mine_words(apps)
    words = set(mined.get("sign", []))
    leaked = words & candidates.STOPWORDS
    assert not leaked, f"停用词泄漏进共性词表: {leaked}"
    assert "签到页" in words, words
    print("OK  停用词不进归纳结果")


def t_category_for_intent():
    assert candidates.category_for_intent("sign") == "sign"
    assert candidates.category_for_intent("reserve") == "sign"
    assert candidates.category_for_intent("unknown_behavior") is None
    print("OK  行为→词表类别映射")
```

### `tests/test_click_first_pool.py`（162 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_click_first_pool.py - R1：_step_click_first 知识库展开合一（AUTOP-REFINE-2026-10-21）

R1 把 _step_click_first 里「显式 candidates」与「词表 candidates」两段几乎相同的
kb 展开（_kb_candidates -> _parse_sel -> 去重入 pool）抽为共享 helper
`_expand_kb_pool(app_name, pool, primary)`，行为零变化：

  * 无知识库记录（kb_cands=[primary]）-> 加入 pool，primary 传 None（原语义）；
  * 知识库 disabled（kb_cands=[]）-> 返回 False，调用点跳过该候选；
  * 有记录且启用 -> 主 + alternatives 全部按 (k,v) 去重追加，primary 透传。

锁定三例（均可红：helper 未实现时 AttributeError）：
  1. 同一候选经 kb 展开只入池一次（去重）；
  2. 词表候选追加在显式之后（顺序）；
  3. kb disabled 候选被跳过。

运行：
    python -m pytest tests/test_click_first_pool.py -q
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from executor import Executor, LocateResult  # noqa: E402


class _Rec:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = []

    def log(self, event, app="", level="INFO"):
        self.lines.append(f"[{level}] {event}")


class _Human:
    def delay(self, *a):
        pass

    def maybe_wander(self, *a):
        pass

    def think(self):
        pass

    def natural_swipe(self, d):
        pass


class _HitList:
    """按 value 命中的 _locate 桩：记录收到的 (kind, value, zone)。"""

    def __init__(self, hits):
        self.hits = set(hits)
        self.calls = []

    def __call__(self, k, v, timeout=2.0, zone=None, screen_w=0):
        self.calls.append((k, v, zone))
        if v in self.hits:
            return LocateResult(True, {"kind": k, "value": v}, "测试命中")
        return LocateResult(False, None, "测试未命中")


def _mk(script=None):
    ex = Executor("fake-serial", _Human(), _Rec(), dry_run=True, config={})
    ex.dry_run = False
    ex._locate = script or _HitList(set())
    ex._kb_candidates = lambda app, primary: ([primary], False)
    ex._kb_mark_fail = lambda app, prims: None
    ex._click_obj = lambda obj, relocate=None: None
    ex._app_extras = lambda app: {}
    return ex


def t_expand_kb_pool_no_record_adds_primary_none():
    """无知识库记录：kb_cands=[primary]，入池且 primary=None（原语义）。"""
    ex = _mk()
    pool = []
    assert ex._expand_kb_pool("T", pool, "text=签到") is True
    assert pool == [("text", "签到", None)], pool
    print("OK  kb 无记录时入池且 primary=None")


def t_expand_kb_pool_disabled_returns_false():
    """知识库 disabled（kb_cands=[]）：返回 False，不入池。"""
    ex = _mk()
    ex._kb_candidates = lambda app, primary: ([], True)
    pool = [("text", "签到", None)]
    assert ex._expand_kb_pool("T", pool, "text=签到") is False
    assert pool == [("text", "签到", None)], "disabled 不应改动 pool"
    print("OK  kb disabled 返回 False 且不入池")


def t_expand_kb_pool_dedup_and_primary():
    """有记录且启用：主 + alternatives 展开，(k,v) 已存在的不重复追加。"""
    ex = _mk()
    ex._kb_candidates = lambda app, primary: (
        ["text=签到", "id=btn_sign", "text=签到"], True)
    pool = [("text", "签到", "text=签到")]
    assert ex._expand_kb_pool("T", pool, "text=签到") is True
    assert pool == [("text", "签到", "text=签到"),
                    ("id", "btn_sign", "text=签到")], pool
    print("OK  kb 展开去重、primary 透传")


def t_explicit_dup_enters_pool_once():
    """同一候选经 kb 展开只入池一次：重复显式候选只尝试一次。"""
    script = _HitList({"签到"})
    ex = _mk(script)
    act = {"type": "click_first",
           "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"},
                          {"type": "text", "value": "签到"}]}
    assert ex._step_click_first(act, "T") is True
    assert script.calls == [("text", "签到", None)], script.calls
    print("OK  显式重复候选只入池一次")


def t_words_after_explicit_order():
    """词表候选追加在显式之后：调用顺序先显式后词表（精确定向优先）。"""
    script = _HitList({"立即签到"})
    ex = _mk(script)
    act = {"type": "click_first",
           "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "今日签到"}],
           "candidates_from": "sign"}
    assert ex._step_click_first(act, "T") is True
    values = [c[1] for c in script.calls]
    assert values == ["今日签到", "立即签到"], values
    print("OK  词表候选追加在显式之后")


def t_kb_disabled_candidate_skipped():
    """kb disabled 候选被跳过：其 value 不会被尝试。"""
    script = _HitList({"签到"})
    ex = _mk(script)
    # 显式「今日签到」被知识库 disabled；词表「签到」正常
    ex._kb_candidates = lambda app, primary: (
        ([], True) if primary == "text=今日签到" else ([primary], False))
    act = {"type": "click_first",
           "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "今日签到"}],
           "candidates_from": "sign"}
    assert ex._step_click_first(act, "T") is True
    values = [c[1] for c in script.calls]
    assert "今日签到" not in values, values
    assert "签到" in values, values
    print("OK  kb disabled 候选被跳过、其余正常入池")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_click_first_scroll.py`（155 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_click_first_scroll.py - 第2步：click_first 滚动重试 scroll_retry（AUTOP-SELFSEARCH-2026-10-20）

用户原话：「应该总结这些app的领现金过程中…常用步骤的共性，使程序自己有一套思路，
不用依赖外部描述而能自己搜寻」——入口按钮常不在首屏（如「任务中心」在二级页），
引擎需要能自己翻页找。`scroll_retry: N` = 候选全未命中后自然向下滑动 N 次再重试。

语义：
  * 默认 0 = 旧行为（绝不滑动），回归保护；
  * scroll 只发生在「一轮全 miss」之后，首轮命中绝不滑动；
  * N 次滚动后仍未命中：行为与原逻辑完全一致（optional 放行 / 记 _last_fail）。

运行：
    python -m pytest tests/test_click_first_scroll.py -q
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from executor import Executor, LocateResult  # noqa: E402


class _Rec:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = []

    def log(self, event, app="", level="INFO"):
        self.lines.append(f"[{level}] {event}")


class _Human:
    def __init__(self):
        self.swipes = 0

    def delay(self, *a):
        pass

    def maybe_wander(self, *a):
        pass

    def think(self):
        pass

    def natural_swipe(self, d):
        self.swipes += 1


class _ScriptLocate:
    """按已发生滑动次数演进的 _locate 桩：滑满 hit_after 次才对 text 命中。"""

    def __init__(self, human, hit_after):
        self.human = human
        self.hit_after = hit_after
        self.calls = []

    def __call__(self, k, v, timeout=2.0):
        self.calls.append((k, v))
        if self.human.swipes >= self.hit_after:
            return LocateResult(True, {"kind": k, "value": v}, "测试命中")
        return LocateResult(False, None, "测试未命中")


def _mk(script, human):
    ex = Executor("fake-serial", human, _Rec(), dry_run=True, config={})
    ex.dry_run = False          # 让命中分支真正走到 _click_obj（桩）
    ex._locate = script
    ex._kb_candidates = lambda app, primary: ([primary], False)
    ex._kb_mark_fail = lambda app, prims: None
    ex._click_obj = lambda obj, relocate=None: None
    return ex


def _act(retry, optional=True):
    return {"type": "click_first",
            "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"}],
            "scroll_retry": retry, "optional": optional}


def t_default_zero_no_swipe():
    """无 scroll_retry（默认 0）：未命中不滑动（旧行为回归保护）。"""
    h = _Human()
    ex = _mk(_ScriptLocate(h, hit_after=99), h)
    assert ex._step_click_first(_act(0, optional=False), "T") is False
    assert h.swipes == 0, f"默认不应滑动，实际 {h.swipes}"
    assert ex._last_fail and "所有候选均未出现" in ex._last_fail, ex._last_fail
    print("OK  默认 scroll_retry=0 不滑动")


def t_scroll_one_then_hit():
    """scroll_retry=1：首轮全 miss -> 滑动一次 -> 命中并点击。"""
    h = _Human()
    ex = _mk(_ScriptLocate(h, hit_after=1), h)
    assert ex._step_click_first(_act(1), "T") is True
    assert h.swipes == 1, f"应恰好滑动 1 次，实际 {h.swipes}"
    assert any("多候选命中并点击" in l for l in ex.recorder.lines), ex.recorder.lines
    print("OK  scroll_retry=1 滑动一次后命中并点击")


def t_scroll_exhausted_returns_false():
    """scroll_retry=2：两轮全 miss -> 恰好滑动 2 次 -> 返回 False。

    2026-10-29 复核：本用例原先用 optional=True（_act 默认）断言 False，看起来
    像在钉一个矛盾，其实钉的是**必须保留**的语义 —— click_first 常作为 any_of 的
    分支，any_of 靠这个返回值判「分支是否命中」。若 optional 全 miss 返回 True，
    any_of 会在第 1 个分支就收工，后面的兜底分支永不执行。
    故保留 False，并补一条用例钉住"日志不得再写放行"。
    """
    h = _Human()
    ex = _mk(_ScriptLocate(h, hit_after=99), h)
    assert ex._step_click_first(_act(2, optional=False), "T") is False
    assert h.swipes == 2, f"应恰好滑动 2 次，实际 {h.swipes}"
    print("OK  scroll_retry=2 滑满仍 miss 返回 False")


def t_scroll_exhausted_optional_still_false_and_log_honest():
    """optional=True 且全 miss：返回值仍是 False（any_of 依赖），日志不得说"放行"。

    这两件事必须同时成立：返回值承载 any_of 的分支判定，日志承载人的判断。
    历史上二者相反（日志写放行、返回值 False），读日志的人会以为这步没失败。
    """
    h = _Human()
    ex = _mk(_ScriptLocate(h, hit_after=99), h)
    assert ex._step_click_first(_act(2), "T") is False
    assert h.swipes == 2, f"应恰好滑动 2 次，实际 {h.swipes}"
    joined = "\n".join(ex.recorder.lines)
    assert "optional 放行" not in joined, joined
    assert "optional 仅抑制告警" in joined, joined
    print("OK  optional 全 miss 仍返回 False，且日志不再谎称放行")


def t_no_scroll_when_first_round_hits():
    """首轮即命中：绝不滑动（scroll 只发生在全 miss 之后）。"""
    h = _Human()
    ex = _mk(_ScriptLocate(h, hit_after=0), h)
    assert ex._step_click_first(_act(2), "T") is True
    assert h.swipes == 0, f"首轮命中不应滑动，实际 {h.swipes}"
    print("OK  首轮命中不滑动")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_click_obj_bounds.py`（155 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_click_obj_bounds.py - 锁 _click_obj 元素 bounds API 坑（2026-10-21 二轮回归实崩）

背景：回归 7/12「命中」全是坐标兜底，把 `_click_obj` 从不能被真机走到的
状态遮住；二轮文案一旦命中即崩：
  AttributeError: 'UiObject' object has no attribute 'rect'
u2 UiObject 的正确 API 是 `.bounds()` -> (lx, ly, rx, ry) plain tuple。

覆盖：
  1. 真机形态（.bounds() tuple）-> 点中心；
  2. 测试/探针假元素（.rect 属性对象）-> 兼容点中心；
  3. dry_run -> 零点击；
  4. 统一走 human.tap（F2 入口），不裸调 d.click。
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from locator import Locator  # noqa: E402
from behavior_runner import StepError  # noqa: E402


class _Human:
    def __init__(self):
        self.taps = []

    def jitter_point(self, x, y):
        return x, y

    def tap(self, d, x, y):
        self.taps.append((x, y))


class _D:
    def click(self, x, y):  # 直连 click 若被调用说明绕过了 human.tap
        raise AssertionError("_click_obj 不得裸调 d.click（须走 human.tap）")


class _Loc:
    """只借 Locator._click_obj 的真实实现，不构造完整 Locator。"""

    def __init__(self, human, dry_run=False):
        self.human = human
        self.d = _D()
        self.dry_run = dry_run

    _click_obj = Locator._click_obj


class _U2Obj:
    """真机形态：u2 UiObject，无 .rect，.bounds() 回 tuple。"""

    def bounds(self):
        return (100, 200, 300, 400)


class _RectObj:
    """假元素形态：.rect 属性对象（tests/test_dismiss_popups.py 同款）。"""

    @property
    def rect(self):
        class _R:
            left, top, right, bottom = 100, 200, 300, 400
        return _R()


def t_u2_bounds_tuple():
    h = _Human()
    _Loc(h)._click_obj(_U2Obj())
    assert h.taps == [(200, 300)], h.taps
    print("t_u2_bounds_tuple OK")


def t_rect_attr_compat():
    h = _Human()
    _Loc(h)._click_obj(_RectObj())
    assert h.taps == [(200, 300)], h.taps
    print("t_rect_attr_compat OK")


def t_dry_run_no_click():
    h = _Human()
    _Loc(h, dry_run=True)._click_obj(_U2Obj())
    assert h.taps == [], h.taps
    print("t_dry_run_no_click OK")


def t_goes_through_human_tap():
    """ bounds 非偶数轴：(lx+rx)//2 取整；且只许 human.tap 一处出口。"""
    class _Odd:
        def bounds(self):
            return (101, 201, 302, 403)
    h = _Human()
    _Loc(h)._click_obj(_Odd())
    assert h.taps == [((101 + 302) // 2, (201 + 403) // 2)], h.taps
    print("t_goes_through_human_tap OK")


class _StaleObj:
    """真机竞态形态：bounds() 抛 RPCUnknownError(StaleObjectException)。"""

    def __init__(self, box=(100, 200, 300, 400), boom=True):
        self._box, self._boom = box, boom

    def bounds(self):
        if self._boom:
            raise RuntimeError("Unknown RPC error: -32001 StaleObjectException")
        return self._box


def t_stale_relocate_retry():
    """失效后 relocate 重定位成功 -> 点新元素中心（快手 2026-10-21 实崩场景）。"""
    h = _Human()
    fresh = _U2Obj()
    _Loc(h)._click_obj(_StaleObj(), relocate=lambda: fresh)
    assert h.taps == [(200, 300)], h.taps
    print("t_stale_relocate_retry OK")


def t_stale_no_relocate_raises_steperror():
    """失效且无 relocate -> StepError（上层记「步骤失败」不崩）。"""
    h = _Human()
    try:
        _Loc(h)._click_obj(_StaleObj())
    except StepError as e:
        assert "元素已失效" in str(e), str(e)
        assert h.taps == []
        print("t_stale_no_relocate_raises_steperror OK")
        return
    raise AssertionError("应抛 StepError")


def t_stale_relocate_miss_raises():
    """relocate 也找不到 -> StepError，section 不崩。"""
    h = _Human()
    try:
        _Loc(h)._click_obj(_StaleObj(), relocate=lambda: None)
    except StepError:
        assert h.taps == []
        print("t_stale_relocate_miss_raises OK")
        return
    raise AssertionError("应抛 StepError")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    t_u2_bounds_tuple()
    t_rect_attr_compat()
    t_dry_run_no_click()
    t_goes_through_human_tap()
    t_stale_relocate_retry()
    t_stale_no_relocate_raises_steperror()
    t_stale_relocate_miss_raises()
    print("ALL click_obj bounds TESTS PASSED")
```

### `tests/test_config_loader.py`（114 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_config_loader.py - config_loader 单元测试（C9）

覆盖：旧单文件原样返回 / 拆分后拼接 / 顺序保持 / money_behaviors /
app_config_path 定位 / latest_config_mtime 覆盖 apps 目录。

运行：
    python tests/test_config_loader.py
"""
import os
import sys
import tempfile

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

try:
    import yaml
except ImportError:  # noqa: E402
    import miniyaml as yaml  # noqa: E402

from config_loader import (  # noqa: E402
    load_config, money_behaviors, app_config_path,
    safe_app_name, latest_config_mtime,
)


def _write(path, data):
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        yaml.safe_dump(data, f, allow_unicode=True, sort_keys=False)


def t_old_single_file_returns_as_is():
    """apps 非空 → 旧单文件原样返回（向后兼容），不碰 apps_dir。"""
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
        p = os.path.join(td, "config.yaml")
        _write(p, {"device": {"serial": "x"}, "apps": [{"name": "A"}]})
        cfg = load_config(p)
        assert cfg["apps"] == [{"name": "A"}], cfg["apps"]
        assert cfg["device"]["serial"] == "x"
    print("OK  旧单文件（apps 非空）原样返回")


def t_split_dir_merged_in_order():
    """apps 空 + apps_dir → 按文件名序拼接（序号前缀保序）。"""
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
        p = os.path.join(td, "config.yaml")
        ad = os.path.join(td, "apps")
        _write(os.path.join(ad, "01_B.yaml"), {"name": "B"})
        _write(os.path.join(ad, "02_A.yaml"), {"name": "A"})
        _write(p, {"apps_dir": "apps", "apps": []})
        cfg = load_config(p)
        assert [a["name"] for a in cfg["apps"]] == ["B", "A"], cfg["apps"]
        assert cfg["apps_dir"] == "apps"  # 顶层透传
    print("OK  拆分后按文件名序拼接（保序）")


def t_money_behaviors_default_and_custom():
    assert money_behaviors({}) == {"withdraw"}
    assert money_behaviors({"money_behaviors": ["withdraw", "cash"]}) == \
        {"withdraw", "cash"}
    print("OK  money_behaviors 缺省/自定义")


def t_app_config_path_resolves_prefixed_file():
    """app_config_path 在带序号前缀的文件名里按 safe_name 定位。"""
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
        ad = os.path.join(td, "apps")
        os.makedirs(ad)
        with open(os.path.join(ad, "03_趣头条.yaml"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("name: 趣头条\n")
        got = app_config_path("趣头条", {"apps_dir": ad}, td)
        assert os.path.basename(got) == "03_趣头条.yaml", got
    print("OK  app_config_path 定位带序号前缀的 App 文件")


def t_safe_app_name_strips_bad_chars():
    assert safe_app_name("抖音极速版") == "抖音极速版"
    assert safe_app_name("A/B:C") == "A_B_C"
    assert safe_app_name("") == "app"
    print("OK  safe_app_name 清洗")


def t_latest_config_mtime_covers_apps_dir():
    """拆分后主文件 mtime 变化不再等于缓存键——必须覆盖 apps 目录。"""
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
        p = os.path.join(td, "config.yaml")
        ad = os.path.join(td, "apps")
        _write(os.path.join(ad, "01_A.yaml"), {"name": "A"})
        _write(p, {"apps_dir": "apps", "apps": []})
        m0 = latest_config_mtime(p)
        assert m0 >= os.path.getmtime(os.path.join(ad, "01_A.yaml"))
        # 只动 apps 文件，缓存键也应变化
        os.utime(os.path.join(ad, "01_A.yaml"), (m0 + 100, m0 + 100))
        m1 = latest_config_mtime(p)
        assert m1 > m0, "apps 目录文件变更应推高 latest_config_mtime"
    print("OK  latest_config_mtime 覆盖 apps 目录")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_config_split_parity.py`（84 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_config_split_parity.py - 拆分前后 apps 深比较对拍（C9 不变式）

拆分前：config.yaml.pre_split_<ts> 备份内的内联 apps；
拆分后：config_loader.load_config 拼接 config/apps/ 的结果。
要求深比较相等（数量、顺序、字段、值全一致）。

基线维护（C9 不变式的活基线）：
  * `_backup_pre_fix/config.yaml.split_baseline` 存在时以其为准；
    否则回退到最后一个 `config.yaml.pre_split_*` 历史快照。
  * 有意变更 App 配置（新增/修改 behavior）后，测试会如实报差异——
    这是设计行为（防误改），确认变更无误后刷新基线：
        python -c "import sys; sys.path.insert(0,'.'); import yaml; from config_loader import load_config; \
cfg=yaml.safe_load(open('config.yaml',encoding='utf-8').read()) or {}; \
cfg['apps']=load_config('config.yaml')['apps']; \
open('_backup_pre_fix/config.yaml.split_baseline','w',encoding='utf-8').write(yaml.safe_dump(cfg, allow_unicode=True, sort_keys=False))"

运行：
    python tests/test_config_split_parity.py
"""
import glob
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

try:
    import yaml
except ImportError:  # noqa: E402
    import miniyaml as yaml  # noqa: E402

from config_loader import load_config  # noqa: E402

CONFIG = os.path.join(ROOT, "config.yaml")
# 规范基线：有意变更 App 配置（加/改 behavior）后，由人刷新一次，
# 刷新方法见下方 _before_apps 注释；历史 pre_split_* 快照仅作向后兼容回退。
BASELINE = os.path.join(ROOT, "_backup_pre_fix", "config.yaml.split_baseline")
BACKUPS = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(ROOT, "_backup_pre_fix",
                                        "config.yaml.pre_split_*")))


def _baseline_path():
    if os.path.exists(BASELINE):
        return BASELINE
    return BACKUPS[-1] if BACKUPS else None


def _before_apps():
    path = _baseline_path()
    if not path:
        return None
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        cfg = yaml.safe_load(f) or {}
    return cfg.get("apps") or []


def t_split_parity_deep_equal():
    before = _before_apps()
    if before is None:
        print("SKIP 未找到 pre_split 备份，跳过（首次拆分场景）")
        return
    after = load_config(CONFIG).get("apps") or []
    assert len(after) == len(before), \
        f"apps 数量不一致: before={len(before)} after={len(after)}"
    for i, (b, a) in enumerate(zip(before, after)):
        assert a == b, f"第 {i + 1} 个 app 不一致\nbefore={b}\nafter={a}"
    print(f"OK  拆分前后 apps 深比较相等（{len(after)} 个，顺序一致）")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_dismiss_popups.py`（282 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_dismiss_popups.py - F1 全局弹窗清扫（AUTOP-FEAT-2026-10-20）无需真机

用户原话三条需求的落点：
  1. 「遇到各种不是程序自身引起的弹窗，都可以看到就关闭」
  2. 「关闭弹窗的方式还可以尝试系统的回退键」  -> 文案 -> id -> 右上角 -> BACK
  3. 「不是主动观看的广告，也可以跳过」        -> protect_ad 关掉正在看的广告文案

硬护栏（2026-10-20 蜗牛睡眠 + 资金红线教训）：
  - 资金类黑名单命中 -> "blocked"：停扫且禁用 BACK
  - 干净屏（无广告特征）不动坐标/返回键

运行：
    python tests/test_dismiss_popups.py
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from behavior_runner import (  # noqa: E402
    BehaviorRunner, DEFAULT_POPUP_TEXTS, POPUP_BLACKLIST_TEXTS)


class _Rec:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = []

    def log(self, event, app="", level="INFO"):
        self.lines.append((level, event))


class _Human:
    def __init__(self):
        self.thoughts = 0

    def jitter_point(self, x, y):
        return x, y

    def think(self):
        self.thoughts += 1


class _Obj:
    def __init__(self, label):
        self.label = label

    @property
    def rect(self):
        class _R:
            left, top, right, bottom = 100, 200, 300, 400
        return _R()


class _Size:
    def __init__(self, w, h):
        self.width, self.height = w, h


class _D:
    """window_size 返回 plain tuple（2026-10-20 真机实测形态）。"""

    def __init__(self, ws=(1080, 2340)):
        self._ws = ws
        self.clicked = []   # (x, y)
        self.pressed = []   # key

    def window_size(self):
        return self._ws

    def click(self, x, y):
        self.clicked.append((x, y))

    def press(self, key):
        self.pressed.append(key)


def _mk(present=None, xml="", ws=(1080, 2340)):
    """present: {(kind, value): label} _locate 命中表；xml: dump 内容。"""
    br = BehaviorRunner.__new__(BehaviorRunner)
    br.recorder = _Rec()
    br.human = _Human()
    br.d = _D(ws)
    br.dry_run = False
    present = present or {}

    def _dump_safe():
        return xml

    def _locate(kind, value, timeout=6.0):
        if (kind, value) in present:
            return type("R", (), {"hit": True, "obj": _Obj(present[(kind, value)])})()
        return type("R", (), {"hit": False, "obj": None})()

    clicked_labels = []

    def _click_obj(obj):
        clicked_labels.append(obj.label)

    br._dump_safe = _dump_safe
    br._locate = _locate
    br._click_obj = _click_obj
    br._clicked_labels = clicked_labels
    return br


def t_text_priority():
    """文案优先：多个关闭文案同时在场，点 DEFAULT_POPUP_TEXTS 首个命中。"""
    br = _mk(present={("text", "我知道了"): "btn-know"})
    # 「我知道了」在默认表第 8 位；assert 它被点且没有动坐标/返回键
    ok = br._popup_close_one(DEFAULT_POPUP_TEXTS, [], corner=True,
                             back_key=True, app_name="X", rnd=1)
    assert ok is True
    assert br._clicked_labels == ["btn-know"], br._clicked_labels
    assert br.d.clicked == [] and br.d.pressed == []
    print("t_text_priority OK")


def t_blacklist_blocks_back():
    """资金黑名单熔断：命中即停扫且禁用 BACK。"""
    xml = "<node text='确认提现'/>"
    br = _mk(xml=xml)
    ret = br._step_dismiss_popups(
        {"max_rounds": 3, "blacklist": POPUP_BLACKLIST_TEXTS}, "X")
    assert ret == "blocked", ret
    assert br.d.pressed == [], "资金屏禁止按 BACK（提现确认必须留给人）"
    assert any("熔断" in e and "资金" in e for _, e in br.recorder.lines)
    print("t_blacklist_blocks_back OK")


def t_blacklist_extra_merge():
    """extra_blacklist 配置追加：屏上只有自定义资金文案也应熔断。"""
    xml = "<node text='红包余额已发放'/>"
    br = _mk(xml=xml)
    ret = br._step_dismiss_popups(
        {"max_rounds": 2, "blacklist": POPUP_BLACKLIST_TEXTS + ["红包余额"]},
        "X")
    assert ret == "blocked", ret
    assert br.d.pressed == []
    print("t_blacklist_extra_merge OK")


def t_watch_protects_ad():
    """watch 流程 protect_ad：正在看的广告文案不关，落到右上角坐标。"""
    # 屏上有「跳过」按钮（正在看的广告）与广告特征
    br = _mk(present={("text", "跳过"): "ad-skip"}, xml="<node text='广告'/>")
    act = {"max_rounds": 1, "protect_ad": True}
    ret = br._step_dismiss_popups(act, "X")
    assert ret is True
    assert br._clicked_labels == [], "protect_ad 下不得点『跳过』"
    assert len(br.d.clicked) == 1, "应落右上角坐标兜底"
    x, y = br.d.clicked[0]
    assert abs(x - 1080 * 0.93) < 2 and abs(y - 2340 * 0.11) < 2, (x, y)
    print("t_watch_protects_ad OK")


def t_watch_protect_off():
    """非 watch（protect_ad=False）：「跳过」应正常关。"""
    br = _mk(present={("text", "跳过"): "ad-skip"})
    ret = br._step_dismiss_popups({"max_rounds": 1, "protect_ad": False}, "X")
    assert ret is True
    assert br._clicked_labels == ["ad-skip"]
    assert br.d.clicked == [] and br.d.pressed == []
    print("t_watch_protect_off OK")


def t_back_fallback_order():
    """顺序最后一级：有广告特征 + corner_fallback=False -> 系统返回键。"""
    br = _mk(xml="<node text='跳过广告 5'/>")
    act = {"max_rounds": 1, "corner_fallback": False, "back_key": True}
    ret = br._step_dismiss_popups(act, "X")
    assert ret is True
    assert br.d.pressed == ["back"], br.d.pressed
    assert br.d.clicked == []
    print("t_back_fallback_order OK")


def t_clean_screen_no_action():
    """干净屏零交互：无文案/id 命中且无广告特征 -> 不动坐标不动 BACK。"""
    br = _mk(xml="<node text='签到领金币'/>")
    ret = br._step_dismiss_popups({"max_rounds": 3}, "X")
    assert ret is False, ret
    assert br.d.clicked == [] and br.d.pressed == []
    print("t_clean_screen_no_action OK")


def t_multi_rounds():
    """多轮：每轮关一个，最多 max_rounds 轮后返回 True。"""
    present = {("text", "取消"): "btn-cancel"}
    br = _mk(present=present)
    ret = br._step_dismiss_popups({"max_rounds": 3}, "X")
    assert ret is True
    assert br._clicked_labels == ["btn-cancel"] * 3, br._clicked_labels
    assert br.human.thoughts >= 3
    print("t_multi_rounds OK")


def t_sweep_disabled():
    """未启用：_popups_enabled=False 时零扫描零交互。"""
    br = _mk()
    br._popups_enabled = False
    br._maybe_sweep_popups("sign", "sign", "X")
    assert br.d.clicked == [] and br.d.pressed == []
    assert br.recorder.lines == []
    print("t_sweep_disabled OK")


def t_sweep_merge_extras():
    """_maybe_sweep_popups 合并 extra_texts/extra_ids/extra_blacklist。"""
    br = _mk()
    br._popups_enabled = True
    br._popups_cfg = {
        "enabled": True, "max_rounds": 2,
        "extra_texts": ["狠心拒绝"], "extra_ids": ["com.x.iv_close"],
        "extra_blacklist": ["零钱"],
    }
    captured = {}

    def _cap(act, app_name):
        captured.update(act)

    br._step_dismiss_popups = _cap
    br._maybe_sweep_popups("sign", "b_sign", "X")
    assert "狠心拒绝" in captured["texts"]
    assert all(t in captured["texts"] for t in DEFAULT_POPUP_TEXTS)
    assert "com.x.iv_close" in captured["ids"]
    assert "零钱" in captured["blacklist"]
    assert all(b in captured["blacklist"] for b in POPUP_BLACKLIST_TEXTS)
    assert captured["protect_ad"] is False   # sign 非 watch
    print("t_sweep_merge_extras OK")


def t_sweep_watch_flag():
    """watch intent / watch 行为 id 任一命中 -> protect_ad=True。"""
    for intent, bid in (("watch", "watch"), ("sign", "watch_ad")):
        br = _mk()
        br._popups_enabled = True
        br._popups_cfg = {"enabled": True}
        captured = {}
        br._step_dismiss_popups = lambda a, n, c=captured: c.update(a)
        br._maybe_sweep_popups(intent, bid, "X")
        assert captured["protect_ad"] is True, (intent, bid)
    print("t_sweep_watch_flag OK")


def t_dry_run_no_device():
    """dry-run：只记日志不碰设备。"""
    br = _mk()
    br.dry_run = True
    ret = br._step_dismiss_popups({"max_rounds": 3}, "X")
    assert ret is False
    assert br.d.clicked == [] and br.d.pressed == []
    assert any("dry-run" in e for _, e in br.recorder.lines)
    print("t_dry_run_no_device OK")


def t_win_wh_tuple():
    """corner 兜底坐标用 plain tuple 的 window_size（崩溃族回归）。"""
    br = _mk(xml="<node text='广告'/>", ws=(1080, 2340))
    ret = br._popup_close_one(DEFAULT_POPUP_TEXTS, [], corner=True,
                              back_key=False, app_name="X", rnd=1)
    assert ret is True
    assert br.d.clicked == [(1004, 257)], br.d.clicked
    print("t_win_wh_tuple OK")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    t_text_priority()
    t_blacklist_blocks_back()
    t_blacklist_extra_merge()
    t_watch_protects_ad()
    t_watch_protect_off()
    t_back_fallback_order()
    t_clean_screen_no_action()
    t_multi_rounds()
    t_sweep_disabled()
    t_sweep_merge_extras()
    t_sweep_watch_flag()
    t_dry_run_no_device()
    t_win_wh_tuple()
    print("ALL dismiss_popups TESTS PASSED")
```

### `tests/test_diversify_sensor.py`（178 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
tests/test_diversify_sensor.py - 行为序列多样化 / 随机分布升级 / 传感器模拟 / IP 助手

运行：python -m tests.test_diversify_sensor（从项目根目录运行）
"""
import os
import sys
import tempfile

_HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
_PROJ = os.path.dirname(_HERE)
if _PROJ not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _PROJ)

from human import Human
from sensor_sim import SensorProfile, stats, is_dead, self_check

import executor as executor_mod


class _Recorder:
    def log(self, *a, **k):
        pass


def _human(human_cfg=None):
    """Human 入参需顶层含 human 节。"""
    return Human({"human": human_cfg or {}, "trajectory": None})


def _make_executor(human, cfg):
    rec = _Recorder()
    return executor_mod.Executor(None, human, rec, dry_run=True, config=cfg)


# ---------- 随机分布升级（对数正态） ----------
def test_lognormal_in_range_and_clamped():
    human = _human()  # 默认 use_lognormal=True
    lo, hi = human.delay_between
    for _ in range(500):
        v = human._sample(human.delay_between, human.delay_between_ln)
        assert lo <= v <= hi, f"采样越界: {v}"
    print("[lognormal] 500 次采样全部落在区间内")


def test_lognormal_right_skewed():
    """对数正态应偏态：大部分值贴近低端（人类间隔多数短、偶尔长）。"""
    human = _human()
    vals = [human._sample(human.delay_between, True) for _ in range(2000)]
    lo, hi = human.delay_between
    mid = (lo + hi) / 2
    below = sum(1 for v in vals if v < mid)
    ratio = below / len(vals)
    assert ratio > 0.55, f"期望偏态(低端更多)，实际 low 占比 {ratio:.3f}"
    assert max(vals) <= hi and min(vals) >= lo
    print(f"[lognormal] 偏态检查: 低于中值占比={ratio:.3f}")


def test_uniform_fallback():
    human = _human({"delay_between_actions":
                    {"range": [0.5, 1.5], "dist": "uniform"}})
    assert human.delay_between_ln is False, "显式 uniform 应关闭对数正态"
    vals = [human._sample(human.delay_between, False) for _ in range(1000)]
    assert max(vals) <= 1.5 and min(vals) >= 0.5
    print("[uniform] 显式 uniform 生效")


def test_parse_delay_dict():
    lo, hi = Human._parse_delay({"range": [1.0, 3.0], "dist": "uniform"})[0]
    assert (lo, hi) == (1.0, 3.0)
    lo, hi = Human._parse_delay([0.2, 0.9])[0]
    assert (lo, hi) == (0.2, 0.9)
    print("[parse_delay] dict/list 两种写法解析一致")


# ---------- 行为序列多样化（候选模板 + random 分支） ----------
def test_pick_variant_single_template_passthrough():
    human = _human()
    ex = _make_executor(human, {"diversify": {"variant_prob": 0.5}})
    single = [{"type": "wait", "seconds": 1}]
    assert ex._pick_variant(single, "app", "sign") is single
    print("[variant] 单模板原样透传")


def test_pick_variant_multi_avoid_repeat():
    human = _human()
    ex = _make_executor(human, {"diversify": {"variant_prob": 1.0}})
    choices = [[{"type": "wait", "seconds": 1}],
               [{"type": "wait", "seconds": 2}],
               [{"type": "wait", "seconds": 3}]]
    first = ex._pick_variant(choices, "app", "a")
    idx0 = ex._variant_last["a"]
    second = ex._pick_variant(choices, "app", "a")
    assert ex._variant_last["a"] != idx0, "应避免连续使用同一模板"
    assert any(second == c for c in choices), "返回值必须来自候选集"
    print("[variant] 多候选命中且避开上次")


def test_step_random_dispatch():
    """random 分支原语：随机选中且正确分发（dry-run，无设备，不失手）。"""
    human = _human()
    ex = _make_executor(human, {})
    for _ in range(60):
        ex._dispatch({
            "type": "random",
            "choices": [{"type": "wait", "seconds": 0},
                        [{"type": "back"}, {"type": "wait", "seconds": 0}],
                        {"type": "think"}],
        }, "app", ritual=False)
    print("[random] 60 次 random 分支分发无异常")


def test_pick_variant_last_key_update():
    human = _human()
    ex = _make_executor(human, {"diversify": {"variant_prob": 1.0}})
    choices = [[{"type": "wait", "seconds": 1}], [{"type": "wait", "seconds": 2}]]
    ex._pick_variant(choices, "app", "s")
    assert "s" in ex._variant_last, "应记录上次用过的模板"
    print("[variant] 最近使用记录已写入")


# ---------- 传感器模拟与死寂检测 ----------
def test_sensor_alive_all_modes():
    alive, res = self_check()
    assert alive, f"生成器不应退化成死寂: {res}"
    print("[sensor] 4 个模式数据均判为'活着':", res)


def test_sensor_dead_detection():
    dead = [dict(t=i * 0.01, acc=[0.0, 0.0, 9.8], lin=[0, 0, 0],
                 gyro=[0.0, 0.0, 0.0], mag=[0.0, 0.0, 0.0]) for i in range(300)]
    assert is_dead(dead), "全死寂数据应被识别"
    print("[sensor] 死寂数据被识别")


def test_sensor_csv_export():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
        path = os.path.join(td, "p.csv")
        p = SensorProfile(fs=50, duration=1.0, mode="resting", seed=1)
```
