```python
        p.write_csv(path)
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            head = f.readline().strip()
        assert head.startswith("t,acc_x"), f"CSV 头异常: {head}"
        assert os.stat(path).st_size > 200, "CSV 应有内容"
    print("[sensor] CSV 导出正常")


def test_sensor_stats_magnitude():
    p = SensorProfile(fs=100, duration=3.0, mode="walking", seed=7)
    s = stats(p.rows)
    assert s["acc_z_std"] > 0.03, "步行时垂直加速度应有明显波动"
    print(f"[sensor] stats 量级合理 acc_z_std={s['acc_z_std']:.4f}")


# ---------- IP 助手（纯逻辑，不触发真 adb） ----------
def test_help_proxy_format():
    import helper_proxy
    assert helper_proxy.set_proxy("no-colon")[0] is False, "错误格式应拒绝"
    assert "代理格式" in helper_proxy.set_proxy("x")[1]
    print("[proxy] 格式校验正常")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    fns = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("test_")]
    passed, failed = 0, []
    for fn in fns:
        try:
            fn()
            passed += 1
        except Exception as e:
            import traceback
            traceback.print_exc()
            failed.append((fn.__name__, str(e)))
    print(f"\n== 结果: {passed}/{len(fns)} 通过 ==")
    for name, err in failed:
        print(f"  FAIL {name}: {err}")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_executor_reset.py`（144 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_executor_reset.py - 执行器的「行为间复位」接线（无需真机）

背景（真实缺陷）：
    一个 App 上要跑多个行为（签到 / 看视频 / 提现），它们**共享同一个 App
    实例**。`run_app_flow` 原本是「一个 App 只 `_open_app` 一次，然后行为
    串行执行」，第 N 个行为跑到二级/三级页后留下的页面会污染第 N+1 个行为。

    实测：喜番免费短剧 sign 三级导航（我的 → 福利中心 → 签到）后停在
    「签到」页，紧随的 watch 比例坐标点飞到了 com.eggflower.read
    （蛋花免费小说，序列里的上一个 App）—— 一个 App 的收益被另一个 App 污染。

正确行为：
    1. 第 2 个及以后的行为开始前必须调用复位；
    2. 已经在顶层页时**不能按 BACK**（多数 App 在「我的」tab 按 BACK 会直接
       退出 App，白吃一次冷启动）；
    3. dry_run 下不得触碰设备。

运行：
    python tests/test_executor_reset.py
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

import executor as E  # noqa: E402


class _Rec:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = []

    def log(self, msg, app=None, level="INFO"):
        self.lines.append((level, str(msg)))


class _Human:
    def think(self):
        pass

    def after_open(self):
        pass

    def close_naturally(self, d):
        pass


def _mk(dry_run=True):
    return E.Executor("serial", _Human(), _Rec(), dry_run=dry_run,
                      config={}, base_dir=ROOT)


APP = {
    "name": "测试App",
    "package": "com.test.app",
    "behaviors": {
        "sign": [{"type": "click_text", "text": "签到"}],
        "watch": [{"type": "click_text", "text": "看视频"}],
        "withdraw": [{"type": "click_text", "text": "提现"}],
    },
}


def t_dry_run_does_not_touch_device():
    """dry_run 下跑完三个行为，不得有任何设备交互。"""
    ex = _mk(dry_run=True)
    ex.run_app_flow(APP, [("sign", "签到"), ("watch", "看视频"),
                          ("withdraw", "提现")])
    assert ex.d is None, "dry_run 不应连设备"
    joined = "\n".join(m for _l, m in ex.recorder.lines)
    assert "[dry-run]" in joined, joined
    assert "行为[sign]" in joined and "行为[watch]" in joined
    print("OK  dry_run 下不触碰设备且计划完整")


def t_reset_is_noop_in_dry_run():
    """_reset_to_landing 在 dry_run 下必须直接返回。"""
    ex = _mk(dry_run=True)
    ex._reset_to_landing("com.test.app", "测试App")
    joined = "\n".join(m for _l, m in ex.recorder.lines)
    assert "复位" not in joined, f"dry_run 不该执行复位: {joined}"
    print("OK  dry_run 下复位为空操作")


def t_reset_called_between_behaviors():
    """第 2 个及以后的行为前必须复位（用计数替身验证接线）。

    注意：dry_run 会在进入循环前就返回，所以这里必须走**真实**循环路径，
    把设备相关方法换成替身。
    """
    ex = _mk(dry_run=False)
    calls = []
    ran = []
    ex._open_app = lambda pkg, name, reset=False: None
    ex._run_actions = lambda actions, name, bid: ran.append(bid) or True
    ex._reset_to_landing = lambda pkg, name, max_back=3: calls.append(pkg)
    ex.run_app_flow(APP, [("sign", "签到"), ("watch", "看视频"),
                          ("withdraw", "提现")])
    assert ran == ["sign", "watch", "withdraw"], ran
    assert len(calls) == 2, f"3 个行为应复位 2 次，实际 {len(calls)}"
    assert all(c == "com.test.app" for c in calls), calls
    print("OK  行为之间复位接线正确（3 个行为 -> 2 次复位）")


def t_reset_not_called_for_single_behavior():
    """只有一个行为时不需要复位。"""
    ex = _mk(dry_run=False)
    calls = []
    ex._open_app = lambda pkg, name, reset=False: None
    ex._run_actions = lambda actions, name, bid: True
    ex._reset_to_landing = lambda pkg, name, max_back=3: calls.append(pkg)
    ex.run_app_flow(APP, [("sign", "签到")])
    assert calls == [], calls
    print("OK  单行为不复位")


def t_at_top_level_true_when_ui_page_missing():
    """ui_page 缺失时关闭复位功能，退化为旧行为而不是崩溃。"""
    ex = _mk(dry_run=True)
    saved = E.TOP_LEVEL_LABELS
    try:
        E.TOP_LEVEL_LABELS = set()
        assert ex._at_top_level() is True
    finally:
        E.TOP_LEVEL_LABELS = saved
    print("OK  ui_page 缺失时优雅降级（关闭复位，不崩）")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_facade_parity.py`（111 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_facade_parity.py - D 批「门面等价性」测试

D 批把 executor.py 1010 行三分拆为 device_session.py / locator.py /
behavior_runner.py，executor.py 收缩为 ~160 行门面（多继承 mixin）。

本测试对 A/B 终态方法清单逐一断言 Executor 门面 hasattr + callable，
防止搬迁漏方法或改名；同时抽查三个部件类的方法归属，防止职责漂移。

运行：
    python -m pytest tests/test_facade_parity.py -q
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

import executor  # noqa: E402
from device_session import DeviceSession  # noqa: E402
from locator import Locator, LocateResult, _parse_sel  # noqa: E402
from behavior_runner import BehaviorRunner, StepError  # noqa: E402


# A/B 终态方法清单（三分拆前 executor.py 中 Executor 的全部可调用方法）
FACADE_METHODS = [
    # 设备会话（device_session）
    "connect", "_at_top_level", "_reset_to_landing", "_open_app",
    "_close_app", "_wait_page_ready", "_dump_safe",
    # 元素定位（locator）
    "_locate", "_label_of", "_pick_best", "_click_obj",
    "_kb_candidates", "_kb_mark_fail", "_locate_with_kb",
    "_click_tracked", "_vision_or_miss", "_vision_click",
    # 行为执行（behavior_runner）
    "run_app_flow", "_resolve", "_pick_variant", "_run_actions",
    "_exec_step", "_dispatch_step", "_dispatch_probe", "_run_kind",
    "_dispatch",
    "_step_any_of", "_step_random", "_step_wait", "_step_think",
    "_step_click_text", "_step_click_id", "_step_click_desc",
    "_step_click_point", "_step_click_first",
    "_step_swipe", "_step_swipe_natural", "_step_captcha",
    "_step_scroll_to", "_step_back", "_step_home",
    "_step_reward", "_reward_candidates", "_step_ensure",
]

# 各部件的专属方法（MRO 归属抽查：门面多继承后仍应可经 Executor 调用）
SESSION_METHODS = ["connect", "_at_top_level", "_reset_to_landing",
                   "_open_app", "_close_app", "_wait_page_ready", "_dump_safe"]
LOCATOR_METHODS = ["_locate", "_pick_best", "_click_obj", "_kb_candidates",
                   "_kb_mark_fail", "_locate_with_kb", "_click_tracked",
                   "_vision_or_miss", "_vision_click"]
RUNNER_METHODS = ["run_app_flow", "_resolve", "_pick_variant", "_run_actions",
                  "_exec_step", "_dispatch_step", "_dispatch_probe",
                  "_run_kind", "_dispatch", "_step_any_of", "_step_random",
                  "_step_wait", "_step_think", "_step_click_text",
                  "_step_click_id", "_step_click_desc", "_step_click_point",
                  "_step_click_first", "_step_swipe", "_step_swipe_natural",
                  "_step_captcha", "_step_scroll_to", "_step_back",
                  "_step_home", "_step_reward", "_reward_candidates",
                  "_step_ensure"]


def t_facade_has_all_methods():
    """门面 Executor 对 A/B 终态方法清单逐一 hasattr + callable。"""
    ex = executor.Executor.__new__(executor.Executor)
    missing = [m for m in FACADE_METHODS if not hasattr(ex, m)]
    assert not missing, f"门面缺失方法: {missing}"
    not_callable = [m for m in FACADE_METHODS
                    if hasattr(ex, m) and not callable(getattr(ex, m))]
    assert not not_callable, f"门面方法不可调用: {not_callable}"
    print(f"OK  门面方法齐全且可调用（{len(FACADE_METHODS)} 个）")


def t_components_exist():
    """三个新部件类存在且方法可调用（防职责漂移）。"""
    for cls, methods in ((DeviceSession, SESSION_METHODS),
                         (Locator, LOCATOR_METHODS),
                         (BehaviorRunner, RUNNER_METHODS)):
        inst = cls.__new__(cls)
        missing = [m for m in methods if not callable(getattr(inst, m, None))]
        assert not missing, f"{cls.__name__} 缺失方法: {missing}"
    print("OK  三个部件类方法齐全")


def t_re_exports():
    """executor 门面 re-export：StepError / LocateResult / _parse_sel。"""
    assert executor.StepError is StepError, "StepError 未 re-export"
    assert executor.LocateResult is LocateResult, "LocateResult 未 re-export"
    assert executor._parse_sel is _parse_sel, "_parse_sel 未 re-export"
    assert _parse_sel("text=签到") == ("text", "签到")
    assert _parse_sel("签到") == ("text", "签到")
    print("OK  re-export 齐备（StepError/LocateResult/_parse_sel）")


def t_top_level_labels_global_available():
    """executor 模块级 TOP_LEVEL_LABELS 保留（test_executor_reset 打桩依赖）。"""
    assert hasattr(executor, "TOP_LEVEL_LABELS")
    print("OK  executor.TOP_LEVEL_LABELS 保留")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"门面等价性测试: {len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_human_seed.py`（72 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_human_seed.py - A5 human 随机配置化（seed 可复现、reseed、lognormal 配置）

运行：
    python -m pytest tests/test_human_seed.py -q
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from human import Human  # noqa: E402


def _human(cfg=None, seed=None):
    return Human({"human": cfg or {}, "trajectory": None}, seed=seed)


def _seq(h, n=50):
    return [h._rand(0.5, 2.5, lognormal=True) for _ in range(n)]


def test_same_seed_same_sequence():
    """同 seed 的两个 Human 产生完全一致的随机序列。"""
    a, b = _human(seed=20261019), _human(seed=20261019)
    sa, sb = _seq(a), _seq(b)
    assert sa == sb, "同 seed 序列必须一致"
    print("OK  同 seed 序列一致")


def test_diff_seed_diff_sequence():
    """异 seed 的序列不同（随机源隔离）。"""
    a, b = _human(seed=1), _human(seed=2)
    sa, sb = _seq(a), _seq(b)
    assert sa != sb, "异 seed 序列不应相同"
    print("OK  异 seed 序列不同")


def test_reseed_resets_sequence():
    """reseed 后用同 seed 重现与初始构造一致的序列。"""
    h = _human(seed=777)
    first = _seq(h)
    _ = _seq(h)                       # 推进若干步，丢掉原状态
    h.reseed(777)
    again = _seq(h)
    assert again == first, "reseed 同 seed 应复现初始序列"
    print("OK  reseed 重置后复现初始序列")


def test_lognormal_config_effective():
    """lognormal 配置项生效：mu_ratio / sigma_range 被实例读取。"""
    h = _human({"lognormal": {"mu_ratio": 0.30,
                              "sigma_range": [0.20, 0.50]}})
    assert abs(h.mu_ratio - 0.30) < 1e-9
    assert abs(h.sigma_lo - 0.20) < 1e-9
    assert abs(h.sigma_hi - 0.50) < 1e-9
    # 自定义参数下采样仍 clamp 在区间内
    lo, hi = 0.5, 2.5
    for _ in range(300):
        v = h._rand(lo, hi, lognormal=True)
        assert lo <= v <= hi, v
    print("OK  lognormal 配置生效且采样仍 clamp 在区间内")


def test_default_lognormal_equiv():
    """默认参数与旧硬编码等价：mu_ratio=0.18、sigma_range=[0.45,0.75]。"""
    h = _human()
    assert abs(h.mu_ratio - 0.18) < 1e-9
    assert abs(h.sigma_lo - 0.45) < 1e-9
    assert abs(h.sigma_hi - 0.75) < 1e-9
    print("OK  默认 lognormal 参数与旧硬编码等价")
```

### `tests/test_landing.py`（145 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_landing.py - 落地校验多信号投票（2026-10-23）

不依赖设备：用 PIL 合成图像验证 dHash / 模板匹配 / 信号投票逻辑。

运行：
    ./.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest tests/test_landing.py -q
"""
import os
import sys
import tempfile

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw  # noqa: E402

import landing  # noqa: E402


def _img(color=(240, 240, 240), size=(240, 480), box=None):
    im = Image.new("RGB", size, color)
    if box:
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
        d.rectangle(box, fill=(200, 30, 30))
    return im


def test_dhash_identical_and_different():
    a = _img()
    b = _img()
    assert landing.hamming(landing.dhash(a), landing.dhash(b)) == 0
    c = _img(box=(20, 20, 120, 120))
    assert landing.hamming(landing.dhash(a), landing.dhash(c)) > 5
    print("OK  dHash：同图距离 0，异图距离显著")


def test_template_score_hits_crop():
    def _striped(size=(240, 480)):
        im = Image.new("RGB", size, (240, 240, 240))
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
        for i in range(150, 220, 5):        # 带纹理，方差非零
            d.line((i, 60, i, 130), fill=(200, 30, 30))
        return im

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        screen = _striped()
        tpl_path = os.path.join(tmp, "tpl.png")
        screen.crop((150, 60, 220, 130)).save(tpl_path)
        score = landing.template_score(screen, tpl_path)
        assert score is not None and score > 0.95, score
        # 均匀模板无判别力：TM_CCOEFF_NORMED 会退化成 1.0，必须判「不适用」
        blank = os.path.join(tmp, "blank.png")
        _img(color=(255, 255, 255), size=(40, 40)).save(blank)
        assert landing.template_score(screen, blank) is None, \
            "近均匀模板应判不适用，否则假阳性会把校验变成摆设"
        # 有纹理但不匹配的模板：得分必须低
        noise = os.path.join(tmp, "noise.png")
        n = _img(color=(10, 10, 10), size=(70, 70))
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(n)
        for i in range(0, 70, 7):
            d.line((i, 0, i, 70), fill=(250, 250, 250))
        n.save(noise)
        assert landing.template_score(screen, noise) < 0.9
    print("OK  模板匹配：带纹理裁切命中 >0.95；均匀模板判不适用；异纹理不误命中")


def test_check_text_any():
    xml = '<h><node text="我的金币"/><node text="签到"/></h>'
    r = landing.check_landing(_img(), {"text_any": ["金币", "签到"]}, xml=xml)
    assert r["ok"] is True, r
    r = landing.check_landing(_img(), {"text_any": ["提现"]}, xml=xml)
    assert r["ok"] is False and "text_nodes" in r["reason"], r
    print("OK  文案信号：命中通过 / 未命中失败")


def test_check_text_forbidden():
    xml = '<h><node text="确认提现"/></h>'
    r = landing.check_landing(_img(), {"text_none": ["确认提现"]}, xml=xml)
    assert r["ok"] is False, r
    print("OK  禁用词信号：出现即失败（防误入提现页）")


def test_check_structure_nodes_and_pkg():
    xml = "<h>" + "<node/>" * 30 + "</h>"
    r = landing.check_landing(_img(), {"min_nodes": 20}, xml=xml)
    assert r["ok"] is True, r
    r = landing.check_landing(_img(), {"min_nodes": 50}, xml=xml)
    assert r["ok"] is False, r
    r = landing.check_landing(_img(), {"pkg": "com.x"}, pkg="com.x")
    assert r["ok"] is True
    r = landing.check_landing(_img(), {"pkg": "com.x"}, pkg="com.y")
    assert r["ok"] is False
    print("OK  结构信号：节点数区间 / 包名")


def test_check_no_signals_returns_unknown():
    r = landing.check_landing(_img(), {})
    assert r["ok"] is None, "无信号时应返回 None（不阻断）"
    print("OK  无可用信号 → ok=None（不阻断，只记日志）")


def test_check_phash_ref():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        ref = os.path.join(tmp, "ref.png")
        _img(box=(30, 300, 200, 420)).save(ref)
        same = _img(box=(30, 300, 200, 420))
        other = _img(box=(0, 0, 100, 100))
        assert landing.check_landing(same, {"phash": ref})["ok"] is True
        assert landing.check_landing(other, {"phash": ref})["ok"] is False
    print("OK  phash：与参考截图一致通过，不一致失败")


def test_min_pass_allows_partial():
    xml = '<h><node text="别的页面"/></h>'
    expect = {"text_any": ["金币"], "min_nodes": 1, "min_pass": 1}
    r = landing.check_landing(_img(), expect, xml=xml)
    assert r["ok"] is True, r        # text 失败但结构通过，min_pass=1 放行
    r2 = landing.check_landing(_img(), {"text_any": ["金币"], "min_nodes": 1}, xml=xml)
    assert r2["ok"] is False, "默认要求全部信号通过"
    print("OK  min_pass：允许部分信号通过（默认全过）")


def test_describe_is_one_line():
    r = landing.check_landing(_img(), {"text_any": ["金币"], "min_nodes": 1},
                              xml='<h><node text="金币"/></h>')
    s = landing.describe(r["signals"])
    assert "text_nodes=ok" in s and "min_nodes=ok" in s and "\n" not in s
    print("OK  describe 输出单行日志")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    fns = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("test_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in fns:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(fns) - failed}/{len(fns)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_locate_pick.py`（139 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_locate_pick.py - Executor._pick_best 单元测试

覆盖两个真实踩坑场景（无需真机）：
  1. textContains 命中多处时，应择优（优先完全相等 / 更短 / 可点击）；
  2. 只命中"长文案里恰好包含关键词"的不可点击节点时，应判为误命中返回 None
     —— 否则 executor 会点错（实测一淘"我的"页里候选 `签到` 唯一命中的是
     FAQ 链接「什么是签到领钱，如何参加？」）。

运行：
    python -m pytest tests/test_locate_pick.py -q
    或直接 python tests/test_locate_pick.py
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from executor import Executor  # noqa: E402


class _Info(dict):
    pass


class _Obj:
    """模拟 UiObject 单元素"""

    def __init__(self, info):
        self.info = _Info(info)


class _Objs:
    """模拟 UiObject 多匹配（支持 .count 与 [i]）"""

    def __init__(self, infos):
        self._infos = [_Info(i) for i in infos]

    @property
    def count(self):
        return len(self._infos)

    def __getitem__(self, i):
        return _Obj(self._infos[i])

    @property
    def info(self):
        return self._infos[0]


class _Rec:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = []

    def log(self, event, app="", level="INFO"):
        self.lines.append(f"[{level}] {event}")


def _mk():
    ex = Executor.__new__(Executor)
    ex.recorder = _Rec()
    return ex


def t_pick_shortest_clickable():
    ex = _mk()
    obj = _Objs([
        {"text": "约返0.13元", "clickable": False},
        {"text": "元", "clickable": True},
        {"text": "元", "clickable": False},
    ])
    best = ex._pick_best(obj, "元", "text")
    assert best.info["text"] == "元" and best.info["clickable"] is True, best.info
    print("OK  多匹配择优：选中可点击的短文本")


def t_exact_beats_clickable():
    ex = _mk()
    obj = _Objs([
        {"text": "每日签到", "clickable": True},
        {"text": "签到", "clickable": False},
    ])
    best = ex._pick_best(obj, "签到", "text")
    assert best.info["text"] == "签到", best.info
    print("OK  完全相等优先于可点击加分")


def t_reject_long_text():
    """FAQ 问句是超长唯一候选 -> 仍判误命中（打分改造后此行为必须保持）。

    2026-10-28：文案从「过长文案」改为「噪声文案」——旧版按长度阈值拒，
    新版按句式甄别拒；判定结果不变，日志措辞更新。
    """
    ex = _mk()
    obj = _Objs([{"text": "什么是签到领钱，如何参加？", "clickable": False}])
    best = ex._pick_best(obj, "签到", "text")
    assert best is None, f"应判为误命中，实际返回 {best.info if best else None}"
    assert any("噪声" in l for l in ex.recorder.lines), ex.recorder.lines
    print("OK  长文案误命中被拒配（返回 None）")


def t_accept_moderate_text():
    ex = _mk()
    obj = _Objs([{"text": "每日签到领金币", "clickable": False}])
    best = ex._pick_best(obj, "签到", "text")
    assert best is not None, "长度在阈值内的文本应被接受"
    print("OK  阈值内的较长文本仍接受")


def t_long_text_but_clickable_ok():
    ex = _mk()
    obj = _Objs([{"text": "看视频领金币任务中心入口", "clickable": True}])
    best = ex._pick_best(obj, "看视频", "text")
    assert best is not None, "可点击节点不因文案长而被拒"
    print("OK  可点击节点不受长文案拒配影响")


def t_single_match_short():
    ex = _mk()
    obj = _Objs([{"text": "红包签到", "clickable": False}])
    best = ex._pick_best(obj, "签到", "text")
    assert best is not None, "短文本单匹配应接受"
    print("OK  单匹配短文本接受（红包签到）")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [t_pick_shortest_clickable, t_exact_beats_clickable,
             t_reject_long_text, t_accept_moderate_text,
             t_long_text_but_clickable_ok, t_single_match_short]
    failed = 0
    for t in tests:
        try:
            t()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {t.__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_locator_sharing.py`（111 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_locator_sharing.py - A3 定位链共享件单元测试（无需真机）

覆盖：
  1. `_locate_with_kb` 的 mark_fail 触发条件：
     - has_entry=True 且 attempted=True 且定位失败 -> 计数；
     - 知识库已 disabled（cands 空，attempted=False）-> 不计数；
     - 无知识库记录（has_entry=False）-> 不计数；
  2. `_vision_or_miss` 的 desc 串透传与 allow_vision 开关。

运行：
    python -m pytest tests/test_locator_sharing.py -q
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from executor import Executor, LocateResult  # noqa: E402


class _Rec:
    def log(self, event, app="", level="INFO"):
        pass


class _KB:
    """记录 mark_fail / mark_ok 调用。"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.fails = []
        self.oks = []

    def mark_fail(self, app, sel):
        self.fails.append((app, sel))

    def mark_ok(self, app, sel):
        self.oks.append((app, sel))


def _mk(kb_cands, locate_result):
    ex = Executor.__new__(Executor)
    ex.recorder = _Rec()
    ex.dry_run = True
    ex.knowledge = _KB()
    if locate_result is None:
        ex._locate = lambda k, v, timeout=6.0: LocateResult(False, None, "测试未命中")
    else:
        ex._locate = lambda k, v, timeout=6.0: LocateResult(True, locate_result, "")
    ex._kb_candidates = lambda app, primary: kb_cands
    return ex


def _kb(has_entry, cands):
    return (cands, has_entry)


def test_mark_fail_trigger_conditions():
    """mark_fail 仅当 has_entry and cands（实际尝试过才计数）。"""
    # a) 有记录且启用、定位失败 -> mark_fail
    ex = _mk(_kb(True, ["text=签到", "text=去签到"]), None)
    obj, has_entry, attempted = ex._locate_with_kb("T", "text=签到", 2.0)
    assert obj is None and has_entry and attempted
    assert ("T", "text=签到") in ex.knowledge.fails, ex.knowledge.fails
    print("OK  has_entry+attempted+失败 -> mark_fail")

    # b) 记录已 disabled（cands 空）-> 不计数
    ex = _mk(_kb(True, []), None)
    obj, has_entry, attempted = ex._locate_with_kb("T", "text=签到", 2.0)
    assert obj is None and has_entry and not attempted
    assert ex.knowledge.fails == [], ex.knowledge.fails
    print("OK  disabled（attempted=False）-> 不 mark_fail")

    # c) 无知识库记录 -> 不计数
    ex = _mk(_kb(False, ["text=签到"]), None)
    obj, has_entry, attempted = ex._locate_with_kb("T", "text=签到", 2.0)
    assert obj is None and not has_entry
    assert ex.knowledge.fails == [], ex.knowledge.fails
    print("OK  无记录（has_entry=False）-> 不 mark_fail")

    # d) 命中时即使 has_entry 也不 mark_fail，而是 mark_ok
    ex = _mk(_kb(True, ["text=签到"]), object())
    obj, has_entry, attempted = ex._locate_with_kb("T", "text=签到", 2.0)
    assert obj is not None
    assert ex.knowledge.fails == []
    print("OK  命中分支不 mark_fail")


def test_vision_or_miss_desc_passthrough():
    """desc 串原样透传给 _vision_click；allow_vision=False 时完全不调用。"""
    seen = []

    def fake_vision(act, app, desc):
        seen.append((app, desc))
        return True

    ex = Executor.__new__(Executor)
    ex._vision_click = fake_vision
    act = {"type": "click_desc", "desc": "我的"}
    ok = ex._vision_or_miss(act, "T", "click_desc[我的]", allow_vision=True)
    assert ok is True
    assert seen == [("T", "click_desc[我的]")], seen
    print("OK  desc 串 click_desc[我的] 原样透传")

    seen.clear()
    ex = Executor.__new__(Executor)
    ex._vision_click = fake_vision
    ok = ex._vision_or_miss(act, "T", "click_first[all-candidates]",
                            allow_vision=False)
    assert ok is False and seen == [], seen
    print("OK  allow_vision=False 时跳过视觉兜底")
```

### `tests/test_miniyaml_nested_seq.py`（146 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_miniyaml_nested_seq.py - 嵌套块序列解析回归（`- - key: v`）

2026-10-20 真机回归 0/12 的根因之一：miniyaml 的 _parse_seq 不处理
「序列项本身是序列」的写法（`choices:` 下每个分支 `- - type: click_first`），
把整段 any_of 解析成 {"- type": "click_first", ...} —— _run_kind 看到
type=None，any_of 静默全灭。webui 契约测试同时抓到 vias 为空。

PyYAML 语义：外层 '-' 占一项，内层 '-' 开出子序列。
    choices:
    - - type: click_first        # 分支1 = [ {type: click_first, ...} ]
        timeout: 3
    - - type: click_point        # 分支2 = [ {type: click_point, ...} ]
        x: 0.9

运行：
    python tests/test_miniyaml_nested_seq.py
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

import miniyaml  # noqa: E402


def t_choices_nested_seq():
    """any_of choices 的双层序列：每个分支是 list，动作键名干净。"""
    text = """behaviors:
  sign:
  - type: any_of
    choices:
    - - type: click_first
        candidates:
        - type: text
          value: 签到
        timeout: 3
    - - type: click_point
        x: 0.9
        y: 0.915
        optional: true
"""
    d = miniyaml.safe_load(text)
    act = d["behaviors"]["sign"][0]
    assert act["type"] == "any_of", act
    ch = act["choices"]
    assert isinstance(ch, list) and len(ch) == 2, ch
    # 分支1
    assert isinstance(ch[0], list) and ch[0][0]["type"] == "click_first", ch[0]
    assert ch[0][0]["timeout"] == 3
    assert ch[0][0]["candidates"] == [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"}]
    # 分支2
    assert ch[1][0]["type"] == "click_point", ch[1]
    assert ch[1][0]["x"] == 0.9 and ch[1][0]["optional"] is True
    # 关键回归点：不允许出现 "- type" 这种拼错键
    flat = repr(d)
    assert '"- type"' not in flat and "'- type'" not in flat, flat
    print("t_choices_nested_seq OK")


def t_inner_multi_item_seq():
    """内层序列多个项：`- - a` 后跟 `  - b` 同在分支里。"""
    text = """choices:
- - type: click_first
    candidates:
    - type: text
      value: 我的
  - type: think
"""
    d = miniyaml.safe_load(text)
    ch = d["choices"]
    assert len(ch) == 1, ch
    branch = ch[0]
    assert [s["type"] for s in branch] == ["click_first", "think"], branch
    assert branch[0]["candidates"][0]["value"] == "我的"
    print("t_inner_multi_item_seq OK")


def t_triple_nested():
    """三层嵌套 `- - - x`：逐层脱壳成 [[[x]]]。"""
    text = """a:
- - - scalar
"""
    d = miniyaml.safe_load(text)
    assert d["a"] == [[["scalar"]]], d
    print("t_triple_nested OK")


def t_flat_seq_unaffected():
    """普通单层序列不受影响。"""
    text = """cands:
- type: text
  value: A
- type: desc
  value: B
"""
    d = miniyaml.safe_load(text)
    assert d["cands"] == [
        {"type": "text", "value": "A"},
        {"type": "desc", "value": "B"},
    ], d
    print("t_flat_seq_unaffected OK")


def t_flow_style_inner():
    """内层流式风格 `- - {type: x}`：按标量容器解析。"""
    text = """choices:
- - {type: click_point, x: 0.5}
- - {type: think}
"""
    d = miniyaml.safe_load(text)
    assert d["choices"] == [
        [{"type": "click_point", "x": 0.5}],
        [{"type": "think"}],
    ], d
    print("t_flow_style_inner OK")


def t_roundtrip_real_config():
    """真配置趣头条 sign：safe_dump -> safe_load 语义等价。"""
    path = os.path.join(ROOT, "config", "apps", "01_趣头条.yaml")
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        orig = miniyaml.safe_load(f.read())
    dumped = miniyaml.safe_dump(orig)
    back = miniyaml.safe_load(dumped)
    assert back == orig, "round-trip 后语义漂移"
    # 显式核对 any_of 分支结构
    sign = back["behaviors"]["sign"]
    anyof = [a for a in sign if a.get("type") == "any_of"]
    assert anyof, "sign 应含 any_of"
    for a in anyof:
        for branch in a["choices"]:
            assert isinstance(branch, list) and branch, branch
            assert all(isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("type") for s in branch), branch
    print("t_roundtrip_real_config OK")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    t_choices_nested_seq()
    t_inner_multi_item_seq()
    t_triple_nested()
    t_flat_seq_unaffected()
    t_flow_style_inner()
    t_roundtrip_real_config()
    print("ALL miniyaml nested-seq TESTS PASSED")
```

### `tests/test_nav_band.py`（114 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_nav_band.py - 底部导航标签的位置合理性校验（无需真机）

背景（踩过的坑）：
    「我的」「我」这类底部导航标签，在页面**中部**也会出现（例如某个卡片
    里写着「我的收益」之外的独立「我的」标签）。实测蛋花免费小说二级页
    命中过 text='我的' @(575,627) —— 1080x2340 的屏幕上 y=627 是页面中部，
    不可能是底部导航栏。若不区分，会：
      1. 度量虚高：把「找到页面中部一个『我的』标签」当成「找到了导航入口」；
      2. 执行出错：执行器可能点到页面中部的那个标签。

新语义：
    * 目标词是底部导航标签时，命中必须落在屏幕底部带（y >= 0.80 * 屏高）
      才算数；否则判为「疑似假命中」。
    * 非导航标签（如「签到」）不受影响。

运行：
    python tests/test_nav_band.py
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(ROOT, "devtools"))

from verify_live import check_anchor, _nav_ok, NAV_BAND_Y  # noqa: E402

H = 2340


def _node(text, cx, cy, clickable=True, desc=""):
    return {"text": text, "desc": desc, "rid": "", "center": (cx, cy),
            "size": (100, 100), "clickable": clickable,
            "bounds": (cx - 50, cy - 50, cx + 50, cy + 50)}


def t_nav_label_in_middle_is_rejected():
    """y=627 的「我的」不是底部导航 -> 假命中。"""
    nodes = [_node("我的", 575, 627)]
    hit, det = check_anchor(nodes, {"type": "click_text", "text": "我的"}, H)
    assert not hit, f"应判假命中，实际 hit={hit} det={det}"
    assert "不在底部导航带" in det, det
    print("OK  页面中部的『我的』被判为疑似假命中")


def t_nav_label_in_bottom_band_accepted():
    nodes = [_node("我的", 918, 2130)]
    hit, det = check_anchor(nodes, {"type": "click_text", "text": "我的"}, H)
    assert hit, det
    print("OK  底部带的『我的』命中")


def t_boundary_at_band_edge():
    """正好在 0.80 * H 上算命中（>=）。"""
    y = int(NAV_BAND_Y * H)
    assert _nav_ok(_node("我的", 500, y), "我的", H)
    assert not _nav_ok(_node("我的", 500, y - 1), "我的", H)
    print("OK  底部带边界按 >= 0.80*屏高 判定")


def t_non_nav_label_unaffected():
    """非导航标签不做位置限制（签到按钮可能在任何位置）。"""
    nodes = [_node("签到", 720, 2093), _node("每日签到", 300, 500)]
    hit, det = check_anchor(nodes, {"type": "click_text", "text": "签到"}, H)
    assert hit, det
    hit2, det2 = check_anchor(nodes, {"type": "click_text", "text": "每日签到"}, H)
    assert hit2, det2
    print("OK  非导航标签不受底部带限制")


def t_click_first_falls_through_to_bottom_match():
    """click_first：中部命中被否，底部命中被接受。"""
    nodes = [_node("我的", 575, 627), _node("我的", 918, 2130)]
    hit, det = check_anchor(
        nodes,
        {"type": "click_first", "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "我的"}]},
        H)
    assert hit, det
    assert "2130" in det, det
    print("OK  click_first 跳过中部假命中、取底部真命中")


def t_no_screen_h_skips_check():
    """未提供屏高时保持旧行为（不做位置校验）。"""
    nodes = [_node("我的", 575, 627)]
    hit, det = check_anchor(nodes, {"type": "click_text", "text": "我的"}, None)
    assert hit, det
    print("OK  未提供屏高时不做位置校验（向后兼容）")


def t_any_of_inherits_nav_band():
    nodes = [_node("我的", 575, 627)]
    act = {"type": "any_of", "optional": True,
           "choices": [[{"type": "click_first",
                         "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "我的"}]}]]}
    hit, det = check_anchor(nodes, act, H)
    assert not hit, f"any_of 应继承位置校验: {det}"
    print("OK  any_of 分支继承底部带校验")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_nav_vision.py`（136 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_nav_vision.py - 导航前置步跳过视觉兜底（无需真机）

背景：
    `click_point` 带 `vlm_prompt` 时执行器会**先走视觉兜底**（截屏 + VLM 识别）。
    本机 VLM 图像请求常 90s 超时或返回 `User Safety: safe`，而导航「我的」是
    **每个行为都要执行**的固定步骤 —— 让它走视觉既慢又不稳。

    `vision: false` 让该步跳过视觉，直接用比例坐标（已在 18 个 App 上验证）。

运行：
    python tests/test_nav_vision.py
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from executor import Executor  # noqa: E402


class _Rec:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = []

    def log(self, event, app="", level="INFO"):
        self.lines.append(f"[{level}] {event}")


class _Human:
    def jitter_point(self, x, y):
        return x, y

    def tap(self, d, x, y):
        # F2 统一点击入口；测试里直通 d.click，保持 clicked 断言口径
        self.tapped = getattr(self, "tapped", [])
        self.tapped.append((x, y))
        d.click(x, y)


class _Size:
    """uiautomator2 的 window_size() 返回带 width/height 的对象，不是元组。"""

    def __init__(self, w, h):
        self.width = w
        self.height = h


class _D:
    def __init__(self):
        self.clicked = []

    def window_size(self):
        return _Size(1080, 2340)

    def click(self, x, y):
        self.clicked.append((x, y))


class _Vision:
    def __init__(self):
        self.calls = 0

    def screenshot_from_device(self, d):
        self.calls += 1
        return "img"

    def locate_on_screen(self, **kw):
        self.calls += 1
        return None          # 模拟 VLM 未命中/超时


def _mk():
    ex = Executor.__new__(Executor)
    ex.recorder = _Rec()
    ex.human = _Human()
    ex.d = _D()
    ex.vision = _Vision()
    ex.vision_enabled = True
    ex.dry_run = False
    return ex


NAV = {"type": "click_point",
       "vlm_prompt": "定位屏幕底部导航栏最右侧的『我的』标签中心",
       "x": 0.9, "y": 0.915, "optional": True}


def t_vision_false_skips_vision():
    ex = _mk()
    act = dict(NAV, vision=False)
    ex._step_click_point(act, "测试")
    assert ex.vision.calls == 0, f"不应调用视觉，实际 {ex.vision.calls} 次"
    assert ex.d.clicked == [(972, 2141)], f"应点比例坐标，实际 {ex.d.clicked}"
    print("OK  vision=false 时跳过视觉，直接点比例坐标")


def t_vision_default_still_tries_vision():
    """未显式关闭时保持旧行为：先试视觉兜底。"""
    ex = _mk()
    ex._step_click_point(dict(NAV), "测试")
    assert ex.vision.calls > 0, "默认应尝试视觉兜底"
    assert ex.d.clicked, "视觉未命中后应回退到比例坐标"
    print("OK  未设 vision 时仍先试视觉兜底，失败后回退比例坐标")


def t_vision_true_explicit():
    ex = _mk()
    ex._step_click_point(dict(NAV, vision=True), "测试")
    assert ex.vision.calls > 0
    print("OK  vision=true 显式开启视觉兜底")


def t_plain_point_without_prompt_never_uses_vision():
    ex = _mk()
    ex._step_click_point({"type": "click_point", "x": 0.5, "y": 0.5}, "测试")
    assert ex.vision.calls == 0
    assert ex.d.clicked == [(540, 1170)]
    print("OK  无视觉绑定的 click_point 不走视觉")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_nl_to_steps.py`（281 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_nl_to_steps.py - 自然语言→动作链 YAML（AUTOP-NL-2026-10-22-A 第1层）

锁定契约（全部纯函数，不碰文件不碰设备）：
  * parse_line/parse_script：动词识别（长词优先）、位置词、中文/数字时长、
    多句拆分、unknown 不抛异常；
  * guess_entry 三分支；
  * render_chain 结构：每 hop 独立 any_of；文案支 click_first(optional=False)
    与坐标支 click_point(vision=False) 分属两个 choice；tab 跳显式
    candidates_from=nav_tab + zone=tab_zone；
  * --h5：坐标支 1 点 -> wait1.5 + 第二点 y 偏移 offset/2340；
  * 缺 *_xy：只出文案支 + warning「缺坐标兜底，建议真机校准后补」，绝不猜坐标；
  * 词表门禁：源码无中文词表字面量 + candidates.candidates_for 是唯一词表来源；
  * 幂等：同输入两次 build_behavior 深相等；
  * round-trip：yaml.safe_load(to_yaml_fragment(actions)) == actions
    （含全角冒号、+、%）。

运行：./.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest tests/test_nl_to_steps.py -q
"""
import inspect
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

import yaml  # noqa: E402

import devtools.nl_to_steps as n  # noqa: E402


# ---------- parse_line / parse_script ----------

def test_parse_line_tap_with_pos():
    steps, w = n.parse_line("点右下角我的")
    assert w == []
    assert len(steps) == 1
    st = steps[0]
    assert st["verb"] == "tap"
    assert st["target"] == "我的"
    assert st["pos"] == (0.90, 0.914)


def test_parse_line_tap_quoted():
    steps, _ = n.parse_line("点击「立即签到」")
    st = steps[0]
    assert st["verb"] == "tap"
    assert st["target"] == "立即签到"
    assert st["pos"] is None


def test_parse_line_wait_digits():
    steps, _ = n.parse_line("等3秒")
    st = steps[0]
    assert st["verb"] == "wait"
    assert st["seconds"] == 3.0
    assert st["target"] is None


def test_parse_line_wait_cn_and_half_minute():
    st, _ = n.parse_line("等三秒")
    assert st[0]["verb"] == "wait" and st[0]["seconds"] == 3.0
    st, _ = n.parse_line("等半分钟")
    assert st[0]["verb"] == "wait" and st[0]["seconds"] == 30.0
    assert n._cn_number("五秒") == 5.0
    assert n._cn_number("十秒") == 10.0
    assert n._cn_number("看不懂") is None


def test_parse_line_swipe_twice():
    steps, _ = n.parse_line("上滑两次")
    st = steps[0]
    assert st["verb"] == "swipe"
    assert st.get("count") == 2
    assert st["pos"] is None


def test_parse_line_swipe_down():
    st, _ = n.parse_line("下滑")
    assert st[0]["verb"] == "swipe"


def test_parse_script_multi():
    steps, w = n.parse_script("点我的，然后点福利中心，最后点签到")
    assert w == []
    assert len(steps) == 3
    assert [s["target"] for s in steps] == ["我的", "福利中心", "签到"]
    assert all(s["verb"] == "tap" for s in steps)


def test_parse_line_unknown_no_raise():
    steps, w = n.parse_line("随便什么没有动词")
    assert len(steps) == 1
    assert steps[0]["verb"] == "unknown"
    assert len(w) == 1 and "无法识别动词" in w[0]


def test_parse_line_back():
    st, _ = n.parse_line("返回")
    assert st[0]["verb"] == "back"


# ---------- guess_entry / templates ----------

def test_guess_entry_personal_center():
    assert n.guess_entry("点我的，点福利中心") == "personal_center"
    assert n.guess_entry("进入个人中心") == "personal_center"


def test_guess_entry_feed_ball():
    assert n.guess_entry("点右上角金币") == "feed_ball"
    assert n.guess_entry("点浮球") == "feed_ball"


def test_guess_entry_home_direct():
    assert n.guess_entry("点签到") == "home_direct"
    assert n.guess_entry("") == "home_direct"


def test_template_names():
    assert n.template_names() == ["personal_center", "feed_ball", "home_direct"]


# ---------- render_chain 结构 ----------

def test_render_chain_personal_structure():
    params = {"tab_xy": (0.9, 0.914), "hub_xy": (0.3, 0.5), "sign_xy": (0.5, 0.641)}
    actions, w = n.render_chain("personal_center", params, "sign")
    assert w == []
    hops = [a for a in actions if a.get("type") == "any_of"]
    assert len(hops) == 3  # tab / hub / sign

    tab, hub, sign = hops
    # tab：文案支 click_first（optional False、candidates_from nav_tab、zone tab_zone）
    assert tab["choices"][0][0]["type"] == "click_first"
    assert tab["choices"][0][0]["optional"] is False
    assert tab["choices"][0][0]["candidates_from"] == "nav_tab"
    assert tab["choices"][0][0]["zone"] == "tab_zone"
    # tab：坐标支 click_point（vision False），与文案支分属两个 choice
    assert tab["choices"][1][0]["type"] == "click_point"
    assert tab["choices"][1][0]["vision"] is False
    # hub：candidates_from task_center、无 zone
    assert hub["choices"][0][0]["candidates_from"] == "task_center"
    assert "zone" not in hub["choices"][0][0]
    # sign：candidates_from sign
    assert sign["choices"][0][0]["candidates_from"] == "sign"
    # 坐标支不复制后续步骤
    for hop in hops:
        assert len(hop["choices"][1]) == 1


def test_render_chain_h5_offset():
    params = {"sign_xy": (0.5, 0.641)}
    actions, _ = n.render_chain("home_direct", params, "sign")
    sign = actions[-1]
    assert len(sign["choices"][1]) == 1  # 默认单点
    assert sign["choices"][1][0]["type"] == "click_point"

    params2 = dict(params)
    params2["h5"] = True
    params2["h5_offset"] = 60.0
    actions2, _ = n.render_chain("home_direct", params2, "sign")
    sign2 = actions2[-1]
    pts = [a for a in sign2["choices"][1] if a["type"] == "click_point"]
    assert len(pts) == 2  # h5：2 个 click_point
    assert pts[1]["y"] == round(0.641 + 60.0 / 2340.0, 6)
    assert sign2["choices"][1][1]["type"] == "wait"
    assert sign2["choices"][1][1]["seconds"] == 1.5


def test_render_chain_missing_xy_warning():
    actions, w = n.render_chain("personal_center", {}, "sign")
    assert len(w) == 3
    assert all("缺坐标兜底，建议真机校准后补" in x for x in w)
    for a in actions:
        assert a["type"] == "any_of"
        assert len(a["choices"]) == 1  # 只出文案支，绝不猜坐标


def test_render_chain_scroll_confirm():
    params = {"sign_xy": (0.5, 0.641), "scroll_text": "签到", "confirm_text": "确认领取"}
    actions, _ = n.render_chain("personal_center", params, "sign")
    types = [a["type"] for a in actions]
    assert types == ["any_of", "any_of", "scroll_to", "any_of", "click_first"]
    assert actions[2]["text"] == "签到"
    assert actions[-1]["type"] == "click_first"
    assert actions[-1]["candidates"][0]["value"] == "确认领取"
    assert actions[-1]["optional"] is True


# ---------- 词表门禁 ----------

def test_vocab_gate_source():
    src = inspect.getsource(n)
    for banned in ("今日签到", "立即签到", "任务中心"):
        assert banned not in src


def test_vocab_gate_candidates_single_source(monkeypatch):
    calls = []

    def fake_candidates_for(category, app_cfg=None):
        calls.append(category)
        return [f"w_{category}_x"]

    monkeypatch.setattr(n.candidates, "candidates_for", fake_candidates_for)
    r = n.build_behavior("点我的，点福利中心，点签到", "A", "sign",
                         params={"tab_xy": "0.9,0.914",
                                 "hub_xy": "0.3,0.5",
                                 "sign_xy": "0.5,0.641"})
    assert "nav_tab" in calls and "task_center" in calls and "sign" in calls
    assert r["candidates_used"]["sign"] == ["w_sign_x"]
    # 动作链里只有 candidates_from，不含字面候选词
    for a in r["actions"]:
        for choice in a.get("choices", []):
            assert "candidates" not in choice[0]


# ---------- 幂等 / round-trip ----------

def test_build_behavior_idempotent():
    kw = dict(params={"tab_xy": "0.9,0.914", "hub_xy": "0.3,0.5",
                      "sign_xy": "0.5,0.641"})
    r1 = n.build_behavior("点我的，点福利中心，点签到", "A", "sign", **kw)
    r2 = n.build_behavior("点我的，点福利中心，点签到", "A", "sign", **kw)
    assert r1 == r2
    assert r1["behavior"] == "sign"
    assert r1["entry"] == "personal_center"


def test_build_behavior_auto_entry():
    r = n.build_behavior("点我的，点福利中心，点签到", "A", "sign")
    assert r["entry"] == "personal_center"
    r2 = n.build_behavior("点右上角金币，点签到", "A", "sign")
    assert r2["entry"] == "feed_ball"
    r3 = n.build_behavior("点签到", "A", "sign")
    assert r3["entry"] == "home_direct"


def test_to_yaml_fragment_roundtrip():
    actions = n.build_behavior(
        "点我的，点福利中心，点签到", "A", "sign",
        params={"tab_xy": "0.9,0.914", "hub_xy": "0.3,0.5",
                "sign_xy": "0.5,0.641"})["actions"]
    frag = n.to_yaml_fragment(actions)
    loaded = yaml.safe_load(frag)
    assert loaded == actions


def test_to_yaml_fragment_special_chars_roundtrip():
    actions = [{
        "type": "click_first",
        "candidates": [
            {"type": "text", "value": "测试：全角冒号＋百分号%100"},
            {"type": "text", "value": "x+y 加号"},
        ],
        "candidates_from": "sign",
        "timeout": 8,
        "optional": False,
    }]
    frag = n.to_yaml_fragment(actions)
    assert yaml.safe_load(frag) == actions


# ---------- render_steps（自由解析兜底入口） ----------

def test_render_steps_basic():
    steps, w = n.parse_script("点我的，等2秒，上滑两次")
    actions, w2 = n.render_steps(steps, {}, "sign")
    assert w == [] and w2 == []
    assert [a["type"] for a in actions] == ["click_first", "wait", "swipe", "swipe"]
    assert actions[0]["candidates"][0]["value"] == "我的"
    assert actions[1]["seconds"] == 2.0


def test_render_steps_unknown_warning():
    steps, w = n.parse_line("随便乱写")
    assert steps[0]["verb"] == "unknown"
    actions, w2 = n.render_steps(steps, {}, "sign")
    assert actions == []
    assert len(w2) == 1 and "无法识别" in w2[0]
```

### `tests/test_page_owner.py`（128 行）

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""页面归属判定单测 —— 防跨 App 污染。

背景（踩过的坑）：
    目标 App 启动失败/崩溃时，前台会停留在**上一个 App**，于是两个 App 会得到
    完全相同的坐标（看似合理、实为垃圾数据）。uiautomator2 的
    `d.info.currentPackageName` 在这种时序下会失真（已报新包名但屏幕上还是旧 App），
    所以必须从 **dump XML 自身** 的 package 属性分布来判断「屏幕上实际是谁」。

本测试用工程内真实 dump 作为语料，确保：
  1. 正常页面被正确判为「属于目标 App」；
  2. 已知污染样本被正确判为「不属于目标 App」。
"""
import os
import sys
import glob
import unittest

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
ROOT = os.path.dirname(HERE)
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(ROOT, "devtools"))

from calibrate import (  # noqa: E402
    package_profile, dominant_package, page_belongs_to, SYS_PKGS,
)

CALIB = os.path.join(ROOT, "devtools", "calib")

# 已知污染样本：天天快讯极速版 的离线 dump 实际抓到的是系统时钟/图库。
# （App 没起来，前台停在系统 App 上）
KNOWN_CONTAMINATED = {
    "天天快讯极速版": "skysky.cy.kuaixun",
}

# 期望判为「属于目标 App」的样本（离线 dump 已确认干净）
KNOWN_CLEAN = {
    "河马剧场": "com.dz.hmjc",
    "红果免费短剧": "com.phoenix.read",
    "抖音": "com.ss.android.ugc.aweme",
    "一淘": "com.taobao.etao",
    "蛋花免费小说": "com.eggflower.read",
}


def _load(app, fname="ui.xml"):
    p = os.path.join(CALIB, app, fname)
    if not os.path.exists(p):
        return None
    with open(p, encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
        return f.read()


class TestPageOwner(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_system_packages_excluded(self):
        """系统 UI 包不应计入归属统计。"""
        xml = '<hierarchy><node package="com.android.systemui" class="x"/>' \
              '<node package="com.dz.hmjc" class="y"/></hierarchy>'
        prof = package_profile(xml)
        self.assertEqual(prof, {"com.dz.hmjc": 1})
        for p in SYS_PKGS:
            self.assertNotIn(p, prof)

    def test_dominant_package_picks_majority(self):
        xml = ('<hierarchy>'
               + '<node package="com.a"/>' * 3
               + '<node package="com.b"/>' * 7
               + '</hierarchy>')
        self.assertEqual(dominant_package(xml), "com.b")

    def test_belongs_true_for_own_page(self):
        xml = '<hierarchy>' + '<node package="com.dz.hmjc"/>' * 5 + '</hierarchy>'
        ok, dom, mine, dom_n = page_belongs_to(xml, "com.dz.hmjc")
        self.assertTrue(ok)
        self.assertEqual(dom, "com.dz.hmjc")
        self.assertEqual((mine, dom_n), (5, 5))

    def test_belongs_false_for_foreign_page(self):
        """目标 App 一个节点都没有 -> 判定为不属于。"""
        xml = '<hierarchy>' + '<node package="com.vivo.gallery"/>' * 9 + '</hierarchy>'
        ok, dom, mine, dom_n = page_belongs_to(xml, "skysky.cy.kuaixun")
        self.assertFalse(ok)
        self.assertEqual(dom, "com.vivo.gallery")
        self.assertEqual(mine, 0)

    def test_belongs_tolerates_webview_minority(self):
        """目标包是少数派但达到 ratio 阈值 -> 仍算属于（WebView 内嵌场景）。"""
        xml = ('<hierarchy>'
               + '<node package="com.dz.hmjc"/>' * 6
               + '<node package="com.other.sdk"/>' * 10
               + '</hierarchy>')
        ok, _, mine, dom_n = page_belongs_to(xml, "com.dz.hmjc", ratio=0.5)
        self.assertTrue(ok, f"mine={mine} dom_n={dom_n}")
        # ratio 提高后应判为不属于
        ok2, _, _, _ = page_belongs_to(xml, "com.dz.hmjc", ratio=0.9)
        self.assertFalse(ok2)

    def test_empty_dump_not_belongs(self):
        ok, dom, mine, dom_n = page_belongs_to("", "com.dz.hmjc")
        self.assertFalse(ok)
        self.assertEqual(dom, "")

    def test_known_clean_samples(self):
        """真实干净 dump 应判为属于目标 App。"""
        for app, pkg in KNOWN_CLEAN.items():
            xml = _load(app)
            if xml is None:
                continue
            ok, dom, mine, dom_n = page_belongs_to(xml, pkg)
            self.assertTrue(ok, f"{app}: 期望属于 {pkg}，实际主导 {dom}")

    def test_known_contaminated_samples(self):
        """真实污染 dump 必须被判为不属于目标 App（防假数据）。"""
        checked = 0
        for app, pkg in KNOWN_CONTAMINATED.items():
            for fname in ("ui.xml", "ui_after.xml"):
                xml = _load(app, fname)
                if xml is None:
                    continue
                ok, dom, mine, dom_n = page_belongs_to(xml, pkg)
                self.assertFalse(
                    ok, f"{app}/{fname}: 污染样本被误判为属于 {pkg}（主导 {dom}）")
                checked += 1
        self.assertGreater(checked, 0, "未找到污染样本语料，测试无效")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)
```

### `tests/test_page_ready.py`（168 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_page_ready.py - Executor._wait_page_ready 单元测试（无需真机）

背景：App 落地页不确定（开屏广告/活动弹窗/新手引导/上次退出的 tab/登录态），
启动后立刻 dump 常抓到启动页，导致锚点全部未命中。实测同一批配置不同轮次
结果差异显著。`_wait_page_ready` 轮询 dump，等节点数连续两次变化很小
（<=10%）且达标才认为页面已稳定。

运行：
    python tests/test_page_ready.py
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from executor import Executor  # noqa: E402

PKG = "com.demo.app"


class _Rec:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = []

    def log(self, event, app="", level="INFO"):
        self.lines.append(f"[{level}] {event}")


class _Info:
    def __init__(self, pkg):
        self._pkg = pkg

    def get(self, k, d=None):
        return self._pkg if k == "currentPackageName" else d


class _FakeDevice:
    """按脚本逐次返回节点数；脚本耗尽后重复最后一个值。"""

    def __init__(self, counts, pkgs=None):
        self.counts = list(counts)
        self.pkgs = list(pkgs) if pkgs else None
        self.i = 0
        self.dumps = 0

    def dump_hierarchy(self):
        n = self.counts[min(self.i, len(self.counts) - 1)]
        self.dumps += 1
        return "<hierarchy>" + "<node/>" * n + "</hierarchy>"

    @property
    def info(self):
        p = (self.pkgs[min(self.i, len(self.pkgs) - 1)]
             if self.pkgs else PKG)
        self.i += 1
        return _Info(p)


def _mk(device, dry_run=False):
    ex = Executor.__new__(Executor)
    ex.recorder = _Rec()
    ex.d = device
    ex.dry_run = dry_run
    return ex


def t_dry_run_short_circuit():
    ex = _mk(_FakeDevice([10]), dry_run=True)
    n, waited, stable = ex._wait_page_ready(PKG, "T", max_wait=1, poll=0.01)
    assert (n, waited, stable) == (0, 0.0, True), (n, waited, stable)
    assert ex.d.dumps == 0, "dry-run 不应 dump 设备"
    print("OK  dry-run 直接返回 (0,0,True)，不触碰设备")


def t_stable_immediately():
    """节点数稳定且达标 -> 很快返回稳定。"""
    ex = _mk(_FakeDevice([100, 100, 100]))
    n, waited, stable = ex._wait_page_ready(PKG, "T", max_wait=5, poll=0.01)
    assert stable is True, (n, waited, stable)
    assert n == 100, n
    print("OK  节点数稳定且达标时判定为已就绪")


def t_waits_until_settled():
    """页面先剧烈变化，稳定后才返回（不会在启动页就动手）。"""
    ex = _mk(_FakeDevice([5, 40, 42, 200, 200]))
    n, waited, stable = ex._wait_page_ready(PKG, "T", max_wait=5, poll=0.01)
    assert stable is True and n == 200, (n, waited, stable)
    assert ex.d.dumps >= 4, f"应至少轮询 4 次，实际 {ex.d.dumps}"
    print("OK  页面从启动页(5节点)变化到稳定(200节点)后才就绪")


def t_never_returns_on_wrong_foreground():
    """前台不是目标包时不应判定就绪（哪怕节点数稳定）。"""
    ex = _mk(_FakeDevice([100, 100, 100, 100, 100, 100],
                         pkgs=["com.other.app"] * 6))
    n, waited, stable = ex._wait_page_ready(PKG, "T", max_wait=0.05, poll=0.01)
    assert stable is False, "前台非目标包不应判就绪"
    print("OK  前台非目标包时不会误判为就绪")


def t_min_nodes_enforced():
    """节点数不达标（疑似启动页）不算稳定。"""
    ex = _mk(_FakeDevice([3, 3, 3, 3, 3, 3]))
    n, waited, stable = ex._wait_page_ready(PKG, "T", max_wait=0.05, poll=0.01)
    assert stable is False, "节点数不足不应判就绪"
    print("OK  节点数低于阈值时不算就绪")


def t_timeout_returns_last():
    """一直变化（始终不稳定）时超时返回，且不抛异常。

    2026-10-23：节点数保持在 80 以下，避免触发「波动型页面提前放行」兜底
    （该兜底见 t_volatile_page_accepted_early）。
    """
    ex = _mk(_FakeDevice([10, 20, 40, 60, 30, 70]))
    n, waited, stable = ex._wait_page_ready(PKG, "T", max_wait=0.05, poll=0.01)
    assert stable is False
    assert n > 0
    print("OK  持续不稳定时超时返回（不阻塞流程）")


def t_volatile_page_accepted_early():
    """波动型页面（节点数一直小幅变化但已很丰富）应提前放行，不再白等满 max_wait。"""
    ex = _mk(_FakeDevice([100, 300, 500, 900, 1300, 1700]))
    n, waited, stable = ex._wait_page_ready(PKG, "T", max_wait=0.2, poll=0.01)
    assert stable is True, "节点数充足且已过半数预算时应提前放行"
    assert n >= 80
    assert waited < 0.2, f"应早于 max_wait 返回，实际 {waited}"
    print("OK  波动型页面提前放行（省下等待时间）")


def t_dump_exception_is_tolerated():
    """dump 抛异常时应继续重试而不是崩掉。"""
    class _Bad(_FakeDevice):
        def __init__(self):
            super().__init__([100, 100, 100, 100])
            self.first = True

        def dump_hierarchy(self):
            if self.first:
                self.first = False
                raise RuntimeError("device offline")
            return super().dump_hierarchy()

    ex = _mk(_Bad())
    n, waited, stable = ex._wait_page_ready(PKG, "T", max_wait=5, poll=0.01)
    assert stable is True and n == 100, (n, waited, stable)
    assert any("探测异常" in l for l in ex.recorder.lines)
    print("OK  dump 异常被容忍并继续重试")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [t_dry_run_short_circuit, t_stable_immediately,
             t_waits_until_settled, t_never_returns_on_wrong_foreground,
             t_min_nodes_enforced, t_timeout_returns_last,
             t_dump_exception_is_tolerated]
    failed = 0
    for t in tests:
        try:
            t()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {t.__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_pick_best_pure.py`（109 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_pick_best_pure.py - pick_locate.pick_best 纯函数单元测试（A2）

覆盖：
  1. nav 带边界：top == NAV_BAND_Y * screen_h 恰好算带内（>= 判据）；
  2. 平局取先：完全同分时返回先出现的下标；
  3. screen_h=0 时跳过 nav 带内加分（未知屏高不猜）；
  4. ~~拒配 limit 边界~~ 2026-10-28 改造：limit+1 且不可点击不再一票否决，
     非噪声句式打分兜底放行、FAQ/通知句式维持拒绝。

运行：
    python -m pytest tests/test_pick_best_pure.py -q
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from pick_locate import pick_best, NAV_BAND_Y  # noqa: E402


def _log(lines):
    return lambda event, app="", level="INFO": lines.append((level, event))


def test_nav_boundary_in_band():
    """top 恰好等于 NAV_BAND_Y*screen_h 时算带内，应获 +120 加分。"""
    screen_h = 2340
    band = int(NAV_BAND_Y * screen_h)
    lines = []
    entries = [
        {"label": "我的", "clickable": True, "top": 500},    # 带外
        {"label": "我的", "clickable": False, "top": band},  # 边界=带内
    ]
    idx = pick_best(entries, "我的", "text", screen_h, _log(lines))
    assert idx == 1, f"边界带内条目应被选中，实际 {idx}"
    print("OK  nav 边界 top==NAV_BAND_Y*screen_h 算带内")


def test_tie_keeps_first():
    """完全同分时返回先出现的下标（平局取先）。"""
    entries = [
        {"label": "签到", "clickable": True, "top": 100},
        {"label": "签到", "clickable": True, "top": 100},
    ]
    idx = pick_best(entries, "签到", "text", 2340)
    assert idx == 0, f"平局应取先，实际 {idx}"
    print("OK  平局取先")


def test_screen_h_zero_skips_nav():
    """screen_h=0（未知屏高）时跳过 nav 带内加分，不猜位置。"""
    entries = [
        {"label": "我的", "clickable": False, "top": 500},
        {"label": "我的", "clickable": False, "top": 2000},
    ]
    idx = pick_best(entries, "我的", "text", 0)
    assert idx == 0, f"screen_h=0 时不应做 nav 加分，平局取先 -> 0，实际 {idx}"
    print("OK  screen_h=0 跳过 nav 加分")


def test_limit_boundary_accept_and_noise_reject():
    """长度阈值边界语义（2026-10-28 版）：

    - len<=limit 照常接受；
    - len==limit+1 且不可点击：**非噪声**句式打分兜底放行（旧版此处拒配），
      **噪声**句式（FAQ/通知/疑问）维持拒绝 —— 后者由
      test_overlong_faq_still_rejected 与 salvage 系列用例锁定。
    本用例用纯数字长串（无目标词、无标点收尾、无噪声标记）验证「放行」侧。
    """
    # target='签到' len=2 -> limit=max(8,6)=8
    accept = pick_best([{"label": "12345678", "clickable": False, "top": 0}],
                       "签到", "text", 0)
    assert accept == 0, "len==limit 应被接受"
    lines = []
    salvage = pick_best([{"label": "123456789", "clickable": False, "top": 0}],
                        "签到", "text", 0, _log(lines))
    assert salvage == 0, "len==limit+1 且非噪声 -> 兜底放行（新语义）"
    assert any("兜底" in m for _l, m in lines), \
        f"放行必须留 WARN 痕迹，实际 {lines}"
    print("OK  limit 边界：<=limit 接受 / +1 非噪声兜底放行+WARN")


def test_overlong_faq_still_rejected():
    """经典冤案反例：FAQ 问句是超长唯一候选时仍判误命中（行为不回退）。"""
    lines = []
    entries = [{"label": "什么是签到领钱，如何参加？什么是签到领钱，如何参加？",
                "clickable": False, "top": 900}]
    idx = pick_best(entries, "签到", "text", 2340, _log(lines))
    assert idx is None, f"FAQ 长句应维持拒绝，实际 {idx}"
    assert any("噪声" in m for _l, m in lines), lines
    print("OK  FAQ 超长句维持拒配")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("test_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_pick_scoring.py`（227 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_pick_scoring.py - 打分择优改造（#2）的回归单测

背景：旧版 pick_best 对「超长且不可点击」的候选一票否决，实测制造了大量
冤案（data/records.db WARN 归纳出 ~20 例）：真短按钮被同页 FAQ/推送文案
挤掉、营销后缀入口（'立即签到 +1000火龙币'）整条扔弃。

新语义：全量打分排序，超长只是重罚；仅当**无任何常规可信候选**时才进入
超长甄别兜底 —— 噪声句式（FAQ/疑问/说明句/标点收尾长句）照旧拒绝。

用例全部取自 records.db 的真实失败文案，按三类场景组织：
  A. score_entry 分值结构（含旧口径兼容：精确>可点>更短）
  B. pick_best 多匹配择优（冤案场景翻案 + 经典正确拒配不回退）
  C. 兜底顺序与降级路径（推送标题降权、probe_deep._ok_len 同口径）

运行：
    python -m pytest tests/test_pick_scoring.py -q
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from pick_locate import (pick_best, score_entry, is_noise_label,
                         overlen_limit, _PLAUSIBLE_FLOOR)  # noqa: E402


def _log(lines):
    return lambda event, app="", level="INFO": lines.append((level, event))


def _ent(label, clickable=False, top=1000, left=None, right=None):
    e = {"label": label, "clickable": clickable, "top": top}
    if left is not None:
        e["left"], e["right"] = left, right
    return e


# ===========================================================================
# A. score_entry 分值结构
# ===========================================================================
def t_exact_beats_everything():
    """精确相等 1000+clickable50=1050 > 任何模糊命中（旧口径保持）。"""
    s_exact, _ = score_entry(_ent("签到", True), "签到", "text")
    s_contains, ov = score_entry(_ent("签到福利", True), "签到", "text")
    assert s_exact == 1050 and s_contains < 300 and ov is False
    print("OK  精确相等压倒一切，正常长度不加 overlong 标志")


def t_clickable_outweighs_length():
    """可点击(+50)在含词同档内足以翻长度差；精确命中(1000)仍是铁王座。"""
    s_a, _ = score_entry(_ent("签到吧", True), "签到", "text")      # 100-3+80+50
    s_b, _ = score_entry(_ent("签到", False), "签到", "text")       # 1000
    s_c, _ = score_entry(_ent("每日签到领现金活动入口", False), "签到", "text")  # 含词长文案
    assert s_a == 227 and s_b > s_a and s_c < s_a, (s_a, s_b, s_c)
    print("OK  clickable 加权生效且不翻转精确命中")


def t_overlong_penalized_not_rejected():
    """超长非可点：罚 700 分但仍返回数值（否决权已上交 pick_best 甄别层）。"""
    lab = "关闭签到提醒后,可能会错过签到福利领取,确认关闭"   # 25 字 > limit 8
    s, ov = score_entry(_ent(lab), "签到", "text")
    assert ov is True and s < 0
    lim = overlen_limit("签到")
    s_ok, ov_ok = score_entry(_ent("x" * lim), "签到", "text")
    assert ov_ok is False, "len==limit 恰在线内（口径与旧版一致）"
    print("OK  超长 -> 罚分为负，阈值边界 len==limit 不罚")


def t_noise_double_penalty_and_exempt():
    """FAQ/通知句式双罚（-700-400）；疑问标记须独立成词才算。"""
    s_faq, _ = score_entry(_ent("什么是签到领钱，如何参加？"), "签到", "text")
    s_mkt, _ = score_entry(_ent("立即签到 +1000火龙币x"), "签到", "text")
    assert s_faq < s_mkt, (s_faq, s_mkt)
    # 「查看」是按钮动词（查看更多入口），不再列为噪声标记 ——
    # 判噪会误伤「查看更多福利」这类真入口文案，锁定该豁免不回退：
    assert not is_noise_label("查看更多福利", "更多")
    print("OK  噪声追加罚分低于纯营销长文案；按钮动词不判噪")


def t_punctuation_tail_rule():
    """标点收尾规则只打不含目标词的说明句；含词营销后缀句放行。"""
    # 含 '取消' -> 标点收尾规则不适用（它是真入口文案的可能性远大于说明句）
    assert not is_noise_label("本周五更新取消，下周一补上 20 集，审核原因，", "取消")
    assert is_noise_label("活动最终解释权归平台所有。", "取消")   # 不含词+句号
    assert not is_noise_label("再看16s可领取奖励", "领取")
    print("OK  标点收尾规则仅作用于不含目标词的长句")


# ===========================================================================
# B. pick_best 多匹配择优（冤案场景）
# ===========================================================================
def t_real_button_wins_over_faq_and_notification():
    """京东金融场景：真短按钮 vs 推送标题 vs FAQ —— 选真按钮。"""
    entries = [
        _ent("京东金融通知：签到即将中断", top=100),
        _ent("签到", clickable=True, top=1500),
        _ent("什么是签到领钱，如何参加？", top=900),
    ]
    idx = pick_best(entries, "签到", "text", 2340)
    assert idx == 1, f"应选中可点的『签到』，实际 {idx}"
    print("OK  多匹配：真按钮胜出（噪声文案沉底但不否决全局）")


def t_marketing_suffix_button_salvaged():
    """冤案翻案：唯一候选是营销后缀入口 -> 兜底放行并留 WARN。"""
    lines = []
    entries = [_ent("立即签到 +1000火龙币", clickable=True, top=300)]
    idx = pick_best(entries, "签到", "text", 2340, _log(lines))
    assert idx == 0, f"可点击营销入口必须放行，实际 {idx}"
    print("OK  营销后缀可点入口照常选中（score>floor 直接可信）")


def t_nonclickable_marketing_salvage_warns():
    """入口文案挂在不可点击子节点（父容器才可点）：兜底放行 + WARN 留痕。"""
    lines = []
    entries = [_ent("去福狸中心做任务领金币", top=900)]
    idx = pick_best(entries, "做任务", "text", 2340, _log(lines))
    assert idx == 0
    assert any(l == "WARN" and "兜底" in m for l, m in lines), lines
    print("OK  不可点入口文案兜底放行且必留 WARN（可审计）")


def t_all_noise_returns_none():
    """全是 FAQ/问句时维持 None（视觉兜底链路入口不能丢）。"""
    entries = [_ent("什么是签到领钱，如何参加？"*2, top=900),
               _ent("怎样查看活动规则？", top=950)]
    assert pick_best(entries, "签到", "text", 2340) is None
    assert pick_best([], "签到", "text", 2340) is None
    print("OK  全噪声/空列表 -> None（旧正确行为零回退）")


def t_plausible_floor_excludes_salvage_zone():
    """可信线含义锁定：含目标词的正常长度候选 > floor，超长候选 <= floor
    —— 兜底甄别分支只对超长/低分池开启。"""
    normal, ov_n = score_entry(_ent("每日签到领现金"), "签到", "text")
    over, ov_o = score_entry(_ent("签到" + "长" * 30), "签到", "text")
    assert normal > _PLAUSIBLE_FLOOR and ov_n is False
    assert over <= _PLAUSIBLE_FLOOR and ov_o is True
    print("OK  可信线两侧划分正确（含词正常长度 / 超长）")


def t_no_target_long_copy_cannot_beat_contains_target_short():
    """铁律：超长/降档的营销长句永远压不过含目标词的常规可点候选。

    冤案原型 target='红包'：'看新闻就能领红包，秒到账'(12字>limit8,非可点)
    vs '领红包'(含词、正常长度、可点)。后者必须胜出。
    """
    entries = [
        _ent("看新闻就能领红包，秒到账", top=400),      # 含词但超长非可点
        _ent("领红包", clickable=True, top=500),        # 含词、正常长度、可点
    ]
    idx = pick_best(entries, "红包", "text", 2340)
    assert idx == 1, f"真按钮必须胜出，实际 {idx}"
    # 不含目标词的等长说明句则被降档到不可信区
    s_nt, _ = score_entry(_ent("蚂蚁搬砖奖励翻倍啦"), "红包", "desc")
    assert s_nt < _PLAUSIBLE_FLOOR
    print("OK  超长罚分+不含词降档共同保证真按钮优先")


# ===========================================================================
# C. 兜底顺序 / zone 与 nav 先验在新框架下不变
# ===========================================================================
def t_salvage_prefers_button_over_push_title():
    """兜底排序：都含目标词时，营销按钮优先于『…通知/提醒』标题。"""
    entries = [
        _ent("闲鱼通知：闲鱼币今日签到提醒", top=100),
        _ent("立即签到 +1000火龙币", top=300),
    ]
    idx = pick_best(entries, "签到", "text", 2340)
    assert idx == 1, f"推送标题应让位于按钮文案，实际 {idx}"
    print("OK  兜底顺序：按钮文案 > 推送标题")


def t_nav_band_prior_still_applies():
    """导航词先验在新打分下保持不变：底部带内胜出中部假命中。"""
    entries = [_ent("我的", top=627), _ent("我的", top=2100)]
    idx = pick_best(entries, "我的", "text", 2340)
    assert idx == 1
    print("OK  nav 带 ±120 先验未受改造影响")


def t_zone_bonus_still_applies():
    """tab_zone 先验保持：声明了 zone 时底部候选加分生效。"""
    entries = [_ent("赚钱", top=600), _ent("赚钱", top=2160)]
    idx = pick_best(entries, "赚钱", "text", 2340, screen_w=1080,
                    zone="tab_zone")
    assert idx == 1
    print("OK  zone 位置先验未受改造影响")


def t_probe_ok_len_parity():
    """度量与执行同口径：probe_deep._ok_len 复用同一甄别器。

    旧版硬滤会连营销入口一起滤除；新版放行非噪声超长文案、
    照旧滤除 FAQ 句式 —— 探针不再比执行器更悲观。
    """
    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(ROOT, "devtools"))
    from probe_deep import _ok_len as ok_len

    def node(text, clickable):
        return {"text": text, "desc": "", "clickable": clickable}

    assert ok_len(node("什么是签到领钱，如何参加？", False), "签到", "text") \
        is False, "FAQ 仍须滤除"
    assert ok_len(node("立即签到 +1000火龙币", False), "签到", "text") \
        is True, "营销入口不再被硬滤"
    assert ok_len(node("签到", False), "签到", "text") is True
    assert ok_len(node("x" * 40, True), "签到", "text") is True, "可点不受限"
    print("OK  probe_deep._ok_len 与 pick_locate 甄别器同口径")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_probe_flag.py`（112 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_probe_flag.py - 非 any_of 行为的定位未命中不崩溃（无需真机）

背景（真实缺陷，B6 修复）：`_step_click_text/_click_id/_click_desc/_click_first`
里直接读 `self._probe_no_vision`，而该属性最初只在 `_step_any_of` 内部赋值，
**任何不含 `any_of` 的行为**一旦走到「元素没找到 → 该走视觉兜底了」就会
`AttributeError`，把「没找到按钮」这种正常情况变成崩溃。

B7 演进：视觉门控从实例标志位改为显式参数链（`_step_click_*(…, allow_vision=True)`），
不再有 `_probe_no_vision` 属性。教训：靠"临时改写再还原"的实例标志位做门控，
异常/新增直调点会留下脏状态；现在 allow_vision 显式贯穿，参数默认值
天然保证非 any_of 行为可走视觉兜底。原「属性必须有默认值」与「反证删除即崩」
两用例随之取消（钉的属性已不存在），语义用例保留：非 any_of 定位未命中
一律返回 False 且不崩溃。

运行：
    python tests/test_probe_flag.py
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from executor import Executor, LocateResult  # noqa: E402


class _Rec:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = []

    def log(self, event, app="", level="INFO"):
        self.lines.append(f"[{level}] {event}")


class _KB:
    def mark_ok(self, app, sel):
        pass

    def mark_fail(self, app, sel):
        pass


class _Human:
    def delay(self, *a):
        pass

    def maybe_wander(self, *a):
        pass


def _mk():
    """用**真实构造函数**造 Executor（dry_run，不连设备）。

    刻意不用 `Executor.__new__` 绕过 `__init__` —— 本文件要钉住的正是
    「`__init__` 缺初始化时行为是否还安全」。B7 后门控是参数默认值，
    `__init__` 不再有 _probe_no_vision，用例只验行为不崩。
    """
    ex = Executor("fake-serial", _Human(), _Rec(), dry_run=True, config={})
    ex._locate = lambda k, v, timeout=2.0: LocateResult(False, None, "测试未命中")
    ex._kb_candidates = lambda app, primary: ([primary], False)
    ex._kb_mark_fail = lambda app, prims: None
    return ex


def t_click_first_without_anyof_does_not_crash():
    """不含 any_of 的行为，候选全未命中 + optional -> 返回 False，不崩。"""
    ex = _mk()
    act = {"type": "click_first",
           "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"}],
           "timeout": 8, "optional": True}
    assert ex._step_click_first(act, "T") is False
    print("OK  非 any_of 行为的 click_first 未命中不崩溃")


def t_click_first_without_anyof_returns_false_when_required():
    """非 optional 时返回 False 并记录 detail（B6 不再抛 StepError）。"""
    ex = _mk()
    act = {"type": "click_first",
           "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"}],
           "timeout": 8, "optional": False}
    res = ex._step_click_first(act, "T")
    assert res is False, f"应返回 False，实际 {res!r}"
    assert ex._last_fail and "所有候选均未出现" in ex._last_fail, ex._last_fail
    print("OK  非 optional 未命中返回 False 并记录 detail（语义正确）")


def t_click_text_without_anyof_returns_false():
    ex = _mk()
    act = {"type": "click_text", "text": "提现", "timeout": 6, "optional": True}
    try:
        res = ex._step_click_text(act, "T")
    except AttributeError as e:
        raise AssertionError(f"不应是 AttributeError: {e}")
    assert res is False, f"应返回 False，实际 {res!r}"
    print("OK  非 any_of 行为的 click_text 未命中返回 False 不崩溃")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_probe_pick.py`（139 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_probe_pick.py - 探针候选挑选逻辑的单元测试（无需真机）

为什么必须测：
    `probe_deep` 报「二级入口」和「三级锚点」直接决定我们往 config 里写什么路径。
    挑错一次，就会写进一条**永远命不中的三级导航**（而且要下一轮才发现）。

这里固化两个关键决策：
  1. **可点击优先排序，但不硬过滤** —— 实测入口文案常常挂在不可点击的 text
     子节点上，真正可点的是父容器（一淘的「去福狸中心做任务」就是这种）。
     早期版本用「必须可点击」硬过滤，把整个入口滤掉了，导致漏判。
  2. **长文案拒配** —— `textContains` 会命中"长文案里恰好包含关键词"的节点
     （例如 FAQ 链接「什么是签到领钱，如何参加？」里含「签到」）。

运行：
    python tests/test_probe_pick.py
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(ROOT, "devtools"))

from probe_deep import find_all, find_first, _ok_len  # noqa: E402
from probe_sidebar import find_more, MORE_DESC  # noqa: E402


def _n(text="", desc="", cx=500, cy=1000, clickable=True, rid=""):
    return {"text": text, "desc": desc, "rid": rid, "center": (cx, cy),
            "size": (100, 100), "clickable": clickable,
            "bounds": (cx - 50, cy - 50, cx + 50, cy + 50)}


def t_ok_len_rejects_long_nonclickable():
    long_text = "什么是签到领钱，如何参加？" * 3
    assert not _ok_len(_n(text=long_text, clickable=False), "签到", "text")
    # 可点击的长文案不受限（可能是按钮整体）
    assert _ok_len(_n(text=long_text, clickable=True), "签到", "text")
    # 短文案接受
    assert _ok_len(_n(text="签到"), "签到", "text")
    print("OK  长文案拒配：不可点击的超长文本被拒，可点击的不受影响")


def t_find_first_prefers_clickable():
    nodes = [_n(text="任务中心", clickable=False, cy=300),
             _n(text="任务中心", clickable=True, cy=2100)]
    hit = find_first(nodes, ["任务中心"])
    assert hit is not None
    lab, c, kind = hit
    assert c == (500, 2100), f"应优先可点击的，实际 {c}"
    print("OK  find_first 优先返回可点击节点")


def t_find_first_without_hard_filter_keeps_nonclickable():
    """入口文案挂在不可点击的 text 上时，不能整个滤掉。"""
    nodes = [_n(text="去福狸中心做任务", clickable=False, cy=900)]
    hit = find_first(nodes, ["任务中心", "做任务"])
    assert hit is not None, "不可点击的入口不应被硬过滤掉"
    assert hit[0] == "去福狸中心做任务"
    print("OK  不做硬过滤：不可点击的入口仍能被选中")


def t_find_first_require_clickable_is_strict():
    nodes = [_n(text="签到", clickable=False)]
    assert find_first(nodes, ["签到"], require_clickable=True) is None
    assert find_first(nodes, ["签到"]) is not None
    print("OK  require_clickable=True 时才硬过滤")


def t_find_all_dedupes_and_limits():
    nodes = [
        _n(text="福利", cx=100, cy=100),
        _n(text="福利", cx=100, cy=100),      # 完全重复 -> 去重
        _n(text="福利中心", cx=200, cy=200),
        _n(text="任务中心", cx=300, cy=300),
        _n(text="金币中心", cx=400, cy=400),
    ]
    out = find_all(nodes, ["福利", "任务中心", "金币中心"], limit=6)
    labs = [o[0] for o in out]
    assert len(labs) == len(set(labs)), f"未去重: {labs}"
    assert labs.count("福利") == 1, labs
    assert len(out) <= 6
    out2 = find_all(nodes, ["福利", "任务中心", "金币中心"], limit=2)
    assert len(out2) == 2, out2
    print("OK  find_all 去重 + 受 limit 限制")


def t_find_all_orders_clickable_first():
    nodes = [_n(text="赚钱", clickable=False, cy=300),
             _n(text="赚钱", clickable=True, cy=2000)]
    out = find_all(nodes, ["赚钱"])
    assert out[0][1] == (500, 2000), out
    print("OK  find_all 可点击优先排序")


def t_find_all_empty_when_no_match():
    assert find_all([_n(text="首页")], ["任务中心"]) == []
    print("OK  无匹配返回空列表")


def t_find_more_prefers_exact_desc():
    nodes = [_n(desc="更多功能", clickable=True, cx=1, cy=1),
             _n(desc=MORE_DESC, clickable=True, cx=984, cy=174)]
    xy, via = find_more(nodes)
    assert xy == (984, 174), (xy, via)
    assert via == "desc精确", via
    print("OK  find_more 优先 content-desc 精确等于『更多』")


def t_find_more_falls_back_to_contains():
    nodes = [_n(desc="查看更多", clickable=True, cx=900, cy=170)]
    xy, via = find_more(nodes)
    assert xy == (900, 170), (xy, via)
    assert via == "desc包含", via
    print("OK  find_more 退而求其次用 desc 包含")


def t_find_more_ignores_nonclickable_and_returns_none():
    nodes = [_n(desc=MORE_DESC, clickable=False)]
    xy, via = find_more(nodes)
    assert xy is None, (xy, via)
    assert via == "未找到", via
    print("OK  find_more 忽略不可点击的『更多』并返回未找到")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_record_flow.py`（471 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_record_flow.py - 引导式录制（AUTOP-NL-2026-10-22-B 第2层）

覆盖：ui_digest / page_delta / step_to_action / steps_to_actions / guess_entry /
is_money_step / assemble_actions / machine_check / record_step（mock 设备）/ finish 合并顺序。

运行：
    ./.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest tests/test_record_flow.py -q
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

import pytest  # noqa: E402

import devtools.record_flow as rf  # noqa: E402

PKG = "com.example.app"


# ---------- 工具 ----------

def _xml(nodes, package=PKG):
    """节点 -> UI dump XML。节点键：text/desc/rid/clickable/l/t/r/b。"""
    parts = ['<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>', "<hierarchy>"]
    for n in nodes:
        attrs = [
            f'package="{package}"',
            f'text="{n.get("text", "")}"',
            f'content-desc="{n.get("desc", "")}"',
            f'resource-id="{n.get("rid", "")}"',
            'class="android.view.View"',
            f'clickable="{str(n.get("clickable", True)).lower()}"',
            f'bounds="[{n["l"]},{n["t"]}][{n["r"]},{n["b"]}]"',
        ]
        parts.append("<node " + " ".join(attrs) + "/>")
    parts.append("</hierarchy>")
    return "\n".join(parts)


MAIN_XML = _xml([
    {"text": "首页", "l": 0, "t": 0, "r": 200, "b": 100},
    {"text": "我的", "l": 880, "t": 2200, "r": 1080, "b": 2340},
])
SIGN_XML = _xml([
    {"text": "签到", "l": 400, "t": 900, "r": 680, "b": 1100, "clickable": True},
    {"text": "我的", "l": 880, "t": 2200, "r": 1080, "b": 2340},
])
JUMP_XML = _xml([
    {"text": "相机", "l": 100, "t": 100, "r": 300, "b": 300, "clickable": True},
], package="com.other.app")


class FakeDevice:
    """按页序返回 dump 的假设备；超界重复最后一页。"""

    def __init__(self, pages, package=PKG):
        self._pages = list(pages)
        self._idx = 0
        self.window = (1080, 2340)
        self.current = {"package": package, "activity": ".Main"}
        self.clicks = []
        self.swipes = []
        self.presses = []

    def dump_hierarchy(self):
        if not self._pages:
            return ""
        p = self._pages[min(self._idx, len(self._pages) - 1)]
        self._idx += 1
        return p

    def click(self, x, y):
        self.clicks.append((x, y))

    def swipe(self, x1, y1, x2, y2, **kw):
        self.swipes.append((x1, y1, x2, y2))

    def press(self, key):
        self.presses.append(key)

    def window_size(self):
        return self.window

    def app_current(self):
        return dict(self.current)

    def screenshot(self, path):  # pragma: no cover - 无真机不写盘
        pass


def mk_step(seq=1, cmd="点签到", verb="tap", target="签到", hit_kind="text",
            hit_value="签到", point_norm=(0.5, 0.5), delta=0.5,
            confirmed=True, **kw):
    act_before = kw.pop("act_before", ".Main")
    act_after = kw.pop("act_after", ".Sign")
    return rf.Step(seq=seq, cmd=cmd, verb=verb, target=target, pos=None,
                   hit_kind=hit_kind, hit_value=hit_value,
                   bounds=(0, 0, 100, 100), point_norm=point_norm,
                   act_before=act_before, act_after=act_after, delta=delta,
                   confirmed=confirmed, **kw)


def _no_sleep(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(rf.time, "sleep", lambda *a, **k: None)


# ---------- ui_digest / page_delta ----------

def t_ui_digest_order_insensitive():
    xml1 = _xml([
        {"text": "签到", "l": 10, "t": 20, "r": 30, "b": 40, "clickable": True},
        {"text": "我的", "l": 50, "t": 60, "r": 70, "b": 80},
    ])
    xml2 = _xml([
        {"text": "我的", "l": 50, "t": 60, "r": 70, "b": 80},
        {"text": "签到", "l": 10, "t": 20, "r": 30, "b": 40, "clickable": True},
    ])
    assert rf.ui_digest(xml1) == rf.ui_digest(xml2)
    assert len(rf.ui_digest(xml1)) == 2


def t_ui_digest_skips_inert_nodes():
    xml = _xml([
        {"text": "", "desc": "", "rid": "", "l": 0, "t": 0, "r": 10, "b": 10,
         "clickable": False},
        {"text": "签到", "l": 0, "t": 0, "r": 100, "b": 50, "clickable": True},
    ])
    digest = rf.ui_digest(xml)
    assert len(digest) == 1
    assert any(t == "签到" for t, _, _, _ in digest)


def t_page_delta_same_zero():
    d = rf.ui_digest(MAIN_XML)
    assert rf.page_delta(d, d) == 0.0


def t_page_delta_all_changed_one():
    a = rf.ui_digest(_xml([{"text": "A", "l": 0, "t": 0, "r": 10, "b": 10}]))
    b = rf.ui_digest(_xml([{"text": "B", "l": 0, "t": 0, "r": 10, "b": 10}]))
    assert rf.page_delta(a, b) == 1.0


def t_page_delta_both_empty_zero():
    assert rf.page_delta(frozenset(), frozenset()) == 0.0


def t_page_delta_partial_between():
    shared = {"text": "我的", "l": 880, "t": 2200, "r": 1080, "b": 2340}
    a = rf.ui_digest(_xml([shared, {"text": "首页", "l": 0, "t": 0,
                                    "r": 200, "b": 100}]))
    b = rf.ui_digest(_xml([shared, {"text": "签到", "l": 400, "t": 900,
                                    "r": 680, "b": 1100}]))
    d = rf.page_delta(a, b)
    assert 0.0 < d < 1.0


# ---------- step_to_action ----------

def t_step_to_action_text_hit():
    act = rf.step_to_action(mk_step(hit_kind="text", hit_value="签到"))
    assert act == {
        "type": "click_first",
        "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"}],
        "timeout": 8,
        "optional": True,
    }


def t_step_to_action_desc_id_hit():
    for kind in ("desc", "id"):
        act = rf.step_to_action(mk_step(hit_kind=kind, hit_value="v"))
        assert act["type"] == "click_first"
        assert act["candidates"] == [{"type": kind, "value": "v"}]


def t_step_to_action_point_hit():
    act = rf.step_to_action(mk_step(hit_kind="point", hit_value="右下角",
                                    point_norm=(0.9, 0.88)))
    assert act["type"] == "click_point"
    assert act["x"] == 0.9 and act["y"] == 0.88
    assert act["vision"] is False


def t_step_to_action_swipe_wait_back():
    assert rf.step_to_action(mk_step(verb="swipe", target="上滑",
                                     hit_kind=None, hit_value=None)) == \
        {"type": "swipe_natural"}
    assert rf.step_to_action(mk_step(verb="wait", target="等2秒",
                                     hit_kind=None, hit_value=None)) == \
        {"type": "wait", "seconds": 2.0}
    assert rf.step_to_action(mk_step(verb="back", target="返回",
```
