```python
# ---------- finish ----------

def t_finish_merge_order_hops_first(monkeypatch):
    _no_sleep(monkeypatch)
    rec = rf.FlowRecorder("七猫免费小说", PKG)
    rec.trace = [mk_step(seq=1, hit_kind="text", hit_value="签到"),
                 mk_step(seq=2, verb="wait", target="等2秒", hit_kind=None,
                         hit_value=None)]
    captured = {}

    monkeypatch.setattr(rf, "NL_TO_STEPS_READY", True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(rf, "render_hop", lambda entry, params: [
        {"type": "click_first",
         "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "我的"}],
         "timeout": 8, "optional": True}])

    def fake_app_path(name, **kw):
        return "/fake/apps.yaml"

    def fake_insert(path, bid, actions, **kw):
        captured["bid"] = bid
        captured["actions"] = list(actions)
        return rf.yw.WriteResult(path, "inserted", None, "", [])

    monkeypatch.setattr(rf.yw, "app_path", fake_app_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(rf.yw, "insert_behavior", fake_insert)

    res = rec.finish("七猫免费小说", "sign", entry="我的")
    assert res.action == "inserted"
    assert captured["bid"] == "sign"
    assert captured["actions"][0]["candidates"][0]["value"] == "我的"  # hop 在前
    assert captured["actions"][1]["candidates"][0]["value"] == "签到"  # 录制步在后
    assert captured["actions"][2] == {"type": "wait", "seconds": 2.0}


def t_finish_existing_behavior_falls_back_to_replace(monkeypatch):
    rec = rf.FlowRecorder("七猫免费小说", PKG)
    rec.trace = [mk_step(seq=1, hit_kind="text", hit_value="签到")]
    captured = {}

    def fake_app_path(name, **kw):
        return "/fake/apps.yaml"

    def fake_insert(path, bid, actions, **kw):
        return rf.yw.WriteResult(path, "skipped", None, "",
                                 ["sign 已存在；如需覆盖请用 replace_behavior(force=True)"])

    def fake_replace(path, bid, actions, **kw):
        captured["bid"] = bid
        captured["force"] = kw.get("force")
        return rf.yw.WriteResult(path, "replaced", "bak", "", [])

    monkeypatch.setattr(rf.yw, "app_path", fake_app_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(rf.yw, "insert_behavior", fake_insert)
    monkeypatch.setattr(rf.yw, "replace_behavior", fake_replace)

    res = rec.finish("七猫免费小说", "sign", entry="auto", apply=True, force=True)
    assert res.action == "replaced"
    assert captured["bid"] == "sign"
    assert captured["force"] is True


# ---------- 2026-10-22 真机实跑修正：tap 未命中且无坐标必须拦截 ----------

def t_machine_check_rejects_missed_tap_even_when_delta_high():
    """真机踩坑：App 自身加载会造成高 delta/activity 变化，
    旧判据把「什么都没点」误判为有效点击。现在显式拦截。"""
    rec = rf.FlowRecorder.__new__(rf.FlowRecorder)
    rec.allow_money = False
    rec.money_words = set()
    step = mk_step(hit_kind=None, hit_value=None, point_norm=None,
                   delta=1.0, act_before=".Loading", act_after=".Home")
    ok, note, fatal = rec._machine_check(step, "<x/>", ".Loading")
    assert ok is False, "未定位到元素的 tap 不应通过"
    assert "未定位到元素" in note, note
    assert fatal is False
    print("OK  tap 未命中且无坐标：即使 delta=1.0 也拦截")


def t_machine_check_allows_tap_with_point_only():
    rec = rf.FlowRecorder.__new__(rf.FlowRecorder)
    rec.allow_money = False
    rec.money_words = set()
    step = mk_step(hit_kind="point", hit_value=None, point_norm=(0.5, 0.927))
    ok, _note, _fatal = rec._machine_check(step, "<x/>", ".Main")
    assert ok is True, "有坐标兜底的 tap 应放行"
    print("OK  有坐标兜底的 tap 正常放行")


def t_steps_to_actions_names_the_dropped_step():
    good = mk_step(seq=1, cmd="点福利")
    bad = mk_step(seq=2, cmd="点不存在的按钮", hit_kind=None,
                  hit_value=None, point_norm=None)
    actions, warnings = rf.steps_to_actions([good, bad])
    assert len(actions) == 1, actions
    assert any("step#2" in w and "未定位到元素" in w for w in warnings), warnings
    print("OK  steps_to_actions 告警指名到步与原因")


def t_suggest_targets_prints_clickable_texts(capsys):
    """未命中时给出当前屏可点文案，交互录制的关键提示。"""
    rec = rf.FlowRecorder.__new__(rf.FlowRecorder)
    # 注意：parse_nodes 会丢弃 y2<120 的状态栏节点，测试数据要避开
    xml = ('<h><node text="福利" clickable="true" bounds="[0,200][100,300]"/>'
           '<node text="我的" clickable="true" bounds="[0,400][100,500]"/></h>')
    rec._dump = lambda: xml
    rec._suggest_targets(limit=5)
    out = capsys.readouterr().out
    assert "福利" in out and "我的" in out and "未定位到元素" in out, out
    print("OK  未命中时打印当前屏可点文案")
```

### `tests/test_recorder_batch.py`（153 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_recorder_batch.py - recorder 批量缓冲（A1）单元测试

覆盖：
  1. ops 按条数阈值批量 commit（未达阈值不落库）；
  2. ops 按超时秒数批量 commit；
  3. close() 会把积压 ops 全量落库；
  4. earnings（资金数据）立即 commit，不受缓冲影响；
  5. 缓冲满前 ops 对其他连接不可见；
  6. 显式 flush() 立即可见；
  7. 重复 close 安全；
  8. DB 异常时 log 不抛（except: pass 语义）。

运行：
    python -m pytest tests/test_recorder_batch.py -q
"""
import os
import sqlite3
import sys
import tempfile
import time

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from recorder import Recorder  # noqa: E402


def _mk(flush_every=50, flush_seconds=2.0):
    tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="rec_batch_")
    db = os.path.join(tmp, "test.db")
    r = Recorder(db)
    r._flush_every = flush_every
    r._flush_seconds = flush_seconds
    r._last_flush = time.time()
    return r, db


def _op_count(db):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(db)
    try:
        return conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ops").fetchone()[0]
    finally:
        conn.close()


def _earning_count(db):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(db)
    try:
        return conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM earnings").fetchone()[0]
    finally:
        conn.close()


def test_flush_by_count():
    """达条数阈值自动落库。"""
    r, db = _mk(flush_every=3, flush_seconds=99)
    r.log("a1")
    r.log("a2")
    assert _op_count(db) == 0, "未达阈值不应落库"
    r.log("a3")
    assert _op_count(db) == 3, "达阈值应落库 3 条"
    r.close()
    print("OK  条数触发批量 commit")


def test_flush_by_time():
    """超时秒数触发自动落库。"""
    r, db = _mk(flush_every=999, flush_seconds=0.3)
    r.log("t1")
    time.sleep(0.5)
    r.log("t2")
    assert _op_count(db) == 2, "超时应已落库"
    r.close()
    print("OK  时间触发批量 commit")


def test_close_flushes_all():
    """close 前积压的 ops 在 close 后全量可见。"""
    r, db = _mk(flush_every=999, flush_seconds=99)
    for i in range(7):
        r.log(f"c{i}")
    assert _op_count(db) == 0, "close 前不应落库"
    r.close()
    assert _op_count(db) == 7, "close 后应全量落库"
    print("OK  close 全量落库")


def test_earning_immediate():
    """earnings 立即 commit，不等批量。"""
    r, db = _mk(flush_every=999, flush_seconds=99)
    r.record_earning("测试App", "签到", 1.5, "元", "detail")
    assert _earning_count(db) == 1, "earnings 应立即可见"
    r.close()
    print("OK  earnings 立即落库")


def test_ops_buffered_invisible_before_threshold():
    """缓冲满前 ops 对其他连接不可见。"""
    r, db = _mk(flush_every=50, flush_seconds=99)
    r.log("b1")
    r.log("b2")
    assert _op_count(db) == 0, "未达阈值且未 flush 时 ops 不应可见"
    r.close()
    assert _op_count(db) == 2, "close 后应可见"
    print("OK  缓冲语义：阈值前不可见")


def test_flush_explicit():
    """显式 flush() 立即落库。"""
    r, db = _mk(flush_every=999, flush_seconds=99)
    r.log("f1")
    r.flush()
    assert _op_count(db) == 1, "flush 后应立即可见"
    r.close()
    print("OK  显式 flush 落库")


def test_double_close_safe():
    """重复 close 不抛异常、不重复落库。"""
    r, db = _mk(flush_every=999, flush_seconds=99)
    r.log("d1")
    r.close()
    r.close()
    assert _op_count(db) == 1
    print("OK  重复 close 安全")


def test_db_error_swallowed():
    """DB 写入失败时 log 不抛（except: pass 语义）。"""
    tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="rec_batch_")
    db = os.path.join(tmp, "test.db")
    r = Recorder(db)
    r.close()
    # 关闭后 conn 已失效，此时 log 不应抛异常
    r.log("should-not-crash")
    print("OK  DB 异常被吞，不抛")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("test_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_reward_pick.py`（126 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_reward_pick.py - Executor 收益数字提取的单元测试（无需真机）

背景（踩过的坑）：
    原实现是 `re.search(pattern, xml)` 直接在**整份 UI XML** 上取第一个匹配。
    页面上的活动横幅（如「新人 1 元提现」）、广告位数字都会被当成账户余额，
    于是收益表里记的是横幅数字，不是真实收益。

新语义：
    1. 只在 `text="..."` / `content-desc="..."` 属性值里找；
    2. 多处匹配时，金额类（unit=元）优先取带「提现/余额/钱包/现金」语义的文案；
    3. 配置里给了 `context` 关键词时，context 优先级最高；
    4. 属性里一处都没有时，退化为整份 XML 兜底（兼容跨标签的旧 pattern）。

运行：
    python tests/test_reward_pick.py
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from executor import Executor  # noqa: E402

AMOUNT_YUAN = r"(?P<amount>[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*(?P<unit>元)"


def _mk():
    return Executor.__new__(Executor)


def _xml(*texts):
    nodes = "".join(f'<node text="{t}" resource-id="x{i}"/>'
                    for i, t in enumerate(texts))
    return f"<hierarchy>{nodes}</hierarchy>"


def _pick(xml, pattern=AMOUNT_YUAN, **act):
    ex = _mk()
    cands = ex._reward_candidates(xml, pattern, act)
    return cands


def t_ignores_non_text_attributes():
    """resource-id / bounds 里的数字不该被当成收益。"""
    xml = ('<hierarchy><node text="首页" resource-id="tv_1yuan_balance" '
           'bounds="[0,0][1080,100]"/></hierarchy>')
    assert _pick(xml) == [], "不应从 resource-id 里抠出数字"
    print("OK  只在 text/content-desc 里找，忽略 resource-id")


def t_prefers_balance_semantics():
    """页面同时有横幅「新人专享1元」和「可提现0.32元」-> 取后者。"""
    xml = _xml("新人专享1元", "可提现0.32元", "看视频再得50金币")
    cands = _pick(xml, unit="元")
    assert cands, "应有匹配"
    assert cands[0][0] == "可提现0.32元", f"取错了: {cands[0][0]}"
    assert cands[0][1].group("amount") == "0.32"
    print("OK  多处匹配时优先取带「可提现/余额」语义的文案")


def t_banner_only_still_returns_something():
    """只有横幅数字时仍然返回（宁可给个数字也别静默丢），但只有一条候选。"""
    xml = _xml("新人专享1元")
    cands = _pick(xml, unit="元")
    assert len(cands) == 1
    assert cands[0][1].group("amount") == "1"
    print("OK  仅横幅匹配时仍返回单条候选（并在日志里标注）")


def t_context_keyword_wins():
    """配置给了 context 时，context 优先级高于内置语义表。"""
    xml = _xml("可提现0.32元", "账户余额5.00元")
    cands = _pick(xml, unit="元", context=["账户"])
    assert cands[0][0] == "账户余额5.00元", f"context 未生效: {cands[0][0]}"
    print("OK  context 关键词优先级最高")


def t_content_desc_also_scanned():
    xml = '<hierarchy><node content-desc="可提现金额 1.50 元"/></hierarchy>'
    cands = _pick(xml, unit="元")
    assert cands, "content-desc 也应被扫描"
    assert cands[0][1].group("amount") == "1.50"
    print("OK  content-desc 属性同样参与匹配")


def t_fallback_to_whole_xml():
    """属性里没有时退化为整份 XML（兼容跨标签 pattern）。"""
    xml = '<hierarchy><node text="签到"/><node text="3 金币"/></hierarchy>'
    pat = r"签到.*?(?P<amount>[0-9]+)\s*(?P<unit>金币)"
    cands = _pick(xml, pattern=pat)
    assert cands, "整份 XML 兜底应生效"
    assert cands[0][1].group("amount") == "3"
    print("OK  属性无匹配时退化为整份 XML 兜底")


def t_no_match_returns_empty():
    xml = _xml("首页", "我的")
    assert _pick(xml) == []
    print("OK  无匹配返回空列表")


def t_coin_unit_not_reordered():
    """非金额单位（金币）不做「余额语义」重排，保持文档顺序。"""
    xml = _xml("看视频得100金币", "签到得5金币")
    cands = _pick(xml, pattern=r"(?P<amount>[0-9]+)\s*(?P<unit>金币)",
                  unit="金币")
    assert cands[0][0] == "看视频得100金币", f"金币不该重排: {cands[0][0]}"
    print("OK  金币类不做余额语义重排，保持文档顺序")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_reward_pick_pure.py`（72 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_reward_pick_pure.py - reward_pick.rank_reward_candidates 纯函数直测（B8）

test_reward_pick.py 9 例走 executor 正则壳（零修改）保持集成语义；
本文件直接对抽出的纯函数做单元直测，钉住排序规则本身。

运行：
    python tests/test_reward_pick_pure.py
"""
import os
import re
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from reward_pick import rank_reward_candidates, _BALANCE_HINTS  # noqa: E402

PAT = re.compile(r"(?P<amount>[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*(?P<unit>元)")


def _out(*texts):
    """构造 [(来源文案, match), ...]：每个文案都匹配 PAT。"""
    return [(t, PAT.search(t)) for t in texts]


def t_context_wins_over_balance():
    """context 唯一前置：即便后面有带余额语义的文案，也取 context 命中项。"""
    out = _out("可提现0.32元", "账户余额5.00元")
    res = rank_reward_candidates(out, ["账户"], _BALANCE_HINTS, "元", "元")
    assert res[0][0] == "账户余额5.00元", res
    print("OK  context 关键词唯一前置（覆盖余额语义）")


def t_balance_hints_reorder_yuan():
    """unit==money_unit("元") 且命中余额 hints → 该候选前置。"""
    out = _out("看视频再得50金币", "可提现0.32元", "新人专享1元")
    res = rank_reward_candidates(out, [], _BALANCE_HINTS, "元", "元")
    assert res[0][0] == "可提现0.32元", res
    print("OK  金额类命中余额语义候选前置")


def t_coin_unit_keeps_order():
    """非金额单位不做余额语义重排，保持文档原序。"""
    out = _out("看视频得100金币", "签到得5金币")
    res = rank_reward_candidates(out, [], _BALANCE_HINTS, "元", "金币")
    assert res == out, res
    print("OK  金币类不重排，原序返回")


def t_single_or_empty_returns_as_is():
    """空/单候选直接原样返回，不做任何排序。"""
    assert rank_reward_candidates([], ["账户"], _BALANCE_HINTS, "元", "元") == []
    one = _out("可提现1.00元")
    assert rank_reward_candidates(one, ["账户"], _BALANCE_HINTS, "元", "元") == one
    print("OK  空/单候选原样返回")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_selfsearch_candidates.py`（122 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_selfsearch_candidates.py - 第1步：全局分类候选词表（AUTOP-SELFSEARCH-2026-10-20）

数据源 = 派单卡 P3 按钮文案族 + dispatch/AUTOP-SELFSEARCH-2026-10-20/APP_PATHS.md
12 条用户描述。要求：
  * APP_PATHS 中每个按钮文案必须被四张表之一覆盖（未覆盖即红）；
  * App 级 extra_candidates 追加/去重/保序（config/apps/*.yaml 可微调）；
  * config_loader.extra_candidates 只读读取（含别名），不破坏既有加载行为。

运行：
    python -m pytest tests/test_selfsearch_candidates.py -q
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

import candidates  # noqa: E402
import config_loader  # noqa: E402

# APP_PATHS.md 12 条描述中的按钮文案（提现组暂略，但仍入表，供后续提现行为启用）
APP_WORDS = [
    # 01 趣头条
    "我的", "任务", "今日签到", "提现兑换", "立即提现",
    # 02 拼多多
    "多多视频", "提现", "签到",
    # 03 京东
    "逛", "连续签到", "去提现",
    # 04 美团
    "视频", "今日签到", "提现",
    # 05 快手
    "任务中心", "签到",
    # 06 百度地图
    "首页", "我的", "现金签到",
    # 07 海马畅听
    "我的", "领现金", "签到",
    # 08 UC浏览器极速版
    "任务", "签到领元宝",
    # 09 飞猪旅行
    "能量红包", "里程签到",
    # 10 米读小说
    "任务", "我的", "福利中心", "立即签到",
    # 11 米读极速版
    "我的", "福利", "立即签到",
    # 13 大众点评
    "签到", "得金币", "立即签到",
]


def _all_words():
    return (candidates.SIGN + candidates.TASK_CENTER + candidates.NAV_TAB
            + candidates.WITHDRAW)


def t_tables_exist_and_nonempty():
    for name in ("SIGN", "TASK_CENTER", "NAV_TAB", "WITHDRAW"):
        words = getattr(candidates, name)
        assert isinstance(words, list) and words, f"{name} 表缺失或为空"
    print("OK  四张表存在且非空")


def t_app_paths_words_covered():
    """APP_PATHS 每个按钮文案必须被四张表之一覆盖（未覆盖即红）。"""
    allw = set(_all_words())
    miss = [w for w in APP_WORDS if w not in allw]
    assert not miss, f"APP_PATHS 文案未覆盖: {miss}"
    print(f"OK  APP_PATHS 共 {len(set(APP_WORDS))} 个独立文案全部覆盖")


def t_candidates_for_returns_global_without_app():
    got = candidates.candidates_for("sign")
    assert got == candidates.SIGN, "无 app_cfg 时应原样返回全局表"
    print("OK  candidates_for 无 App 配置返回全局表")


def t_candidates_for_appends_extra_and_dedup():
    app_cfg = {"extra_candidates": {"sign": ["独家签到", "今日签到"]}}
    got = candidates.candidates_for("sign", app_cfg)
    assert "独家签到" in got, "App 特有词应追加"
    assert got.count("今日签到") == 1, "与全局重复的词不应重复出现"
    assert got.index("今日签到") == got.index(candidates.SIGN[0]), "保序：全局在前"
    print("OK  candidates_for 追加 App 特有词且去重保序")


def t_unknown_category_raises():
    try:
        candidates.candidates_for("bogus")
    except ValueError:
        return
    raise AssertionError("未知词表应抛 ValueError")


def t_loader_reads_app_extra_candidates():
    cfg = {"apps": [
        {"name": "拼多多", "extra_candidates": {"sign": ["独家签到"]}},
        {"name": "京东"},
    ]}
    assert config_loader.extra_candidates(cfg, "拼多多") == {"sign": ["独家签到"]}
    assert config_loader.extra_candidates(cfg, "京东") == {}
    assert config_loader.extra_candidates(cfg, "不存在") == {}
    # 别名命中
    cfg2 = {"apps": [{"name": "趣头条", "aliases": ["趣看天下"],
                      "extra_candidates": {"nav_tab": ["逛逛"]}}]}
    assert config_loader.extra_candidates(cfg2, "趣看天下") == {"nav_tab": ["逛逛"]}
    print("OK  config_loader.extra_candidates 只读读取（含别名）")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_selfsearch_inject.py`（166 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_selfsearch_inject.py - 第5步：click_first 自搜寻注入（AUTOP-SELFSEARCH-2026-10-20）

用户原话：「使程序自己有一套思路，不用依赖外部描述而能自己搜寻」。
第5步把全局词表接进引擎执行路径：

  * `candidates_from: sign|task_center|nav_tab|withdraw`：
    显式 candidates 优先（精确定向），全局词表（+ App 级 extra_candidates）
    按序追加兜底（自搜寻）。candidates 可省略——纯词表即为纯自搜寻；
  * `zone: tab_zone|top_right|right_edge`：
    click_first 定位透传位置先验（多匹配时底部带/右上/右缘加分）。

规则：
  * 显式候选与词表重复 -> 去重（词表不重复注入同一 (kind,value)）；
  * 词表注入同样走知识库过滤（disabled 候选跳过）；
  * 未知 candidates_from 分类 -> WARN 忽略，不中断；
  * zone 只透传不改变无 zone 的既有行为（默认 None）。

运行：
    python -m pytest tests/test_selfsearch_inject.py -q
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from executor import Executor, LocateResult  # noqa: E402


class _Rec:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = []

    def log(self, event, app="", level="INFO"):
        self.lines.append(f"[{level}] {event}")


class _Human:
    def delay(self, *a):
        pass

    def maybe_wander(self, *a):
        pass

    def think(self):
        pass

    def natural_swipe(self, d):
        pass


class _HitList:
    """按 value 命中的 _locate 桩：记录收到的 (kind, value, zone)。"""

    def __init__(self, hits):
        self.hits = set(hits)      # 命中的 value 集合
        self.calls = []            # (kind, value, zone)

    def __call__(self, k, v, timeout=2.0, zone=None, screen_w=0):
        self.calls.append((k, v, zone))
        if v in self.hits:
            return LocateResult(True, {"kind": k, "value": v}, "测试命中")
        return LocateResult(False, None, "测试未命中")


def _mk(script):
    ex = Executor("fake-serial", _Human(), _Rec(), dry_run=True, config={})
    ex.dry_run = False
    ex._locate = script
    ex._kb_candidates = lambda app, primary: ([primary], False)
    ex._kb_mark_fail = lambda app, prims: None
    ex._click_obj = lambda obj, relocate=None: None
    ex._app_extras = lambda app: {}
    return ex


def t_candidates_from_falls_back_to_global():
    """显式候选未命中 -> 全局词表兜底命中（自搜寻）。"""
    # 显式「今日签到」不在屏上，全局表里的「立即签到」在屏上
    script = _HitList({"立即签到"})
    ex = _mk(script)
    act = {"type": "click_first",
           "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "今日签到"}],
           "candidates_from": "sign"}
    assert ex._step_click_first(act, "T") is True
    values = [c[1] for c in script.calls]
    assert "今日签到" in values and "立即签到" in values, values
    print("OK  显式未命中后全局词表兜底命中")


def t_candidates_from_pure_selfsearch():
    """无显式 candidates：纯 candidates_from 即为自搜寻。"""
    script = _HitList({"签到领元宝"})
    ex = _mk(script)
    act = {"type": "click_first", "candidates_from": "sign"}
    assert ex._step_click_first(act, "T") is True
    assert ("text", "签到领元宝") in [(c[0], c[1]) for c in script.calls]
    print("OK  纯 candidates_from 自搜寻命中")


def t_explicit_wins_before_global_dup_dedup():
    """显式候选在词表之前被尝试；重复词不二次注入。"""
    script = _HitList({"今日签到"})
    ex = _mk(script)
    act = {"type": "click_first",
           "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "今日签到"}],
           "candidates_from": "sign"}
    assert ex._step_click_first(act, "T") is True
    # 首轮即命中：不应再去试词表里的「今日签到」
    assert script.calls == [("text", "今日签到", None)], script.calls
    print("OK  显式优先且重复词去重")


def t_unknown_category_warn_and_ignore():
    """未知 candidates_from -> WARN 忽略，仍走显式候选。"""
    script = _HitList({"今日签到"})
    ex = _mk(script)
    act = {"type": "click_first",
           "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "今日签到"}],
           "candidates_from": "bogus"}
    assert ex._step_click_first(act, "T") is True
    assert any("未知 candidates_from" in l for l in ex.recorder.lines), \
        ex.recorder.lines
    print("OK  未知分类 WARN 忽略不中断")


def t_app_extra_candidates_injected():
    """App 级 extra_candidates 参与词表注入（不重复已有全局词）。"""
    script = _HitList({"独家签到"})
    ex = _mk(script)
    ex._app_extras = lambda app: {"sign": ["独家签到", "今日签到"]}
    act = {"type": "click_first", "candidates_from": "sign"}
    assert ex._step_click_first(act, "T") is True
    values = [c[1] for c in script.calls]
    assert "独家签到" in values, values
    assert values.count("今日签到") == 1, "全局+extra 重复词应去重"
    print("OK  App 级 extra_candidates 注入且去重")


def t_zone_passed_to_locate():
    """zone 透传：click_first 定位带 zone（tab_zone 底部带优先）。"""
    script = _HitList({"任务"})
    ex = _mk(script)
    act = {"type": "click_first",
           "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "任务"}],
           "zone": "tab_zone"}
    assert ex._step_click_first(act, "T") is True
    assert script.calls == [("text", "任务", "tab_zone")], script.calls
    print("OK  zone 透传定位")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_sign_evidence.py`（106 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_sign_evidence.py - 「签到按钮确实被点过了吗」判定规则（2026-10-25）

用户定的口径：**只有复核过签到按钮确实被点过了，才认为已经成功签到。**

本测试锁死三件事：
  1. 认可的三类点击凭据能判出来（多候选 / 点击文本 / 视觉兜底命中）
  2. 一堆看着像成功、其实没有点击凭据的信号，一律判「未确认」
  3. 盲点（点击坐标）和导航类入口（任务/赚钱/任务中心）不算签到凭据

运行：
    ./.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest tests/test_sign_evidence.py -q
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from sign_evidence import signed_evidence, SIGN_WORDS, SIGN_EVIDENCE_RULE  # noqa: E402


# ---------- 1. 认可的点击凭据 ----------

def test_multicandidate_hit_on_qiandao():
    log = ("[13:57:51] [INFO] [趣头条] 已启动 趣头条\n"
           "[13:57:51] [INFO] [趣头条] 多候选命中并点击: (text)今日签到\n")
    ok, line, kind = signed_evidence(log)
    assert ok is True
    assert "今日签到" in line
    assert kind == "多候选命中"


def test_click_text_hit():
    log = "[14:13:12] [INFO] [梧桐免费小说] 点击文本: 签到\n"
    ok, line, kind = signed_evidence(log)
    assert ok is True and kind == "文本命中" and "签到" in line


def test_vision_hit_with_desc():
    # click_point 带 desc 时，视觉兜底日志里带得出人类语义
    log = ("[12:00:00] [INFO] [悟空浏览器] 视觉兜底点击成功: "
           "click_point[签到弹窗『立即领取』] -> (812,1463)\n")
    ok, line, kind = signed_evidence(log)
    assert ok is True and kind == "视觉兜底命中"
    assert "立即领取" in line


def test_all_sign_words_are_recognised():
    for w in SIGN_WORDS:
        ok, _, _ = signed_evidence(f"[t] 点击文本: {w}\n")
        assert ok is True, f"词表里的「{w}」应算签到凭据"


# ---------- 2. 没有点击凭据的信号一律未确认 ----------

def test_clean_run_without_click_is_not_success():
    """跑得干干净净、零失败信号，但没有点过签到按钮 → 仍是未确认。"""
    log = ("[t] [INFO] [XX] 已启动 XX\n"
           "[t] [INFO] [XX] 弹窗清扫: 屏幕干净，无需处理\n"
           "[t] [INFO] [XX] 行为[sign] 执行完成\n")
    ok, line, kind = signed_evidence(log)
    assert ok is False and line is None and kind is None


def test_gold_counter_is_not_evidence():
    log = "[t] [INFO] [XX] 金币收益: 3000（当前余额 12800）\n"
    assert signed_evidence(log)[0] is False


def test_no_failure_text_is_not_evidence():
    """没有「步骤失败」字样 ≠ 点过签到按钮。"""
    log = "[t] [INFO] [XX] 行为[sign] 执行完成（无失败步骤）\n"
    assert signed_evidence(log)[0] is False


def test_step_fail_and_miss_are_not_evidence():
    log = ("[t] [WARN] [XX] 步骤失败: 所有候选均未出现: "
           "[{'type': 'text', 'value': '签到'}]\n"
           "[t] [WARN] [XX] 视觉兜底全部未命中，放弃该步: click_point[visual]\n")
    assert signed_evidence(log)[0] is False


def test_empty_log():
    assert signed_evidence("") == (False, None, None)
    assert signed_evidence(None) == (False, None, None)


# ---------- 3. 盲点与导航入口不算凭据 ----------

def test_blind_coordinate_click_is_not_evidence():
    """click_point 的盲点兜底只留「点击坐标: (x,y)」——不能当签到凭据。"""
    log = "[t] [INFO] [XX] 点击坐标: (540,2141)\n"
    assert signed_evidence(log)[0] is False


def test_nav_entry_is_not_sign_button():
    """任务 / 任务中心 / 赚钱 / 多多视频 只是进门，不是签到按钮。"""
    for nav in ("任务", "任务中心", "赚钱", "多多视频", "我的", "首页"):
        log = f"[t] [INFO] [XX] 多候选命中并点击: (text){nav}\n"
        ok, _, _ = signed_evidence(log)
        assert ok is False, f"导航入口「{nav}」不应算签到凭据"


def test_rule_text_mentions_click_verification():
    assert "点过" in SIGN_EVIDENCE_RULE
```

### `tests/test_stability_agg.py`（132 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_stability_agg.py - 多轮稳定性聚合口径的单元测试（无需真机）

为什么必须测这个：
    `aggregate()` 的输出就是**头条指标**（总命中率 / 稳定命中数）。
    如果它的口径写错（例如把"作废"轮次算进分母、或把 `导航可救` 漏算成未命中），
    整轮结论都会错，而且是**看起来合理**的错。这类代码必须被测住。

口径（与 `ROUND3_FINDINGS.md` / `STABILITY.md` 必须一致）：
    命中   = 首屏命中 / 导航后命中 / 导航可救(建议加前置步)
    未命中 = 首屏未命中 / 导航后仍未命中 / 导航后也未命中(不可救)
    作废   = 数据作废(...) / 异常 / 无锚点动作   -> **不进分母**

运行：
    python tests/test_stability_agg.py
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(ROOT, "devtools"))

from stability_run import (  # noqa: E402
    aggregate, classify, is_hit, is_void, HIT_STATUS,
)


def t_hit_status_set_is_exact():
    """命中口径就是这三个状态，不多不少。"""
    assert HIT_STATUS == {"首屏命中", "导航后命中", "导航可救(建议加前置步)"}
    assert is_hit("首屏命中") and is_hit("导航后命中")
    assert is_hit("导航可救(建议加前置步)")
    assert not is_hit("首屏未命中")
    assert not is_hit("导航后仍未命中")
    assert not is_hit("导航后也未命中(不可救)")
    print("OK  命中口径 = 首屏命中/导航后命中/导航可救")


def t_void_status_set():
    assert is_void("数据作废(页面非目标App)")
    assert is_void("数据作废(前台非目标App)")
    assert is_void("异常")
    assert is_void("无锚点动作")
    assert not is_void("首屏命中")
    assert not is_void("导航后也未命中(不可救)")
    print("OK  作废口径 = 数据作废(...)/异常/无锚点动作")


def t_void_rounds_excluded_from_denominator():
    """作废轮次不进分母：2 命中 + 1 作废 -> 2/2，不是 2/3。"""
    acc = {("A", "sign"): ["首屏命中", "数据作废(页面非目标App)", "导航后命中"]}
    per, tot = aggregate(acc)
    row = per[0]
    assert row["valid"] == 2, row
    assert row["void"] == 1, row
    assert row["hits"] == 2, row
    assert row["rate"] == 1.0, row
    assert tot == {"hits": 2, "valid": 2, "void": 1}, tot
    print("OK  作废轮次不进分母（2/2 而非 2/3）")


def t_all_void_gives_none_rate():
    acc = {("A", "sign"): ["数据作废(页面非目标App)"] * 3}
    per, tot = aggregate(acc)
    assert per[0]["valid"] == 0
    assert per[0]["rate"] is None, "全作废时不应给出命中率"
    assert tot["valid"] == 0 and tot["hits"] == 0
    print("OK  全作废时 rate=None，不污染总命中率")


def t_total_rate_math():
    """2/2 + 0/2 -> 2/4 = 50%。"""
    acc = {
        ("A", "sign"): ["首屏命中", "导航后命中"],
        ("B", "sign"): ["首屏未命中", "导航后也未命中(不可救)"],
    }
    per, tot = aggregate(acc)
    assert tot["hits"] == 2 and tot["valid"] == 4, tot
    print("OK  总命中率 = 总命中/总有效 = 2/4")


def t_classify_three_buckets():
    acc = {
        ("A", "sign"): ["首屏命中", "首屏命中", "首屏命中"],          # 稳定
        ("B", "sign"): ["首屏命中", "首屏未命中", "导航后命中"],        # 波动
        ("C", "sign"): ["首屏未命中", "首屏未命中", "导航后也未命中(不可救)"],  # 从未
        ("D", "sign"): ["数据作废(页面非目标App)"] * 2,              # 全作废 -> 不进任何类
    }
    per, _ = aggregate(acc)
    stable, flaky, never = classify(per)
    assert [x["app"] for x in stable] == ["A"], stable
    assert [x["app"] for x in flaky] == ["B"], flaky
    assert [x["app"] for x in never] == ["C"], never
    assert len(stable) + len(flaky) + len(never) == 3, "全作废的行为不应进任何类"
    print("OK  classify 三分：稳定/波动/从未（全作废不进任何类）")


def t_order_is_sorted_and_statuses_preserved():
    acc = {
        ("B", "watch"): ["首屏命中"],
        ("A", "sign"): ["首屏命中"],
    }
    per, _ = aggregate(acc)
    assert [x["app"] for x in per] == ["A", "B"], per
    assert per[1]["statuses"] == ["首屏命中"]
    print("OK  输出按 (app, behavior) 排序且保留各轮状态")


def t_void_counted_per_row():
    acc = {
        ("A", "sign"): ["首屏命中", "异常"],
        ("B", "sign"): ["数据作废(页面非目标App)"],
    }
    _, tot = aggregate(acc)
    assert tot["void"] == 2, tot
    print("OK  作废轮次总数跨行为累加正确")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_step_model.py`（410 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_step_model.py - v1/v2 编译层回归钉（v6 · S1）

锁定四件事：
  ① v1 旧 type 与 v2 新写法编译出**语义等价**的 Step（project 投影相等）；
  ② 四类非法组合必须抛 ValueError（不许猜、不许静默忽略）；
  ③ 坐标**不许取整** —— click_point 的 x/y 是 0~1 比例值，
     `int(0.9)` = 0 会把"图像锚失败时兜底点 (0.9,0.138)"变成"点屏幕左上角"；
  ④ **34 份真配置 389 个顶层步骤全部编译通过**（这是最强的一条，
     合成用例永远测不出"某个 type 忘了登记映射"）。

运行：
    ./.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest tests/test_step_model.py -q
"""
import glob
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from step_model import (compile_step, compile_actions, project, lint,
                        Step, Target, LEGACY_TO_V2)  # noqa: E402


# ---------- ① v1/v2 语义等价 ----------

def t_click_text_equals_click_by_text():
    a = compile_step({"type": "click_text", "text": "签到"})
    b = compile_step({"action": "click", "by": "text", "text": "签到"})
    assert a.action == b.action == "click"
    assert a.target.by == b.target.by == "text"
    assert a.target.value == b.target.value == "签到"
    assert project(a) == project(b), (project(a), project(b))
    print("OK  click_text == click+by:text")


def t_click_id_and_desc():
    for legacy, field, by in (("click_id", "res_id", "id"),
                              ("click_desc", "desc", "desc")):
        a = compile_step({"type": legacy, field: "v"})
        b = compile_step({"action": "click", "by": by, field: "v"})
        assert a.target.by == b.target.by == by
        assert a.target.value == b.target.value == "v"
        assert project(a) == project(b), (legacy, project(a), project(b))
    print("OK  click_id/click_desc == click+by:id/desc")


def t_click_point_no_anchor_is_tap():
    s = compile_step({"type": "click_point", "x": 0.5, "y": 0.641})
    assert s.action == "tap"
    assert s.target is None
    assert s.point == (0.5, 0.641)
    print("OK  无锚点 click_point -> tap + point")


def t_click_point_image_keeps_float_fallback():
    """有图像锚 + 坐标 -> click + by:image + fallback **保留比例值**。

    这条钉的是"不许取整"：写成 int(0.9) 会得到 (0,0)，
    兜底点击落到屏幕左上角，比不兜底更糟（会误触广告位）。
    """
    s = compile_step({"type": "click_point", "x": 0.9, "y": 0.138,
                      "images": ["ref.png"]})
    assert s.action == "click"
    assert s.target.by == "image"
    assert s.target.value == "ref.png"
    assert s.target.fallback == ((0.9, 0.138),), s.target.fallback
    print(f"OK  图像锚 -> click + fallback={s.target.fallback}（未取整）")


def t_click_point_image_without_xy_has_no_fallback():
    """只写 images 不写坐标（实测 53 个）-> 合法，fallback 为空。"""
    s = compile_step({"type": "click_point",
                      "images": ["ref.png"], "optional": True})
    assert s.action == "click" and s.target.by == "image"
    assert s.target.fallback == ()
    print("OK  只有 images 无坐标 -> click，无 fallback")


def t_click_point_with_vlm_is_click_with_vision():
    s = compile_step({"type": "click_point", "x": 0.9, "y": 0.138,
                      "vlm_prompt": "定位『我的』"})
    assert s.action == "click" and s.target.by == "vision"
    assert s.target.value == "定位『我的』"
    print("OK  vlm_prompt -> click + by:vision")


def t_any_of_is_first_ok():
    s = compile_step({"type": "any_of", "optional": True,
                      "choices": [[{"type": "click_text", "text": "A"}],
                                  [{"type": "click_text", "text": "B"}]]})
    assert s.flow == "first_ok"
    assert len(s.branches) == 2
    assert s.branches[0][0].action == "click"
    print("OK  any_of -> flow:first_ok + 2 branches")


def t_random_is_pick_one():
    s = compile_step({"type": "random", "choices": [[{"type": "think"}]]})
    assert s.flow == "pick_one"
    print("OK  random -> flow:pick_one")


def t_back_home_are_press():
    b = compile_step({"type": "back"})
    h = compile_step({"type": "home"})
    assert (b.action, b.key) == ("press", "back")
    assert (h.action, h.key) == ("press", "home")
    print("OK  back/home -> press+key")


def t_click_first_is_flow_not_click():
    """click_first 语义是"多候选依次试、第一个成功即停"，不是单个 click。"""
    s = compile_step({"type": "click_first",
                      "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "A"},
                                     {"type": "text", "value": "B"}]})
    assert s.flow == "first_ok"
    assert len(s.branches) == 2
    print("OK  click_first -> flow:first_ok + 2 分支")


def t_click_first_pool_from_is_legal():
    """不写静态 candidates、靠 candidates_from 词池动态取候选是合法写法。"""
    s = compile_step({"type": "click_first", "candidates_from": "sign",
                      "optional": True})
    assert s.flow == "first_ok"
    assert s.branches == ()
    assert s.pool_from == "sign"
    assert not [i for i in lint([s]) if i["level"] == "ERROR"], lint([s])
    print("OK  candidates_from 词池写法不报 ERROR")


def t_ensure_preserved_as_legacy():
    """ensure 暂未折叠成三维度：保留 legacy_type，执行层仍走 _step_ensure。"""
    s = compile_step({"type": "ensure", "text": "已签到"})
    assert s.legacy_type == "ensure"
    assert s.action is None and s.flow is None and s.macro is None
    print("OK  ensure 保留 legacy_type（S2 再折叠）")


# ---------- ② 必须抛错 ----------

def t_type_and_action_conflict():
    try:
        compile_step({"type": "click_text", "action": "tap", "x": 0.5, "y": 0.5})
    except ValueError as e:
        assert "冲突" in str(e), e
        return
    raise AssertionError("type 与 action 冲突应抛")


def t_type_and_action_equivalent_is_ok():
    """等价写法（type: click_text + action: click）允许，不算冲突。"""
    s = compile_step({"type": "click_text", "action": "click", "text": "X"})
    assert s.action == "click" and s.target.by == "text"
    print("OK  type/action 等价写法放行")


def t_click_missing_target():
    for bad in ({"action": "click"}, {"type": "click"}):
        try:
            compile_step(bad)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        raise AssertionError(f"click 缺定位字段应抛: {bad}")
    print("OK  click 缺定位字段抛错")


def t_multiple_primary_keys():
    try:
        compile_step({"action": "tap", "flow": "first_ok", "x": 0, "y": 0})
    except ValueError:
        return
    raise AssertionError("多主键应抛")


def t_unknown_type():
    try:
        compile_step({"type": "bogus_action"})
    except ValueError as e:
        assert "未知 type" in str(e), e
        return
    raise AssertionError("未知 type 应抛")


def t_unknown_flow_and_macro():
    for bad in ({"flow": "nope"}, {"macro": "nope"}):
        try:
            compile_step(bad)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        raise AssertionError(f"未知主键值应抛: {bad}")
    print("OK  未知 flow/macro 抛错")


def t_press_requires_valid_key():
    try:
        compile_step({"action": "press", "key": "volume_up"})
    except ValueError:
        return
    raise AssertionError("press 非法 key 应抛")


# ---------- ③ 语义投影 ----------

def t_project_equivalence_v1_v2():
    pairs = [
        ({"type": "click_text", "text": "X"},
         {"action": "click", "by": "text", "text": "X"}),
        ({"type": "back"}, {"action": "press", "key": "back"}),
        ({"type": "click_point", "x": 0.3, "y": 0.4},
         {"action": "tap", "x": 0.3, "y": 0.4}),
    ]
    for a, b in pairs:
        pa, pb = project(compile_step(a)), project(compile_step(b))
        assert pa == pb, (a, b, pa, pb)
    print("OK  语义投影 v1/v2 一致")


def t_project_category_matches_behavior_runner():
    """project 的分类必须与 behavior_runner.ACTION_CATEGORY 同源。

    唯一的例外是 v6 **有意重分类**的那几个 —— 必须逐条列出来，
    不许用 `continue` 悄悄跳过，否则"重分类"和"写错了"就分不清了：

      click_first  v5 分类 click（返回值是 bool，像单次点击）
                   v6 分类 flow（结构上是"多候选依次试"，归 flow 才对）
      click_text/id/desc  是 click 的别名，折叠后同为 click
      swipe_natural       是 swipe 的别名
    """
    import behavior_runner as br

    RECLASSIFIED = {"click_first": "flow"}     # v5 分类 -> v6 分类
    ALIASES = {"click_text", "click_id", "click_desc", "swipe_natural"}

    checked = 0
    for t, v5_cat in br.ACTION_CATEGORY.items():
        if t in ALIASES:
            continue
        if t == "click":
            step = compile_step({"action": "click", "text": "X"})
        elif t == "click_point":
            step = compile_step({"action": "tap", "x": 0.1, "y": 0.2})
        else:
            try:
                step = compile_step({"type": t})
            except ValueError:
                continue
        want = RECLASSIFIED.get(t, v5_cat)
        got = project(step)[0]
        assert got == want, f"{t}: 期望 {want}（v5={v5_cat}），实际 {got}"
        checked += 1
    assert checked >= 15, f"只校验了 {checked} 个 type，覆盖不足"
    print(f"OK  project 分类与 ACTION_CATEGORY 同源（{checked} 个，"
          f"click_first 已按 v6 重分类为 flow）")


def t_compile_actions_keeps_variant_levels():
    """多候选模板 [[..],[..]] 编译后保持两级结构。"""
    out = compile_actions([[{"type": "click_text", "text": "A"}],
                           [{"type": "click_text", "text": "B"}]])
    assert len(out) == 2 and isinstance(out[0], list)
    assert out[0][0].action == "click"
    print("OK  多候选模板保持两级结构")


# ---------- ④ 真配置全量编译（最强的一条） ----------

def t_all_app_configs_compile():
    """34 份 App 配置的 **389 个顶层步骤**全部编译通过，且 lint 无 ERROR。

    合成用例测不出"某个 type 忘了登记 LEGACY_TO_V2"这类漏项 ——
    只有拿真配置全量跑一遍才行。实测基线：389 步 / 0 失败 / 112 WARN。
    """
    from miniyaml import safe_load
    total = 0
    warns = 0
    errors = []
    legacy = {}
    for p in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(ROOT, "config", "apps", "*.yaml"))):
        fn = os.path.basename(p)
        with open(p, encoding="utf-8") as f:
            d = safe_load(f.read())
        for rname, steps in (d.get("behaviors") or {}).items():
            try:
                cs = compile_actions(steps)
            except ValueError as e:
                errors.append(f"{fn}/{rname}: {e}")
                continue
            total += sum(len(x) if isinstance(x, list) else 1 for x in cs)
            for s in cs:
                if isinstance(s, Step) and s.legacy_type:
                    legacy[s.legacy_type] = legacy.get(s.legacy_type, 0) + 1
            for i in lint(cs, fn, rname):
                if i["level"] == "ERROR":
                    errors.append(f"{fn}/{rname}: {i['msg']}")
                elif i["level"] == "WARN":
                    warns += 1
    assert not errors, errors
    assert total == 389, f"顶层步骤数应为 389，实际 {total}"
    assert warns == 112, f"死 optional WARN 应为 112，实际 {warns}"
    assert legacy == {"ensure": 1, "scroll_to": 1}, legacy
    print(f"OK  34 份配置 {total} 步全编译，0 ERROR，{warns} WARN")


def t_no_step_returns_none_contract_violation():
    """编译产物不得改变原语返回值语义：on_miss 只是块级标注，不是返回值。"""
    s = compile_step({"type": "click_first", "optional": True,
                      "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "A"}]})
    # click_first 作为 any_of 分支时必须能在全 miss 时返回 False，
    # 所以它的 on_miss 描述的是"整块可跳过"，不改变原语布尔值。
    assert s.on_miss == "skip"
    assert s.raw.get("optional") is True
    print("OK  on_miss 为块级标注，不改原语返回值")


# ---------- ⑤ 接入执行层后的行为（防止 _validate_step 是摆设） ----------

class _Rec:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = []

    def log(self, msg, app=None, level="INFO"):
        self.lines.append(f"[{level}] {msg}")


def _executor_with(rec):
    """最小 BehaviorRunner 替身：只需要 recorder 与 human。"""
    from behavior_runner import BehaviorRunner
    ex = BehaviorRunner.__new__(BehaviorRunner)
    ex.recorder = rec
    ex.dry_run = True
    ex.d = None
    ex._last_fail = ""
    return ex


def t_validate_step_logs_but_does_not_raise():
    """非法 step 只记 WARN，不抛 —— 抛了会让整条链中断。"""
    from behavior_runner import BehaviorRunner
    rec = _Rec()
    ex = _executor_with(rec)
    BehaviorRunner._validate_step(ex, {"type": "bogus_action"}, "T")
    assert any("编译校验失败" in ln for ln in rec.lines), rec.lines
    print("OK  非法 step 记 WARN 不抛")


def t_validate_step_silent_for_good_step():
    """合法 step 不产生任何日志（否则每次运行都刷屏）。"""
    from behavior_runner import BehaviorRunner
    rec = _Rec()
    ex = _executor_with(rec)
    BehaviorRunner._validate_step(ex, {"type": "click_text", "text": "签到"}, "T")
    assert not rec.lines, rec.lines
    print("OK  合法 step 不记日志")


def t_run_kind_accepts_step_and_uses_raw():
    """_run_kind 收到 Step 时，执行用的是 step.raw —— 而不是从 Step 反推。"""
    from behavior_runner import BehaviorRunner
    rec = _Rec()
    ex = _executor_with(rec)
    calls = []

    def fake_wait(raw, app):
        calls.append(raw)
        return True

    ex._step_wait = fake_wait
    act = {"type": "wait", "seconds": 3}
    step = compile_step(act)
    assert isinstance(step, Step)
    assert BehaviorRunner._run_kind(ex, step, "T") is True
    assert calls and calls[0] is act, "必须把原始 dict 交给原语，不能传 Step"
    print("OK  _run_kind 接受 Step 但执行用 raw")


def t_run_actions_never_skips_on_compile_error():
    """编译失败也必须继续执行下一步 —— 这是"零行为变化"的底线。

    方案原文在编译失败时 `continue`，会让 40+ 个 click 步骤静默不执行。
    这里用一个必然编译失败的 type 验证：它仍然会被送到 _exec_step。
    """
    from behavior_runner import BehaviorRunner
    rec = _Rec()
    ex = _executor_with(rec)
    seen = []
    ex._exec_step = lambda act, app: (seen.append(act), True)[1]
    BehaviorRunner._run_actions(ex, [{"type": "bogus_action"},
                               {"type": "wait", "seconds": 1}], "T", "sign")
    assert len(seen) == 2, f"两步都应被执行，实际 {len(seen)}"
    assert any("编译校验失败" in ln for ln in rec.lines), rec.lines
    print("OK  编译失败不跳过执行（2 步都跑了）")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_to_any_of.py`（158 行）

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""to_any_of 转换器单测。

覆盖两条转换路径：
  * convert_one  —— 二级（首屏直连 / 我的）
  * convert_deep —— 三级（首屏直连 / 我的 / 我的→任务中心）
以及幂等性（已是 any_of 不再转换）。
"""
import os
import sys
import unittest

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
ROOT = os.path.dirname(HERE)
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(ROOT, "devtools"))

import to_any_of as T  # noqa: E402


ANCHOR = {
    "type": "click_first",
    "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"},
                   {"type": "text", "value": "每日签到"}],
    "timeout": 8,
    "optional": True,
}
TAIL = [{"type": "think"},
        {"type": "click_first", "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "领取"}],
         "timeout": 6, "optional": True}]


class TestConvertOne(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_plain_anchor_without_nav_is_skipped(self):
        acts = [dict(ANCHOR)] + [dict(x) for x in TAIL]
        new, ch, why = T.convert_one(acts)
        self.assertFalse(ch)
        self.assertIn("无前置步", why)

    def test_add_nav_creates_two_branches(self):
        acts = [dict(ANCHOR)] + [dict(x) for x in TAIL]
        new, ch, why = T.convert_one(acts, add_nav=True)
        self.assertTrue(ch)
        self.assertEqual(new[0]["type"], "any_of")
        self.assertEqual(len(new[0]["choices"]), 2)
        # 分支1：首屏直连（单个锚点，且必须非 optional 才能"报未命中"）
        self.assertEqual(len(new[0]["choices"][0]), 1)
        self.assertIs(new[0]["choices"][0][0]["optional"], False)
        # 分支2：导航 + think + 锚点
        b2 = new[0]["choices"][1]
        self.assertEqual(b2[0]["type"], "click_point")
        self.assertIn("最右侧", b2[0]["vlm_prompt"])
        self.assertEqual(b2[-1]["type"], "click_first")
        # 尾随步骤保留
        self.assertEqual(new[1:], TAIL)

    def test_existing_nav_becomes_branch2(self):
        nav = dict(T.NAV_POINT)
        acts = [nav, {"type": "think"}, dict(ANCHOR)] + [dict(x) for x in TAIL]
        new, ch, _ = T.convert_one(acts)
        self.assertTrue(ch)
        b2 = new[0]["choices"][1]
        self.assertEqual(len(b2), 3)          # 原前置步(2) + 锚点(1)
        self.assertEqual(b2[0]["type"], "click_point")

    def test_idempotent(self):
        acts = [dict(ANCHOR)]
        new1, ch1, _ = T.convert_one(acts, add_nav=True)
        new2, ch2, why2 = T.convert_one(new1, add_nav=True)
        self.assertTrue(ch1)
        self.assertFalse(ch2)
        self.assertIn("已是 any_of", why2)

    def test_optional_flag_inherited(self):
        acts = [{"type": "click_first", "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "x"}],
                 "timeout": 5, "optional": True}]
        new, ch, _ = T.convert_one(acts, add_nav=True)
        self.assertTrue(new[0]["optional"])
        acts2 = [{"type": "click_first", "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "x"}],
                  "timeout": 5, "optional": False}]
        new2, _, _ = T.convert_one(acts2, add_nav=True)
        self.assertFalse(new2[0]["optional"])

    def test_variant_structure_untouched(self):
        acts = [[dict(ANCHOR)], [dict(ANCHOR)]]
        new, ch, why = T.convert_one(acts)
        self.assertFalse(ch)
        self.assertIn("变体结构", why)


class TestConvertDeep(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_two_level_when_l2_hit_on_mine(self):
        """我的页本身命中 -> 只加一层导航。"""
        acts = [dict(ANCHOR)] + [dict(x) for x in TAIL]
        new, ch, why = T.convert_deep(acts, l2_label=None)
        self.assertTrue(ch)
        b2 = new[0]["choices"][1]
        self.assertEqual([a["type"] for a in b2],
                         ["click_point", "think", "click_first"])
        self.assertIn("二级导航", why)

    def test_three_level_with_l2_entry(self):
        """签到在三级页 -> 我的 → 任务中心 → 签到。"""
        acts = [dict(ANCHOR)] + [dict(x) for x in TAIL]
        new, ch, why = T.convert_deep(acts, l2_label="任务中心")
        self.assertTrue(ch)
        b2 = new[0]["choices"][1]
        self.assertEqual([a["type"] for a in b2],
                         ["click_point", "think", "click_first", "think",
                          "click_first"])
        # 实测到的入口文案排在候选第一位
        self.assertEqual(b2[2]["candidates"][0]["value"], "任务中心")
        self.assertIs(b2[2]["optional"], False)
        # 三级锚点即原锚点
        self.assertEqual(b2[-1]["candidates"][0]["value"], "签到")
        self.assertIn("我的→任务中心", why)

    def test_l2_candidates_deduplicated(self):
        acts = [dict(ANCHOR)]
        new, _, _ = T.convert_deep(acts, l2_label="福利")
        vals = [c["value"] for c in new[0]["choices"][1][2]["candidates"]]
        self.assertEqual(len(vals), len(set(vals)))
        self.assertEqual(vals[0], "福利")

    def test_deep_idempotent(self):
        acts = [dict(ANCHOR)]
        new1, ch1, _ = T.convert_deep(acts, l2_label="任务中心")
        _, ch2, why2 = T.convert_deep(new1, l2_label="任务中心")
        self.assertTrue(ch1)
        self.assertFalse(ch2)
        self.assertIn("已是 any_of", why2)

    def test_extra_steps_for_sidebar_entry(self):
        """抖音系：我的 → ≡更多(desc) → 金币。入口是 desc 不是 text。"""
        acts = [dict(ANCHOR)] + [dict(x) for x in TAIL]
        sidebar = [{"type": "click_desc", "desc": "更多",
                    "timeout": 6, "optional": False}]
        new, ch, why = T.convert_deep(acts, extra_steps=sidebar)
        self.assertTrue(ch)
        b2 = new[0]["choices"][1]
        self.assertEqual([a["type"] for a in b2],
                         ["click_point", "think", "click_desc", "think",
                          "click_first"])
        self.assertEqual(b2[2]["desc"], "更多")
        self.assertIn("自定义入口", why)

    def test_extra_steps_take_precedence_over_label(self):
        acts = [dict(ANCHOR)]
        sidebar = [{"type": "click_desc", "desc": "更多", "optional": False}]
        new, _, why = T.convert_deep(acts, l2_label="任务中心",
                                     extra_steps=sidebar)
        b2 = new[0]["choices"][1]
        self.assertEqual(b2[2]["type"], "click_desc")
        self.assertIn("自定义入口", why)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)
```

### `tests/test_top_level_parity.py`（92 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_top_level_parity.py - A4 _at_top_level 与 ui_page 判据对拍（无需真机）

背景：执行器与验证器必须用同一套「页面长什么样」的判据（度量与执行同口径）。
本文件把**同一份 XML**分别喂给：
  1. 真实 Executor._at_top_level（走 dump -> nodes_from_xml -> at_top_level_nodes）；
  2. ui_page.at_top_level_nodes(nodes_from_xml(xml), screen_h)；
断言两者输出一致，杜绝执行器与判据库悄悄分叉。

运行：
    python -m pytest tests/test_top_level_parity.py -q
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from executor import Executor  # noqa: E402
from ui_page import nodes_from_xml, at_top_level_nodes  # noqa: E402


# 顶层页：底部带 首页/推荐/我的 三个 tab；页面中部还有一个「我的」假命中
XML_TOP = (
    '<hierarchy rotation="0" width="1080" height="2340">'
    '<node index="0" text="" content-desc="" class="android.widget.FrameLayout" '
    'bounds="[0,0][1080,2340]">'
    '<node index="0" text="我的" content-desc="" class="android.widget.TextView" '
    'bounds="[0,627][200,700]"/>'
    '<node index="1" text="首页" content-desc="" class="android.widget.TextView" '
    'bounds="[0,2000][270,2200]"/>'
    '<node index="2" text="推荐" content-desc="" class="android.widget.TextView" '
    'bounds="[270,2000][540,2200]"/>'
    '<node index="3" text="我的" content-desc="" class="android.widget.TextView" '
    'bounds="[810,2000][1080,2200]"/>'
    '</node></hierarchy>'
)

# 二级页：底部只有一个「签到」，中部「我的」是假命中 -> 非顶层页
XML_SECONDARY = (
    '<hierarchy rotation="0" width="1080" height="2340">'
    '<node index="0" text="我的" content-desc="" class="android.widget.TextView" '
    'bounds="[0,627][200,700]"/>'
    '<node index="1" text="签到" content-desc="" class="android.widget.TextView" '
    'bounds="[0,2000][270,2200]"/>'
    '</hierarchy>'
)


class _Rec:
    def log(self, event, app="", level="INFO"):
        pass


def _executor_from(xml, size=(1080, 2340)):
    class _D:
        def window_size(self):
            return size

    ex = Executor.__new__(Executor)
    ex.d = _D()
    ex.recorder = _Rec()
    ex._dump_safe = lambda: xml
    return ex


def _ui_page_verdict(xml, size=(1080, 2340)):
    return at_top_level_nodes(nodes_from_xml(xml), size[1])


def test_parity_top_level_xml():
    """同一顶层页 XML：两个实现都必须判 True。"""
    ex_verdict = _executor_from(XML_TOP)._at_top_level()
    ui_verdict = _ui_page_verdict(XML_TOP)
    assert ex_verdict is True and ui_verdict is True, (ex_verdict, ui_verdict)
    print("OK  顶层页 XML 对拍一致（均 True）")


def test_parity_secondary_xml():
    """同一二级页 XML：两个实现都必须判 False（中部假命中不计）。"""
    ex_verdict = _executor_from(XML_SECONDARY)._at_top_level()
    ui_verdict = _ui_page_verdict(XML_SECONDARY)
    assert ex_verdict is False and ui_verdict is False, (ex_verdict, ui_verdict)
    print("OK  二级页 XML 对拍一致（均 False）")


def test_empty_xml_matches_empty_nodes():
    """空 XML：两个实现输出一致（均为 False）。"""
    ex_verdict = _executor_from("")._at_top_level()
    ui_verdict = _ui_page_verdict("")
    assert ex_verdict is False and ui_verdict is False, (ex_verdict, ui_verdict)
    print("OK  空 XML 对拍一致（均 False）")
```

### `tests/test_trajectory_captcha.py`（218 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
tests/test_trajectory_captcha.py - 轨迹曲线与滑块验证码模块单元测试

运行：python -m tests.test_trajectory_captcha
（从项目根目录运行，保证能 import human_trajectory / captcha_slider）
"""
import os
import sys
import math
import tempfile

# 让脚本能从项目根目录 import
_HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
_PROJ = os.path.dirname(_HERE)
if _PROJ not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _PROJ)

import numpy as np

from human_trajectory import HumanTrajectory


def _avg_intervals(intervals):
    """去掉首段后统计剩余间隔的离散度（验证非匀速）。"""
    if len(intervals) <= 3:
        return 0.0
    mid = intervals[2:-2]
    if not mid:
        return 0.0
    mean = sum(mid) / len(mid)
    if mean <= 0:
        return 0.0
    var = sum((v - mean) ** 2 for v in mid) / len(mid)
    return math.sqrt(var) / mean  # 变异系数


def test_gesture_basics():
    ht = HumanTrajectory({})
    points, intervals = ht.generate_gesture((100, 1000), (800, 1000))
    assert len(points) >= 2, "至少要两个点"
    assert points[-1] == (800, 1000), "exact_end 应精确落在终点"
    assert len(intervals) == len(points) - 1, "间隔数应等于点数-1"
    assert points[0] == (100, 1000), "起点应保持"
    print(f"[basics] ok, points={len(points)}, distance~700px")


def test_gesture_fitts_steps():
    """距离越远步数越多（Fitts 思想），且被上下限钳制。"""
    ht = HumanTrajectory({})
    p_short, _ = ht.generate_gesture((0, 500), (100, 500))
    p_long, _ = ht.generate_gesture((0, 500), (2000, 500))
    assert len(p_short) < len(p_long), "远距离应有更多采样点"
    # 超长距离被 steps_max 钳制
    p_huge, _ = ht.generate_gesture((0, 0), (20000, 0))
    assert len(p_huge) <= ht.steps_max + ht.steps_max // 6  # 过冲可能加回拉点
    print(f"[fitts] ok, short={len(p_short)} long={len(p_long)} huge={len(p_huge)}")


def test_gesture_speed_profile():
    """速度轮廓应非匀速（ease-in-out 慢-快-慢），间隔存在明显离散。"""
    ht = HumanTrajectory({})
    _, intervals = ht.generate_gesture((100, 500), (1600, 500))
    cv = _avg_intervals(intervals)
    assert cv > 0.15, f"间隔变化过小(疑似匀速): cv={cv:.3f}"
    print(f"[speed] ok, 间隔变异系数={cv:.3f}")


def test_gesture_curve_nonstraight():
    """普通滑动应产生弧线（控制点法向偏移），轨迹不完全落在直线上。"""
    ht = HumanTrajectory({"perpendicular_ratio": 0.2})
    # 固定随机种子增强可复现性
    import random
    random.seed(7)
    points, _ = ht.generate_gesture((100, 1000), (1800, 1000))
    max_dev = max(abs(y - 1000) for x, y in points[1:-1])
    assert max_dev > 2, "贝塞尔曲线应偏离直线"
    print(f"[curve] ok, 最大垂向偏离={max_dev:.1f}px")


def test_gesture_slider_exact():
    """滑块模式：终点精确、末段微颤小、轨迹单调向右。"""
    ht = HumanTrajectory({})
    import random
    random.seed(11)
    pts, intervals = ht.generate_gesture((80, 1500), (1200, 1500), for_slider=True)
    assert pts[-1] == (1200, 1500), "滑块终点必须精确"
    xs = [p[0] for p in pts]
    assert all(xs[i] <= xs[i + 1] + 1 for i in range(len(xs) - 1)), "滑块应单调向右"
    # 末段抖动幅度应很小（缺口对齐精度）
    tail = pts[-5:]
    max_tail_dev = max(abs(y - 1500) for x, y in tail)
    assert max_tail_dev <= 4, f"滑块末段抖动过大: {max_tail_dev}"
    print(f"[slider] ok, pts={len(pts)}, 末段垂向误差={max_tail_dev:.2f}px")


def test_closed_form():
    """已禁用轨迹时退化为直线。"""
    ht = HumanTrajectory({})
    ht.enabled = False
    pts, intervals = ht.generate_gesture((10, 10), (50, 50))
    assert len(pts) == 8
    assert pts[-1] == (50, 50)


# ------------------- 缺口识别（需 cv2） -------------------
def _make_synthetic_slider_gap():
    """合成一张 1080x240 的滑块底部图：灰色背景 + 中央一段暗色缺口条。"""
    H, W = 240, 1080
    img = np.full((H, W), 200, dtype=np.uint8)
    # 随机纹理噪声，模拟图片
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    noise = rng.integers(-10, 10, size=img.shape).astype(np.int16)
    img = np.clip(img.astype(np.int16) + noise, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)
    # 在 x=720 附近画一个缺口（暗条）
    x0, x1, w = 700, 760, 14
    img[:, x0:x1] = 60  # 暗色凹槽
    return img, x0 + w // 2  # 缺口中心


def test_locate_gap_edge():
    from captcha_slider import locate_gap_edge
    img, expect_cx = _make_synthetic_slider_gap()
    cx = locate_gap_edge(img, band_y=120, band_h=120)
    assert cx is not None, "应识别出缺口"
    assert abs(cx - expect_cx) <= 12, f"缺口中心偏移过大: got={cx}, expect={expect_cx}"
    print(f"[gap_edge] ok, 识别中心={cx}, 期望≈{expect_cx}")


def test_locate_gap_diff():
    from captcha_slider import locate_gap_diff
    base = np.full((240, 1080), 200, dtype=np.uint8)
    cur = base.copy()
    # 拼图块填补缺口处：当前图在缺口位置颜色不同 -> 差分出现
    cur[:, 700:760] = 130
    x = locate_gap_diff(base, cur, diff_threshold=40)
    assert x is not None
    assert abs(x - 730) <= 40, f"diff 差分中心偏移: {x}"
    # 无差异时应返回 None
    assert locate_gap_diff(base, base.copy(), diff_threshold=40) is None
    print(f"[gap_diff] ok, 差分中心={x}")


# ------------------- 滑块求解器组装测试（不连设备） -------------------
def test_solver_detect():
    from captcha_slider import SliderCaptchaSolver
    cfg = {
        "enabled": True,
        "slider": {
            "detect_texts": ["按住滑块", "向右滑动"],
            "success_texts": ["验证成功"],
        },
    }
    s = SliderCaptchaSolver(cfg)
    assert s.detect_slider(None, dump="<text>按住滑块向右拖动</text>") is True
    assert not s.detect_slider(None, dump="<text>首页</text>")
    assert s.retry_times == 3, "默认重试 3 次"


def test_solver_handle_position_fallback():
    """无模板时用比例兜底定位滑块起点。"""
    from captcha_slider import SliderCaptchaSolver
    s = SliderCaptchaSolver({"enabled": True, "slider": {}})
    class FakeImg:
        width = 1080
        height = 1920
    handle, band_h = s._handle_position(FakeImg())
    sx, sy = handle
    assert 0 < sx < 300, f"起点 x 应在左侧: {sx}"
    assert sy > 1000, f"滑块应在下半屏: {sy}"
    assert band_h >= 100
    print(f"[handle] ok, start=({sx},{sy}) band_h={band_h}")


def test_config_sections():
    """config.yaml 应包含 trajectory 与 captcha 节且结构合法。"""
    import yaml
    cfg_path = os.path.join(_PROJ, "config.yaml")
    with open(cfg_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        cfg = yaml.safe_load(f)
    assert "trajectory" in cfg, "缺少 trajectory 节"
    assert cfg["trajectory"]["enabled"] is True
    assert "captcha" in cfg, "缺少 captcha 节"
    assert cfg["captcha"]["slider"]["gap"]["mode"] in ("edge", "diff", "vlm")
    print(f"[config] ok, trajectory={list(cfg['trajectory'].keys())[:5]} "
          f"captcha.gap.mode={cfg['captcha']['slider']['gap']['mode']}")


# ------------------- main -------------------
def main():
    fns = [
        ("gesture_basics", test_gesture_basics),
        ("gesture_fitts_steps", test_gesture_fitts_steps),
        ("gesture_speed_profile", test_gesture_speed_profile),
        ("gesture_curve_nonstraight", test_gesture_curve_nonstraight),
        ("gesture_slider_exact", test_gesture_slider_exact),
        ("closed_form", test_closed_form),
        ("locate_gap_edge", test_locate_gap_edge),
        ("locate_gap_diff", test_locate_gap_diff),
        ("solver_detect", test_solver_detect),
        ("solver_handle_position_fallback", test_solver_handle_position_fallback),
        ("config_sections", test_config_sections),
    ]
    failed = 0
    for name, fn in fns:
        try:
            fn()
        except Exception as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"[FAIL] {name}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
        else:
            print(f"[PASS] {name}")
    print(f"\n== 结果: {len(fns) - failed}/{len(fns)} 通过 ==")
    return 1 if failed else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

### `tests/test_unified_steps.py`（253 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_unified_steps.py - 2026-10-25 「behavior/type 重新规划整理」回归钉

背景：整理前三处重复/失效，靠这个文件钉住，防止改回去：
  ① click_text / click_id / click_desc 是同一段代码的三份拷贝 →
     合并为统一点击原语 `click`（by=text/id/desc），旧名字降级为别名；
  ② `swipe` 写 `natural: true` 时**忽略 from/to** 且永远向下滑
     （抖音火山版校准实测踩到：想上滑回顶，结果连滑到底）→ 已修；
  ③ behavior 名出现中文（快手「白拿礼物」）→ 收编为标准意图 gift；
     抖音火山版「没有签到只有预约」→ behavior 改名 reserve + intent_map 映射。

运行：
    python tests/test_unified_steps.py
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

import behavior_runner as br  # noqa: E402
from executor import Executor, LocateResult  # noqa: E402
from config_loader import load_config  # noqa: E402
import nlu  # noqa: E402


class _Rec:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = []

    def log(self, event, app="", level="INFO"):
        self.lines.append(f"[{level}] {event}")


class _Traj:
    def __init__(self):
        self.calls = []

    def generate_gesture(self, start, end, **kw):
        self.calls.append((start, end))
        return ([(start[0], start[1]), (end[0], end[1])], [0.1])

    def inject_touch(self, d, points, intervals):
        return True


class _Human:
    def __init__(self):
        self.trajectory = _Traj()
        self.natural_calls = 0

    def natural_swipe(self, d):
        self.natural_calls += 1

    def delay(self, *a):
        pass

    def maybe_wander(self, *a):
        pass

    def think(self, *a):
        pass

    def tap(self, *a, **k):
        pass


class _Dev:
    def __init__(self):
        self.swipes = []

    def swipe(self, sx, sy, ex, ey, duration=0.5):
        self.swipes.append((sx, sy, ex, ey))


def _mk():
    ex = Executor("fake-serial", _Human(), _Rec(), dry_run=True, config={})
    ex._locate = lambda k, v, timeout=2.0: LocateResult(False, None, "测试未命中")
    ex._kb_candidates = lambda app, primary: ([primary], False)
    ex._kb_mark_fail = lambda app, prims: None
    return ex


# ---------- ① 统一点击原语 ----------

def t_click_unified_hit_logs_same_as_before():
    """click by=text 命中时日志必须是「点击文本: X」（与旧 click_text 逐字一致）。"""
    ex = _mk()
    ex._locate_with_kb = lambda app, primary, timeout=6: (object(), False, True)
    act = {"type": "click", "by": "text", "text": "赚钱", "timeout": 8}
    assert ex._step_click(act, "T") is True
    assert any("点击文本: 赚钱" in l for l in ex.recorder.lines), ex.recorder.lines
    print("OK  click by=text 命中日志与旧 click_text 一致")


def t_click_unified_id_and_desc():
    for by, field, value, verb in (("id", "res_id", "iv_btn", "点击控件"),
                                   ("desc", "desc", "领火苗，按钮", "点击描述")):
        ex = _mk()
        ex._locate_with_kb = lambda app, primary, timeout=6: (object(), False, True)
        act = {"type": "click", "by": by, field: value}
        assert ex._step_click(act, "T") is True
        assert any(f"{verb}: {value}" in l for l in ex.recorder.lines), ex.recorder.lines
    print("OK  click by=id / by=desc 日志动词正确")


def t_click_alias_equivalent_to_unified():
    """别名与统一原语必须完全等价：命中日志一致、未命中 _last_fail 一致。"""
    for alias, by in (("click_text", "text"), ("click_id", "id"), ("click_desc", "desc")):
        ex_a, ex_b = _mk(), _mk()
        # 命中分支
        ex_a._locate_with_kb = lambda app, primary, timeout=6: (object(), False, True)
        ex_b._locate_with_kb = lambda app, primary, timeout=6: (object(), False, True)
        act_a = {"type": alias, "text": "X", "res_id": "X", "desc": "X"}
        act_b = dict(act_a, by=by)
        ra = getattr(ex_a, f"_step_{alias}")(act_a, "T")
        rb = ex_b._step_click(act_b, "T")
        assert ra is rb is True, (alias, ra, rb)
        assert ex_a.recorder.lines == ex_b.recorder.lines, (alias, ex_a.recorder.lines,
                                                            ex_b.recorder.lines)
        # 未命中分支
        ex_c, ex_d = _mk(), _mk()
        rc = getattr(ex_c, f"_step_{alias}")(act_a, "T")
        rd = ex_d._step_click(act_b, "T")
        assert rc is rd is False, (alias, rc, rd)
        assert ex_c._last_fail == ex_d._last_fail, (alias, ex_c._last_fail,
                                                    ex_d._last_fail)
    print("OK  click_text/click_id/click_desc 与统一点击原语完全等价")


def t_click_unknown_by_is_rejected():
    ex = _mk()
    ok = ex._step_click({"type": "click", "by": "xpath", "text": "X"}, "T")
    assert ok is False
    assert "未知 click by" in (ex._last_fail or ""), ex._last_fail
    print("OK  未知 by 返回 False 并记录 detail（不静默点错东西）")


def t_dispatch_table_has_click_and_aliases():
    """分派表必须同时认 click 与三个旧名（老配置不改也能跑）。"""
    src = open(os.path.join(ROOT, "behavior_runner.py"), encoding="utf-8").read()
    for t in ('"click": self._step_click', '"click_text": self._step_click_text',
              '"click_id": self._step_click_id', '"click_desc": self._step_click_desc',
              '"swipe_natural": self._step_swipe_natural'):
        assert t in src, f"分派表缺少 {t}"
    assert "click" in br._VISION_GATED_STEPS, br._VISION_GATED_STEPS
    print("OK  分派表含统一点击与全部别名，且 click 在视觉门控集合内")


# ---------- ② swipe natural 尊重 from/to（本次修的 bug） ----------

def _run_swipe(act, natural=True):
    ex = _mk()
    ex.d = _Dev()
    ex.dry_run = False
    br.win_wh = lambda d: (1080, 2340)  # 钉住窗口尺寸，坐标可算
    ex._step_swipe(act, "T")
    return ex


def t_swipe_natural_respects_from_to():
    """natural:true + from/to 必须按坐标走轨迹，而不是随机向下滑。"""
    ex = _run_swipe({"type": "swipe", "natural": True,
                     "from": [0.5, 0.25], "to": [0.5, 0.78], "duration": 0.45})
    assert ex.human.trajectory.calls == [((540, 585), (540, 1825))], \
        ex.human.trajectory.calls
    assert ex.human.natural_calls == 0, "natural+from/to 不应走随机滑动"
    assert any("滑动(轨迹)" in l for l in ex.recorder.lines), ex.recorder.lines
    print("OK  natural:true 尊重 from/to（bug 已修，坐标 540,585 -> 540,1825）")


def t_swipe_natural_without_coords_stays_random():
    """省略 from/to 时保留旧的随机自然滑动语义（recorder 录制的 swipe_natural）。"""
    ex = _run_swipe({"type": "swipe", "natural": True})
    assert ex.human.natural_calls == 1, "无坐标时应走随机自然滑动"
    assert any("自然滑动(随机)" in l for l in ex.recorder.lines), ex.recorder.lines
    print("OK  natural:true 无坐标时仍是随机自然滑动（向后兼容）")


def t_swipe_natural_alias_matches_swipe():
    ex = _run_swipe({"type": "swipe_natural",
                     "from": [0.5, 0.25], "to": [0.5, 0.78]})
    assert ex.human.trajectory.calls == [((540, 585), (540, 1825))], \
        ex.human.trajectory.calls
    assert ex.human.natural_calls == 0
    print("OK  swipe_natural 别名与 swipe+natural 行为一致")


def t_swipe_plain_still_uses_trajectory():
    ex = _run_swipe({"type": "swipe", "from": [0.5, 0.7], "to": [0.5, 0.3]})
    assert ex.human.trajectory.calls == [((540, 1638), (540, 702))], \
        ex.human.trajectory.calls
    print("OK  普通 swipe 仍走人类轨迹（行为未变）")


# ---------- ③ behavior 名规范化 ----------

def t_nlu_sign_not_hijacked_by_growth():
    """「签到领金币」必须解析成 sign —— growth 的「签到领」关键词已删。"""
    intents = [i for i, _ in nlu.parse_intents("帮我签到领金币")]
    assert intents and intents[0] == "sign", intents
    print("OK  「签到领金币」解析为 sign（不再被 growth 劫持）")


def t_nlu_gift_and_reserve_intents():
    assert ("gift", "白拿礼物") in nlu.parse_intents("去快手白拿礼物"), \
        nlu.parse_intents("去快手白拿礼物")
    assert ("reserve", "预约") in nlu.parse_intents("抖音火山版预约"), \
        nlu.parse_intents("抖音火山版预约")
    print("OK  gift / reserve 意图可被自然语言命中")


def t_config_behavior_names_are_ascii():
    """全部 34 个 app 的 behavior 名必须是小写 ASCII（中文名无法被意图命中）。"""
    cfg = load_config(os.path.join(ROOT, "config.yaml"))
    bad = []
    for a in cfg["apps"]:
        for bid in (a.get("behaviors") or {}):
            if not bid.isascii() or bid != bid.lower():
                bad.append((a["name"], bid))
    assert not bad, f"非规范 behavior 名: {bad}"
    print(f"OK  {len(cfg['apps'])} 个 app 的 behavior 名全部规范（无中文/无大写）")


def t_config_huoshan_sign_routes_to_reserve():
    cfg = load_config(os.path.join(ROOT, "config.yaml"))
    app = next(a for a in cfg["apps"] if a["name"] == "抖音火山版")
    assert app["intent_map"].get("sign") == "reserve", app.get("intent_map")
    assert "reserve" in app["behaviors"], list(app["behaviors"])
    print("OK  抖音火山版：sign 意图 → reserve 行为（没有签到只有预约）")


def t_config_kuaishou_gift_behavior():
    cfg = load_config(os.path.join(ROOT, "config.yaml"))
    app = next(a for a in cfg["apps"] if a["name"] == "快手")
    assert "gift" in app["behaviors"], list(app["behaviors"])
    print("OK  快手：白拿礼物 → gift（标准意图名）")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).name__ if False else type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} passed")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_webui.py`（911 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_webui.py - Web 控制台的纯逻辑与接口契约（不触碰真机）

覆盖三类容易出错、且出错后很难发现的地方：
  1. **数据层纯函数**：锚点摘要（层级/路径数/导航前置步）、配置体检、
     收益聚合与趋势、dumpsys/wm 解析。这些直接决定界面上显示什么。
  2. **任务层**：命令拼装（空行为必须跳过、资金类标记、--dry-run/--force）、
     增量日志的 offset 语义（前端靠它续读，错了会丢日志或重复）。
  3. **服务层**：路由匹配、写操作鉴权、**资金类行为必须二次确认**
     （项目硬约束：提现/支付/转账不得自动执行）、静态文件路径穿越防护。

运行：python tests/test_webui.py
"""
import json
import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from webui import data as D      # noqa: E402
from webui import jobs as J      # noqa: E402
from webui import server as S    # noqa: E402


# ===========================================================================
# 1. 数据层纯函数
# ===========================================================================
def _nav_step():
    return {"type": "click_point", "x": 0.9, "y": 0.915,
            "vlm_prompt": "定位屏幕底部导航栏最右侧的『我的』标签中心"}


def t_summarize_simple_behavior():
    """单锚点行为：1 层、1 条路径、无导航前置步。"""
    s = D.summarize_behavior([{"type": "click_first",
                               "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"}]}])
    assert s["levels"] == 1, s
    assert s["branches"] == 1, s
    assert s["has_prelude"] is False, s
    assert s["anchors"][0]["candidates"] == ["签到"], s
    print("OK  单锚点行为 -> 1 层 / 1 路径 / 无前置步")


def t_summarize_three_level_anyof():
    """三级 any_of：识别出导航前置步与最长层级。

    分支2 = [我的(导航), think, 福利中心, 签到]。「我的」不是锚点，但它是
    **前置步**；再加 2 个锚点 —— 这条路径要三级才能到目标。
    口径：层级 = 1(首屏) + 有前置步 + 路径上锚点数 > 1。
    """
    act = [{"type": "any_of", "choices": [
        [{"type": "click_first", "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"}]}],
        [_nav_step(), {"type": "think"},
         {"type": "click_first", "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "福利中心"}]},
         {"type": "click_first", "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"}]}],
    ]}]
    s = D.summarize_behavior(act)
    assert s["branches"] == 2, s
    assert s["levels"] == 3, s          # 1 + 前置步 + 2 个锚点
    assert s["has_prelude"] is True, s
    # 跨分支按 (type, key) 去重：两条分支的「签到」是同一个锚点
    assert [a["key"] for a in s["anchors"]] == ["签到", "福利中心"], s
    print("OK  三级 any_of -> 2 路径 / 3 层 / 检出导航前置步")


def t_anchor_kinds_are_classified_by_payload():
    """锚点必须按**载荷**分类，不能只看 type —— click_point 是重载类型。

    踩过的坑：最初只把 click_text/id/desc/click_first 当锚点，于是把 30 个
    「图像模板匹配」行为误报成「没有可校验的锚点动作」，总览页一片假红字；
    随后又漏了 click_id 靠 res_id 定位，把正常的关闭按钮锚点报成「未配置候选」。
    """
    img = {"type": "click_point", "images": ["images/a/b.png"],
           "desc": "图像-某App-任务界面-签到", "optional": True}
    nav = _nav_step()                                    # 导航前置步，不是锚点
    pure = {"type": "click_point", "x": 0.5, "y": 0.5}   # 纯比例兜底，不是锚点
    rid = {"type": "click_id", "res_id": "com.x:id/iv_close"}
    first = {"type": "click_first",
             "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"},
                            {"type": "text", "value": "每日签到"}]}

    assert D.is_anchor(img) is True, img
    assert D.is_anchor(nav) is False, nav
    assert D.is_anchor(pure) is False, pure
    assert D.is_anchor(rid) is True, rid
    assert D.is_nav_prelude(nav) is True and D.is_nav_prelude(img) is False

    s = D.summarize_behavior([img, nav, pure, rid, first])
    assert s["levels"] == 3, s            # 1 + 前置步 + 路径上 3 个锚点(>1)
    assert s["has_prelude"] is True, s
    by_via = {a["via"]: a for a in s["anchors"]}
    assert set(by_via) == {"图像", "rid", "候选"}, s
    assert by_via["图像"]["key"] == "images/a/b.png", s   # key 取参考图
    assert by_via["图像"]["images"] == 1, s
    assert by_via["rid"]["key"] == "com.x:id/iv_close", s  # key 取 res_id
    assert by_via["候选"]["key"] == "签到", s              # key 取首个候选
    # 纯比例兜底点不能混进锚点
    assert len(s["anchors"]) == 3, s
    print("OK  锚点按载荷分类（图像/rid/候选/导航前置/纯比例）")


def t_validate_config_accepts_image_and_rid_anchors():
    """图像锚点与 rid 锚点都是合法定位手段，体检不得报错。"""
    cfg = {"apps": [
        {"name": "图像App", "package": "com.img", "behaviors": {
            "sign": [{"type": "click_point", "images": ["images/x/y.png"]}]}},
        {"name": "RidApp", "package": "com.rid", "behaviors": {
            "sign": [{"type": "click_id", "res_id": "com.rid:id/btn"}]}},
    ]}
    issues = D.validate_config(cfg)
    assert issues == [], issues
    # 反向：真的没有定位依据时仍要报错
    bad = {"apps": [{"name": "空App", "package": "com.bad", "behaviors": {
        "sign": [{"type": "click_point", "x": 0.5, "y": 0.5}]}}]}
    got = D.validate_config(bad)
    assert any(i["level"] == "ERROR" for i in got), got
    print("OK  体检接受图像/rid 锚点，仍拒真·无锚点行为")


def t_summarize_variant_templates():
    """多候选模板 [[..],[..]] 取第一组，不把模板层数算成路径数。"""
    act = [[{"type": "click_text", "text": "签到"}],
           [{"type": "click_text", "text": "每日签到"}]]
    s = D.summarize_behavior(act)
    assert s["levels"] == 1, s
    assert s["anchors"][0]["text"] == "签到", s
    print("OK  多候选模板取第一组")


def t_validate_config_catches_real_problems():
    cfg = {"apps": [
        {"name": "A", "package": "com.a", "behaviors": {
            "sign": [{"type": "click_text", "text": "签到"}]}},
        {"name": "A", "package": "com.a2", "behaviors": {}},          # 重名 + 无行为
        {"name": "B", "package": "", "behaviors": {                   # 缺 package
            "sign": [{"type": "think"}]}},                            # 无锚点
    ]}
    issues = D.validate_config(cfg)
    msgs = " | ".join(i["msg"] for i in issues)
    assert any("App 名重复" in i["msg"] for i in issues), msgs
    assert any("未配置任何行为" in i["msg"] for i in issues), msgs
    assert any("缺少 package" in i["msg"] for i in issues), msgs
    assert any("没有可校验的锚点动作" in i["msg"] for i in issues), msgs
    assert all(i["level"] in ("ERROR", "WARN") for i in issues)
    print(f"OK  配置体检抓到 4 类问题（共 {len(issues)} 条）")


def t_aggregate_and_series():
    rows = [
        ("2026-09-15 08:00:00", "A", "sign", 100, "金币", ""),
        ("2026-09-15 09:00:00", "B", "watch", 50, "金币", ""),
        ("2026-09-14 09:00:00", "A", "sign", 1.5, "元", ""),
    ]
    agg = D.aggregate_earnings(rows, today="2026-09-15")
    by = {x["unit"]: x for x in agg["summary"]}
    assert by["金币"]["total"] == 150, by
    assert by["金币"]["today"] == 150, by
    assert by["元"]["today"] == 0, by
    assert agg["total_rows"] == 3
    ser = D.daily_series(rows, days=2, today="2026-09-15")
    assert [x["date"] for x in ser] == ["2026-09-14", "2026-09-15"], ser
    assert ser[1]["total"] == 150, ser
    print("OK  收益聚合（按单位/今日）与近 N 日趋势正确")


def t_parse_device_outputs():
    out = ("  mCurrentFocus=Window{a1b2 u0 com.kwai.theater/"
           "com.kwai.theater.MainActivity}\n")
    fg = D.parse_foreground(out)
    assert fg["package"] == "com.kwai.theater", fg
    assert fg["activity"] == "com.kwai.theater.MainActivity", fg
    assert D.parse_foreground("") == {"package": "", "activity": ""}
    assert D.parse_wm_size("Physical size: 1080x2340") == (1080, 2340)
    assert D.parse_wm_size("Override size: 720x1280") == (720, 1280)
    assert D.parse_wm_size("garbage") == (None, None)
    print("OK  dumpsys / wm size 解析（含空输入）")


def t_behavior_task_text():
    assert D.behavior_task_text(["sign", "watch"]) == "签到，然后看视频"
    assert D.behavior_task_text([]) == "签到"
    assert D.behavior_task_text(["unknown_x"]) == "unknown_x"

# 2. 任务层
```
