```python
def t_build_app_steps_skips_empty_and_flags_money():
    steps = J.build_app_steps([
        {"name": "A", "behaviors": ["sign", "watch"]},
        {"name": "B", "behaviors": []},                  # 必须跳过，不能退化成默认签到
        {"name": "", "behaviors": ["sign"]},             # 无名，跳过
        {"name": "C", "behaviors": ["withdraw"]},
    ], dry_run=True, force=True, serial="1.2.3.4",
       money_behaviors=["withdraw"])                     # C9 后由调用方注入，测试同步适配
    assert [s.title for s in steps] == ["A", "C"], [s.title for s in steps]
    a = steps[0].cmd
    assert "--dry-run" in a and "--force" in a and "--serial" in a, a
    assert a[a.index("--task") + 1] == "签到，然后看视频", a
    assert steps[1].meta["money"] == ["withdraw"], steps[1].meta
    assert steps[0].meta["money"] == [], steps[0].meta
    print("OK  空行为/无名跳过；--dry-run/--force/--serial 与资金标记正确")


def t_job_log_offset_semantics():
    """前端靠 offset 续读：next 之后必须返回空，不能重复也不能丢。"""
    job = J.Job("tool", "t", [])
    job.append("l1\nl2\n")
    n1, t1 = job.read_log(0)
    assert t1 == "l1\nl2\n" and n1 == 2, (n1, t1)
    job.append("l3\n")
    n2, t2 = job.read_log(n1)
    assert t2 == "l3\n", t2
    n3, t3 = job.read_log(n2)
    assert t3 == "", t3
    assert n3 == n2
    print("OK  日志增量 offset 语义（不重复、不丢失）")


def t_job_runs_and_reports_steps():
    steps = [
        J.Step("ok", [sys.executable, "-c", "print('hello')"]),
        J.Step("bad", [sys.executable, "-c", "import sys;sys.exit(2)"]),
    ]
    job = J.Job("tool", "t", steps)
    job.start()
    for _ in range(100):
        if job.finished:
            break
        time.sleep(0.05)
    assert job.state == "failed", job.state
    assert steps[0].state == "done" and steps[1].state == "failed", \
        (steps[0].state, steps[1].state)
    assert job.progress()["done"] == 2
    _, txt = job.read_log(0)
    assert "hello" in txt, txt
    print("OK  任务串行执行、逐步状态与失败传播")


def t_job_stop_kills_current_step():
    """停止必须真的终止子进程，且后续步骤不再执行。"""
    steps = [
        J.Step("sleep", [sys.executable, "-c", "import time;time.sleep(30)"]),
        J.Step("never", [sys.executable, "-c", "print('should not run')"]),
    ]
    job = J.Job("tool", "t", steps)
    job.start()
    for _ in range(100):
        if steps[0].state == "running":
            break
        time.sleep(0.05)
    job.stop()
    for _ in range(200):
        if job.finished:
            break
        time.sleep(0.05)
    assert job.finished, job.state
    assert steps[0].state == "stopped", steps[0].state
    assert steps[1].state == "stopped", steps[1].state
    _, txt = job.read_log(0)
    assert "should not run" not in txt, "停止后不应继续执行后续步骤"
    print("OK  停止真的杀掉进程且阻止后续步骤")


def t_job_manager_single_active():
    m = J.JobManager()
    j = m.create("tool", "t", [J.Step("s", [sys.executable, "-c", "import time;time.sleep(2)"])])
    j.start()
    assert m.active() is not None
    assert m.list()[0]["id"] == j.id
    m.stop_all()
    for _ in range(200):
        if j.finished:
            break
        time.sleep(0.05)
    assert m.active() is None
    print("OK  JobManager 活动任务追踪与 stop_all")


def t_tool_step_whitelist():
    st = J.build_tool_step("verify_live", ["--apps", "一淘"])
    assert st.cmd[-2:] == ["--apps", "一淘"], st.cmd
    assert "verify_live.py" in st.cmd[1], st.cmd
    try:
        J.build_tool_step("nope")
        raise AssertionError("未知工具应报错")
    except ValueError:
        pass
    ids = {t["id"] for t in J.tool_catalog()}
    assert {"preflight", "shot", "verify_live"} <= ids, ids
    print("OK  工具白名单与参数拼装")


# ===========================================================================
# 3. 服务层（起真实 HTTP 服务，只测本机接口）
# ===========================================================================
def _client(port, path, method="GET", body=None, token=None):
    url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}{path}"
    data = json.dumps(body).encode() if body is not None else None
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, method=method)
    if data is not None:
        req.add_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
    if token:
        req.add_header("X-Auth-Token", token)
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=20) as r:
            raw = r.read()
            return r.status, raw, r.headers.get("Content-Type", "")
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        return e.code, e.read(), e.headers.get("Content-Type", "")


def _start_test_server():
    port = S.find_free_port(18800, tries=40)
    console = S.Console(D.CONFIG_YAML, D.DB_PATH)
    httpd = S.make_server("127.0.0.1", port, console)
    threading.Thread(target=httpd.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
    return port, httpd, console


def t_overview_exposes_frontend_contract():
    """锁定 /api/overview 的字段契约 —— 前端读什么，后端就必须给什么。

    踩过的坑：`Store.apps()` 是**逐字段手工拼**的，新增 `vias` 后忘了往
    这里加，接口静默少一个字段，前端 `b.vias.length` 直接抛 TypeError，
    而所有后端单测照样全绿。这里按前端实际读取的字段逐一断言。
    """
    port, httpd, console = _start_test_server()
    try:
        st, raw, _ = _client(port, "/api/overview", "GET")
        assert st == 200, (st, raw)
        ov = json.loads(raw)
        for key in ("device", "app_count", "behavior_count",
                    "money_behavior_count", "apps", "issues",
                    "verify_coverage"):
            assert key in ov, f"overview 缺字段 {key}"
        assert ov["apps"], "配置里应有 App"
        beh_keys = ("id", "trigger", "money", "levels", "branches",
                    "has_prelude", "vias", "anchors")
        anc_keys = ("type", "via", "key", "text", "candidates", "images")
        for a in ov["apps"]:
            for key in ("index", "name", "package", "aliases", "behaviors"):
                assert key in a, f"{a.get('name')} 缺字段 {key}"
            for b in a["behaviors"]:
                for key in beh_keys:
                    assert key in b, f"{a['name']}.{b.get('id')} 缺字段 {key}"
                assert isinstance(b["vias"], list) and b["vias"], b
                for an in b["anchors"]:
                    for key in anc_keys:
                        assert key in an, f"{a['name']}.{b['id']} 锚点缺 {key}"
                    assert an["key"], f"{a['name']}.{b['id']} 锚点定位依据为空"
        print("OK  /api/overview 字段契约完整（含 vias / 锚点 key）")
    finally:
        httpd.shutdown()
        httpd.server_close()


def t_frontend_only_reads_fields_api_provides():
    """扫前端源码里 `b.<字段>` / `a.<字段>` 的读取点，逐个核对接口确实给了。

    这是上面那个 bug 的**反向**护栏：契约测试只保证「接口有这些字段」，
    但如果哪天前端新读一个接口没给的字段（`b.vias` 当初就是这样漏的），
    后端单测照样全绿、浏览器里直接 TypeError。所以这里从 JS 源码反推需求。
    """
    js_path = os.path.join(ROOT, "webui", "static", "app.js")
    with open(js_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        js = f.read()
    api_keys = {
        "b": {"id", "trigger", "money", "levels", "branches", "has_prelude",
              "vias", "anchors"},
        # a.* 里的 class/classList/dataset 是 DOM 元素，不是 App 对象
        "a": {"name", "package", "aliases", "behaviors", "class", "classList",
              "dataset"},
    }
    bad = []
    for var, allowed in api_keys.items():
        found = set(re.findall(r"\b%s\.([a-z_][a-z_0-9]*)" % var, js))
        for k in sorted(found - allowed):
            bad.append(f"{var}.{k}")
    assert not bad, "前端读取了接口未提供的字段：" + "、".join(bad)
    print("OK  前端只读接口已提供的字段（防 vias 式静默漏字段）")


def t_verifier_coverage_uses_verifier_rule():
    """覆盖率必须照 verify_live.py 的口径算（含 click_point 图像锚点）。

    verify_live.py 的 is_anchor_action 已把「带参考图的 click_point」算作锚点，
    因此图像锚点行为会进入 stability_run 的命中率分母、被真正度量。
    界面必须与它同口径，否则会把「已度量」误显示成「未度量」。
    """
    img = [{"type": "click_point", "images": ["i/a.png"]}]
    txt = [{"type": "click_first",
            "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"}]}]
    anyof = [{"type": "any_of", "choices": [[
        {"type": "click_first", "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "签到"}]}]]}]
    assert D.verifier_can_check(img) is True, "图像锚点行为现在应可校验（进分母）"
    assert D.verifier_can_check(txt) is True
    assert D.verifier_can_check(anyof) is True
    # 与 verify_live.py 的 is_anchor_action 口径一致：图像锚点必须是锚点
    assert D.summarize_behavior(img)["anchors"], "GUI 侧图像锚点必须是锚点"

    cov = D.Store(D.CONFIG_YAML, D.DB_PATH).verify_coverage()
    assert cov["verifiable"] + cov["unverifiable"] == cov["total"], cov
    assert cov["total"] > 0, cov
    # 图像锚点已纳入实机验证器：不再有「仅图像锚点未度量」；配置里无纯兜底行为
    assert cov["image_only"] == 0, cov
    assert cov["unverifiable"] == 0, cov
    print(f"OK  实机覆盖率口径（{cov['verifiable']}/{cov['total']} 可校验，"
          f"图像锚点已进分母，未度量 {cov['unverifiable']}）")


def t_route_match():
    assert S._match("/api/jobs/abc", "/api/jobs/{id}") == {"id": "abc"}
    assert S._match("/api/jobs/abc/log", "/api/jobs/{id}/log") == {"id": "abc"}
    assert S._match("/api/jobs", "/api/jobs/{id}") is None
    assert S._match("/api/jobs//log", "/api/jobs/{id}/log") is None
    print("OK  路由匹配（含空段拒绝）")


def t_http_endpoints_and_auth():
    port, httpd, console = _start_test_server()
    try:
        st, raw, ct = _client(port, "/")
        assert st == 200 and b"__CONSOLE_TOKEN__" not in raw, "token 必须被替换"
        assert console.token.encode() in raw, "首页应注入真实 token"
        assert "text/html" in ct

        st, raw, _ = _client(port, "/api/health")
        assert st == 200 and json.loads(raw)["ok"] is True

        st, raw, _ = _client(port, "/api/overview")
        ov = json.loads(raw)
        assert ov["app_count"] > 0 and "apps" in ov

        # 静态资源：/static/ 前缀必须剥掉（曾拼成 static/static/ 导致 404）
        st, raw, ct = _client(port, "/static/app.css")
        assert st == 200 and b"{" not in raw[:20], (st, ct)

        # 写操作无 token -> 403
        st, raw, _ = _client(port, "/api/jobs", "POST", {"plan": []})
        assert st == 403, st

        # 路径穿越
        for p in ("/static/../webui/data.py", "/static/%2e%2e/webui/data.py"):
            st, _, _ = _client(port, p)
            assert st in (403, 404), (p, st)
        print("OK  HTTP 接口、token 注入、写操作鉴权、静态前缀与路径穿越")
    finally:
        httpd.shutdown()
        httpd.server_close()


def t_money_behavior_requires_confirmation():
    """资金类行为：服务端必须强制二次确认（不能只靠前端弹窗）。"""
    port, httpd, console = _start_test_server()
    try:
        tok = console.token
        # 找一个含 withdraw 的真实 App
        target = next((a for a in console.store.apps()
                       if any(b["money"] for b in a["behaviors"])), None)
        assert target is not None, "配置里应存在资金类行为"
        body = {"plan": [{"name": target["name"], "behaviors": ["withdraw"]}]}

        st, raw, _ = _client(port, "/api/jobs", "POST", body, token=tok)
        assert st == 403, st
        assert "资金" in json.loads(raw)["error"], raw

        # 带 confirm_money 才放行（用 dry_run 避免真操作设备）
        body2 = dict(body, confirm_money=True, dry_run=True)
        st, raw, _ = _client(port, "/api/jobs", "POST", body2, token=tok)
        assert st == 200, (st, raw)
        jid = json.loads(raw)["job"]["id"]
        assert json.loads(raw)["job"]["meta"]["money_apps"] == [target["name"]]
        _client(port, f"/api/jobs/{jid}/stop", "POST", {}, token=tok)
        print(f"OK  资金类行为强制二次确认（{target['name']}）")
    finally:
        httpd.shutdown()
        httpd.server_close()


def t_job_validation_rejects_bad_input():
    port, httpd, console = _start_test_server()
    try:
        tok = console.token
        cases = [
            ({"plan": [{"name": "不存在的App", "behaviors": ["sign"]}]}, "不存在"),
            ({"plan": [{"name": "一淘", "behaviors": ["不存在的行为"]}]}, "未配置行为"),
            ({"plan": []}, "没有可执行"),
            ({"plan": [{"name": "一淘", "behaviors": []}]}, "没有可执行"),
            # 键名写错（如把 plan 写成 apps）时，必须直说「缺字段」并回显收到的
            # 字段名 —— 否则会落到「没有可执行」那句上，让人以为是勾选问题。
            ({"apps": [{"name": "一淘", "behaviors": ["sign"]}]},
             "缺少 plan 字段"),
        ]
        for body, expect in cases:
            st, raw, _ = _client(port, "/api/jobs", "POST", body, token=tok)
            assert st == 400, (body, st)
            assert expect in json.loads(raw)["error"], (body, raw)
        st, raw, _ = _client(port, "/api/jobs", "POST",
                             {"plan": [{"name": "一淘", "behaviors": ["sign"]}],
                              "dry_run": True}, token=tok)
        assert st == 200, (st, raw)
        jid = json.loads(raw)["job"]["id"]
        # 已有任务运行时不得再起一个（设备是独占资源）
        st, raw, _ = _client(port, "/api/jobs", "POST",
                             {"plan": [{"name": "一淘", "behaviors": ["sign"]}],
                              "dry_run": True}, token=tok)
        assert st == 409, (st, raw)
        _client(port, f"/api/jobs/{jid}/stop", "POST", {}, token=tok)
        print("OK  任务参数校验（未知 App / 未知行为 / 空计划 / 缺 plan 字段 / 并发冲突）")
    finally:
        httpd.shutdown()
        httpd.server_close()


def t_css_scroll_containers_disable_flex_shrink():
    """滚动容器：flex 纵向 + max-height 的容器，子项必须禁掉收缩。

    踩过的坑（真实且极难排查）：`.applist` 是 `display:flex;flex-direction:column`
    且有 `max-height`，34 个 App 子项默认 `flex-shrink:1` 被**压扁**到 ~19px，
    再被 `overflow:hidden` 裁掉文字 —— 表现为「DOM 里内容完整、界面上是一片空条」。
    导出 DOM 完全看不出来，只有真正渲染出来才发现。
    """
    css_path = os.path.join(ROOT, "webui", "static", "app.css")
    with open(css_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        css = f.read()
    rules = []
    for sel, body in re.findall(r"([^{}]+)\{([^{}]*)\}", css):
        # 去掉注释与换行，得到「选择器列表 + 紧凑声明」
        sel = re.sub(r"/\*.*?\*/", "", sel, flags=re.S)
        sel = " ".join(sel.split())
        rules.append((sel, body.replace(" ", "")))

    def shrink_guard_for(container):
        """容器或其 `> *` 子项选择器上是否有禁收缩声明。"""
        base = container.split(",")[0].strip()
        for sel, body in rules:
            if not ("flex:00auto" in body or "flex-shrink:0" in body
                    or "flex:none" in body):
                continue
            for one in sel.split(","):
                one = one.strip()
                if one.startswith(base) and (">" in one or one == base):
                    return True
        return False

    offenders = []
    for sel, body in rules:
        if "display:flex" in body and "flex-direction:column" in body \
                and "max-height" in body:
            for one in sel.split(","):
                one = one.strip()
                if one and not shrink_guard_for(one):
                    offenders.append(one)
    assert not offenders, (
        "以下滚动容器未禁掉子项收缩，内容会被压扁裁掉：" + "、".join(offenders))
    print("OK  CSS 滚动容器均已禁掉 flex 收缩（防「空条」回归）")


def t_http_stop_really_stops():
    """通过 HTTP 停止任务：必须真的结束，而不是一直 running。"""
    port, httpd, console = _start_test_server()
    try:
        tok = console.token
        job = console.jobs.create("tool", "长任务", [
            J.Step("sleep", [sys.executable, "-c", "import time;time.sleep(60)"]),
            J.Step("never", [sys.executable, "-c", "print('MUST-NOT-RUN')"]),
        ])
        job.start()
        for _ in range(60):
            if job.steps[0].state == "running":
                break
            time.sleep(0.05)
        st, raw, _ = _client(port, f"/api/jobs/{job.id}/stop", "POST", {}, token=tok)
        assert st == 200, (st, raw)
        for _ in range(200):
            if job.finished:
                break
            time.sleep(0.05)
        assert job.finished, f"停止后仍是 {job.state}"
        _, txt = job.read_log(0)
        assert "MUST-NOT-RUN" not in txt, "停止后不应继续执行后续步骤"
        print("OK  HTTP 停止任务真正生效（不依赖子进程输出）")
    finally:
        httpd.shutdown()
        httpd.server_close()


# ===========================================================================
# 5. 校准 / 稳定性（devtools/calib 的产物）
# ===========================================================================
def t_stability_report_matches_stability_run():
    """校准页的数字必须与命令行 stability_run **逐项一致**。

    这是本项目踩过的「静默不一致」类型的护栏：如果 GUI 自己另写一份
    命中/作废判定，两套口径会慢慢分叉 —— 界面显示 70.5%、命令行显示别的数，
    而两边单测都是绿的（因为各自都自洽）。所以这里拿 stability_run 的
    分类函数把同一份文件重算一遍做对照。
    """
    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(ROOT, "devtools"))
    import stability_run as SR
    if not os.path.exists(D.STABILITY_JSON):
        print("SKIP 无 STABILITY.json（未跑过稳定性统计）")
        return
    rep = D.load_stability(D.STABILITY_JSON)
    with open(D.STABILITY_JSON, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        raw = json.load(f)

    stable, flaky, never = SR.classify(raw["per_behavior"])
    assert rep["counts"]["stable"] == len(stable), rep["counts"]
    assert rep["counts"]["flaky"] == len(flaky), rep["counts"]
    assert rep["counts"]["never"] == len(never), rep["counts"]
    assert rep["counts"]["behaviors"] == len(raw["per_behavior"])
    assert rep["rounds"] == raw["rounds"]

    # 逐行用 statuses 重算，顺带校验报告文件自身没写错
    for row in rep["behaviors"]:
        valid = [s for s in row["statuses"] if not SR.is_void(s)]
        hits = sum(1 for s in valid if SR.is_hit(s))
        assert row["valid"] == len(valid), row
        assert row["hits"] == hits, row
        assert row["kind"] == D._kind_of(row["valid"], row["hits"]), row
    assert rep["total"]["hits"] == sum(r["hits"] for r in rep["behaviors"])
    assert rep["total"]["valid"] == sum(r["valid"] for r in rep["behaviors"])
    assert rep["counts"]["unmeasured"] == sum(
        1 for r in rep["behaviors"] if not r["valid"])
    print(f"OK  校准报告与 stability_run 口径一致"
          f"（{rep['total']['hits']}/{rep['total']['valid']}）")


def t_stability_kind_classification():
    """逐行为三分类的边界：未度量 / 稳定 / 波动 / 从未命中。"""
    assert D._kind_of(0, 0) == "未度量", "valid=0 是「未度量」，不是「从未命中」"
    assert D._kind_of(3, 3) == "稳定"
    assert D._kind_of(3, 0) == "从未命中"
    assert D._kind_of(3, 1) == "波动"
    assert D._kind_of(3, 2) == "波动"
    print("OK  逐行为三分类边界正确（未度量/稳定/波动/从未命中）")


def t_stability_chain_orders_stages():
    """版本对比链必须按**声明的阶段序**排，不能靠文件名排序。"""
    chain = D.stability_chain()
    if not chain:
        print("SKIP 无版本备份")
        return
    assert sum(1 for x in chain if x["current"]) <= 1, "最多一个「当前」"
    known = [x["stage"] for x in chain if x["stage"] in D.STAGE_ORDER]
    expect = [s for s in D.STAGE_ORDER if s in known]
    assert known == expect, f"阶段序不对：{known} != {expect}"
    for x in chain:
        assert x["valid"] >= 0 and x["stable"] + x["flaky"] + x["never"] >= 0
        assert x["rate"] is None or 0.0 <= x["rate"] <= 1.0, x
    print(f"OK  版本对比链按阶段序排列（{len(chain)} 个版本）")


def t_stability_missing_file_degrades():
    """报告缺失时必须降级成「一句可操作的提示」，不能让整页 500。"""
    store = D.Store(config_path=D.CONFIG_YAML, db_path=D.DB_PATH)
    orig = D.STABILITY_JSON
    try:
        D.STABILITY_JSON = os.path.join(D.CALIB_DIR, "__nope__.json")
        r = store.stability()
        assert r["available"] is False, r
        assert r.get("hint"), "降级时必须给出提示，否则用户不知道下一步做什么"
    finally:
        D.STABILITY_JSON = orig
    assert store.stability()["available"] is True, "恢复路径后应能正常读到"
    print("OK  报告缺失时优雅降级（不抛异常、给出可操作提示）")


def t_calib_endpoint_exposes_frontend_contract():
    """锁定 /api/calib 的字段契约。"""
    port, httpd, console = _start_test_server()
    try:
        st, raw, _ = _client(port, "/api/calib", "GET")
        assert st == 200, (st, raw)
        cb = json.loads(raw)
        for key in ("available", "chain", "verify_coverage"):
            assert key in cb, f"/api/calib 缺字段 {key}"
        assert isinstance(cb["chain"], list), cb["chain"]
        assert "verifiable" in cb["verify_coverage"], cb["verify_coverage"]
        if not cb["available"]:
            assert cb.get("hint"), "不可用时必须给 hint"
            print("OK  /api/calib 契约完整（当前无报告，走降级分支）")
            return
        for key in ("rounds", "total", "counts", "behaviors"):
            assert key in cb, f"/api/calib 缺字段 {key}"
        for key in ("hits", "valid", "void", "rate"):
            assert key in cb["total"], f"total 缺 {key}"
        for key in ("stable", "flaky", "never", "behaviors", "unmeasured"):
            assert key in cb["counts"], f"counts 缺 {key}"
        row_keys = ("app", "behavior", "statuses", "valid", "void",
                    "hits", "rate", "kind")
        assert cb["behaviors"], "报告里应有行为行"
        for r in cb["behaviors"]:
            for key in row_keys:
                assert key in r, f"行为行缺 {key}"
            assert r["kind"] in ("稳定", "波动", "从未命中", "未度量"), r
        for x in cb["chain"]:
            for key in ("stage", "label", "current", "mtime", "rate",
                        "hits", "valid", "stable", "flaky", "never"):
                assert key in x, f"版本项缺 {key}"
        print(f"OK  /api/calib 字段契约完整（{len(cb['behaviors'])} 行 / "
              f"{len(cb['chain'])} 个版本）")
    finally:
        httpd.shutdown()
        httpd.server_close()


def t_calib_frontend_only_reads_fields_api_provides():
    """校准页的前端字段护栏（`cb.` 前缀）。

    通用护栏只扫 `b.`/`a.`（App / 行为对象）；`c.` 在总览卡片里被复用成
    「卡片对象」，没法区分。所以校准页刻意把变量命名为 `cb.`，这里单独护栏。

    `assert found` 不是多余的：如果哪天变量被改名，扫不到读取点，
    这道护栏会**静默失效**，比没有护栏更危险。
    """
    js_path = os.path.join(ROOT, "webui", "static", "app.js")
    with open(js_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        js = f.read()
    # hint 只在降级分支下发；available/chain/verify_coverage 恒在；
    # 其余四项仅 available=True 时下发。
    allowed = {"available", "hint", "rounds", "total", "counts",
               "behaviors", "chain", "verify_coverage"}
    found = set(re.findall(r"\bcb\.([a-z_][a-z_0-9]*)", js))
    assert found, "没扫到任何 cb.* 读取点 —— 护栏已失效（变量被改名？）"
    bad = sorted(found - allowed)
    assert not bad, "校准页读取了接口未提供的字段：" + "、".join(bad)
    print(f"OK  校准页只读接口已提供字段（{len(found)} 个）")


def t_job_history_exposes_fields_frontend_reads():
    """任务历史列表的字段契约。

    前端靠 `jobs[0]` 当「最近一次」、靠 `j.id/j.state/j.title` 渲染历史条、
    靠 `active` 决定是否跟随轮询。三者任一缺失都会让历史条变空白。
    """
    port, httpd, console = _start_test_server()
    try:
        for t in ("历史甲", "历史乙"):
            console.jobs.create("tool", t, [
                J.Step("noop", [sys.executable, "-c", "print(1)"])])
        st, raw, _ = _client(port, "/api/jobs", "GET")
        assert st == 200, (st, raw)
        d = json.loads(raw)
        assert "jobs" in d and isinstance(d["jobs"], list), d
        assert "active" in d, "缺少 active 字段（前端靠它决定是否跟随）"
        for x in d["jobs"]:
            for key in ("id", "state", "title"):
                assert key in x, f"任务项缺 {key}"
            assert x["state"] in ("pending", "running", "done", "failed",
                                  "stopped"), x
        # 最新在最前 —— 前端直接取 jobs[0]
        assert d["jobs"][0]["title"] == "历史乙", [x["title"] for x in d["jobs"]]
        print("OK  /api/jobs 历史列表字段契约完整（最新在前）")
    finally:
        httpd.shutdown()
        httpd.server_close()


def t_calib_diagnose_deeplink_wiring():
    """校准页「诊断 →」深链的三段接线必须同时在位。

    这条链横跨两个文件、四个约定：前端 `data-diag` → `S.toolPrefill.id`
    → 工具输入框的 `data-arg` → 后端 `tool_catalog()` 里的工具 id。
    任何一段被改名都会**静默失效**：按钮还在、点了没反应，
    而且前后端单测都照样绿。所以这里把四段都静态断言一遍。
    """
    js = open(os.path.join(ROOT, "webui", "static", "app.js"),
              encoding="utf-8").read()
    ids = {t["id"] for t in J.tool_catalog()}
    assert "verify_live" in ids, f"工具目录里没有 verify_live：{sorted(ids)}"
    for needle in ("data-diag", "gotoDiagnose", "toolPrefill", "data-arg="):
        assert needle in js, f"前端缺少深链接线片段：{needle}"
    m = re.search(r"toolPrefill\s*=\s*\{\s*id:\s*'([^']+)'", js)
    assert m, "没找到 gotoDiagnose 里设置的 toolPrefill.id"
    assert m.group(1) in ids, f"深链指向了不存在的工具：{m.group(1)}"
    print(f"OK  校准→诊断深链四段接线完整（目标工具 {m.group(1)}）")


def t_job_title_distinguishes_apps():
    """任务标题必须带 App 名 —— 任务历史列表靠它区分条目。

    实测踩过：标题原本是「预览 1 个 App」，历史里三条一模一样，
    根本认不出哪条是哪个 App，新加的历史列表等于白给。
    用 dry-run 建任务（不碰设备），断言完立即停掉。
    """
    port, httpd, console = _start_test_server()
    try:
        job = console.create_app_job({
            "plan": [{"name": "趣头条", "behaviors": ["sign"]},
                     {"name": "河马剧场", "behaviors": ["watch"]}],
            "dry_run": True,
        })
        title = job.title
        assert "趣头条" in title and "河马剧场" in title, title
        assert "2 个 App" not in title, f"标题退回成「N 个 App」了：{title}"
        print(f"OK  任务标题含 App 名（历史条目可区分）：{title}")
    finally:
        console.jobs.stop_all()
        httpd.shutdown()
        httpd.server_close()


def _find_node():
    for c in (shutil.which("node"),
              os.path.expanduser("~/.workbuddy-ai/binaries/node/versions/"
                                 "22.22.2-2/node.exe")):
        if c and os.path.exists(c):
            return c
    return None


def t_ops_event_humanizer_translates_action_json():
    """`humanEvent` 必须把动作 JSON 翻成人话 —— **包括带列表前缀的那种**。

    实测踩过：日志里的计划 dump 长这样 `- {"type":"wait","seconds":3}`，
    最初只判断「首字符是不是 {」，于是带 `- ` 前缀的行原样透出，
    总览的「最近事件」照样是一屏裸 JSON（改完还是没效果）。

    这是纯 JS 函数，用 Node 真跑一遍再断言 —— 光看源码看不出结果对不对。
    """
    node = _find_node()
    if not node:
        print("SKIP 未找到 node，跳过 JS 纯函数校验")
        return
    js = open(os.path.join(ROOT, "webui", "static", "app.js"),
              encoding="utf-8").read()
    m = re.search(r"const ACTION_LABEL = \{.*?\n\};", js, re.S)
    assert m, "app.js 里没找到 ACTION_LABEL 定义"
    label_def = m.group(0)
    m = re.search(r"function humanEvent\(ev\) \{.*?\n\}", js, re.S)
    assert m, "app.js 里没找到 humanEvent 定义"
    fn_def = m.group(0)

    cases = [
        # 输入, 期望包含的子串（None = 只要求别原样透出 JSON）
        ('- {"type":"wait","seconds":3}', "等待 3s"),
        ('{"type":"think"}', "停顿"),
        ('- {"type":"click_id","res_id":"com.x:id/close"}', "点控件 com.x:id/close"),
        ('{"type":"any_of","branches":[{},{}]}', "分支 2 路"),
        ('- {"type":"click_first","candidates":[{"value":"福利"},{"value":"金币"}]}',
         "点首个命中 [福利/金币]"),
        ('- {"type":"reward","pattern":"\\\\|¥?([Pp]\\\\d+)"}', "读收益 匹配"),
        ("蛋花[watch](看视频) -> 行为[watch]", "蛋花[watch](看视频) -> 行为[watch]"),
    ]
    script = (label_def + "\n" + fn_def + "\n"
              + "const cases = JSON.parse(require('fs').readFileSync(0, 'utf8'));\n"
              + "const bad = [];\n"
              + "for (const [inp, want] of cases) {\n"
              + "  const got = humanEvent(inp);\n"
              + "  if (!got.includes(want) || /^[\\s\\-*\\u2022]*[[{]/.test(got))\n"
              + "    bad.push([inp, want, got]);\n"
              + "}\n"
              + "console.log(JSON.stringify(bad));\n")
    # 传参方式说明（Windows 实机坑，都踩过）：
    # 1) `node -e "多行脚本"` 会被 CreateProcess -> CommandLineToArgvW 把参数里的
    #    真实换行当空白分隔符拆 argv，node 只取 argv[1] -> 多行脚本被截断成首行；
    # 2) 压成单行后源码里的 `//` 注释会吞到行尾，把后续代码全注释掉；
    # 3) 用 argv 传 payload 时，参数一长（>~500 字符）node v26 在 Windows 上
    #    会启动期报 "The system cannot find the path specified."（命令行解析错乱）。
    # 所以：脚本写临时 .js 文件（保留原始换行/注释），payload 走 stdin（readFileSync(0)），
    # 完全绕开 Windows 命令行传参，任何长度/中文都稳。
    import tempfile
    fd, js_path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".js", prefix="human_event_")
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(script)
        p = subprocess.run(
            [node, js_path],
            input=json.dumps(cases, ensure_ascii=True).encode("utf-8"),
            capture_output=True, timeout=60)
    finally:
        os.unlink(js_path)
    if p.returncode != 0:
        try:
            err_txt = p.stderr.decode("utf-8")
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            err_txt = p.stderr.decode("gbk", errors="replace")
        assert False, f"node 执行失败：{err_txt[:400]}"
    bad = json.loads(p.stdout.decode("utf-8").strip() or "[]")
    assert not bad, "动作 JSON 未被翻译成人话：" + "；".join(
        f"{i!r} -> {g!r}（期望含 {w!r}）" for i, w, g in bad)
    print(f"OK  动作 JSON 翻译成人话（{len(cases)} 个用例，含 `- ` 前缀）")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_webui_yaml_api.py`（350 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_webui_yaml_api.py - WebUI YAML API（AUTOP-WEBUI-YAML-2026-10-22）

marvis A 卡会话 failed 零产物，由 Host 接手实现并补测试。
覆盖 6 路由 + 四道闸（YAML 可解析 / eol 保真 / 写后 verify / 自动回滚）+ 资金红线 + 词表门禁。

运行：
    ./.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest tests/test_webui_yaml_api.py -q
"""
import ast
import os
import sys
import tempfile

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

import yaml  # noqa: E402

from devtools import yaml_writer  # noqa: E402
from webui import routes_yaml  # noqa: E402

LF = """name: A
package: com.example.a
behaviors:
  collect:
  - type: click_point
    x: 0.5
    y: 0.5
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    desc: 图像-收集
"""
CRLF = LF.replace("\n", "\r\n")


class _Handler:
    """假 Handler：只记录 _json/_err，测试直接看返回值。"""

    def _json(self, obj, code=200):
        return code, obj

    def _err(self, code, msg):
        return code, {"ok": False, "error": msg}


def _mk(tmp, extra="name: B\npackage: com.example.b\nbehaviors:\n  sign:\n  - type: think\n"):
    apps = os.path.join(tmp, "config", "apps")
    os.makedirs(apps, exist_ok=True)
    with open(os.path.join(apps, "01_A.yaml"), "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
        f.write(LF)
    if extra:
        with open(os.path.join(apps, "02_B.yaml"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(extra)
    bak = os.path.join(tmp, "_backup_pre_fix")
    os.makedirs(bak, exist_ok=True)
    old_apps, old_bak = routes_yaml.APPS_DIR, yaml_writer.BACKUP_DIR
    routes_yaml.APPS_DIR = apps
    yaml_writer.BACKUP_DIR = bak
    return apps, old_apps, old_bak


def _restore(old_apps, old_bak):
    routes_yaml.APPS_DIR = old_apps
    yaml_writer.BACKUP_DIR = old_bak


def test_apps_lists_all():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        apps, oa, ob = _mk(tmp)
        try:
            code, body = routes_yaml.YamlRoutes(_Handler()).handle_get("/api/yaml/apps", {})
            assert code == 200 and body["ok"], body
            names = [a["name"] for a in body["apps"]]
            assert names == ["A", "B"], names
            assert body["apps"][1]["behaviors"] == ["sign"]
            assert all({"name", "file", "size", "mtime"} <= set(a) for a in body["apps"])
        finally:
            _restore(oa, ob)
    print("OK  GET /api/yaml/apps 列出全部 App 与 behaviors")


def test_app_text_returns_raw_and_eol():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        apps, oa, ob = _mk(tmp)
        try:
            r = routes_yaml.YamlRoutes(_Handler())
            code, body = r.handle_get("/api/yaml/app", {"name": ["A"]})
            assert code == 200 and body["eol"] == "\n", body
            assert body["text"] == LF
            # CRLF 文件
            p = os.path.join(apps, "03_C.yaml")
            with open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
                f.write(CRLF.replace("name: A", "name: C", 1))
            code, body = r.handle_get("/api/yaml/app", {"name": ["C"]})
            assert body["eol"] == "\r\n", body
        finally:
            _restore(oa, ob)
    print("OK  GET /api/yaml/app 返回原文 + eol（LF/CRLF 均可辨）")


def test_unknown_app_and_bad_name():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        _apps, oa, ob = _mk(tmp)
        try:
            r = routes_yaml.YamlRoutes(_Handler())
            code, body = r.handle_get("/api/yaml/app", {"name": ["不存在"]})
            assert code == 404, (code, body)
            code, body = r.handle_get("/api/yaml/app", {"name": ["../etc"]})
            assert code == 404 and "非法" in body["error"], (code, body)
        finally:
            _restore(oa, ob)
    print("OK  未知/非法 app 名返回 404")


def test_fragment_renders_behavior():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        _apps, oa, ob = _mk(tmp)
        try:
            r = routes_yaml.YamlRoutes(_Handler())
            code, body = r.handle_get("/api/yaml/fragment",
                                      {"name": ["A"], "behavior": ["collect"]})
            assert code == 200, body
            assert "collect:" in body["fragment"] and "- type: click_point" in body["fragment"]
            code, body = r.handle_get("/api/yaml/fragment",
                                      {"name": ["A"], "behavior": ["sign"]})
            assert code == 404, body
        finally:
            _restore(oa, ob)
    print("OK  GET /api/yaml/fragment 渲染指定行为")


def test_save_valid_and_unchanged():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        apps, oa, ob = _mk(tmp)
        try:
            r = routes_yaml.YamlRoutes(_Handler())
            code, body = r.handle_post("/api/yaml/app", {"name": "A", "text": LF})
            assert body["action"] == "unchanged", body
            new = LF.replace("desc: 图像-收集", "desc: 改过的描述")
            code, body = r.handle_post("/api/yaml/app", {"name": "A", "text": new})
            assert body["action"] == "saved" and body["backup"], body
            assert os.path.exists(body["backup"])
            assert yaml.safe_load(open(os.path.join(apps, "01_A.yaml"), encoding="utf-8")
                                  )["behaviors"]["collect"][0]["desc"] == "改过的描述"
            code, body = r.handle_post("/api/yaml/app", {"name": "A", "text": new})
            assert body["action"] == "unchanged", body
        finally:
            _restore(oa, ob)
    print("OK  保存：unchanged / saved / 备份落盘 / 二次保存 unchanged")


def test_save_rejects_bad_yaml_without_touching_file():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        apps, oa, ob = _mk(tmp)
        try:
            before = open(os.path.join(apps, "01_A.yaml"), "rb").read()
            r = routes_yaml.YamlRoutes(_Handler())
            code, body = r.handle_post("/api/yaml/app",
                                       {"name": "A", "text": "name: [unclosed"})
            assert code == 400 and "解析失败" in body["error"], (code, body)
            assert open(os.path.join(apps, "01_A.yaml"), "rb").read() == before
        finally:
            _restore(oa, ob)
    print("OK  非法 YAML 400 且文件未动")


def test_save_preserves_crlf_and_untouched_lines():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        apps, oa, ob = _mk(tmp)
        try:
            p = os.path.join(apps, "01_A.yaml")
            with open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
                f.write(CRLF)
            raw_before = open(p, "rb").read()
            r = routes_yaml.YamlRoutes(_Handler())
            # 浏览器 textarea 会把 CRLF 归一成 LF —— 服务端必须按原 eol 写回
            sent = CRLF.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("desc: 图像-收集", "desc: 改过")
            code, body = r.handle_post("/api/yaml/app", {"name": "A", "text": sent})
            assert body["action"] == "saved", body
            raw_after = open(p, "rb").read()
            assert b"\r\n" in raw_after, "CRLF 未被保持"
            assert raw_after.count(b"\n") == raw_after.count(b"\r\n")
            for ln in raw_before.split(b"\r\n"):
                if ln.strip() and "图像-收集".encode("utf-8") not in ln:
                    assert ln in raw_after, f"原行被改写: {ln!r}"
            assert "改过".encode("utf-8") in raw_after
        finally:
            _restore(oa, ob)
    print("OK  eol 保真：CRLF 文件保存后仍 CRLF，原行字节不变")


def test_save_rolls_back_on_collateral_damage():
    """写后 verify 发现误伤其他 App -> rolled_back 且文件恢复。"""
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        apps, oa, ob = _mk(tmp)
        try:
            p = os.path.join(apps, "01_A.yaml")
            original = open(p, "rb").read()
            r = routes_yaml.YamlRoutes(_Handler())
            real_verify = r._verify
            r._verify = lambda path, before: (False, ["模拟误伤"])
            code, body = r.handle_post("/api/yaml/app",
                                       {"name": "A", "text": LF.replace("x: 0.5", "x: 0.6")})
            assert body["action"] == "rolled_back", body
            assert open(p, "rb").read() == original, "回滚后文件未恢复"
            r._verify = real_verify
        finally:
            _restore(oa, ob)
    print("OK  复验失败自动回滚，文件字节恢复")


def _points(actions):
    """递归收集所有 click_point（它们嵌在 any_of.choices 里）。"""
    out = []
    for a in actions or []:
        if not isinstance(a, dict):
            continue
        if a.get("type") == "click_point":
            out.append(a)
        for ch in a.get("choices") or []:
            out.extend(_points(ch))
    return out


def test_generate_maps_coord_params():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        _apps, oa, ob = _mk(tmp)
        try:
            r = routes_yaml.YamlRoutes(_Handler())
            code, body = r.handle_post("/api/yaml/generate", {
                "name": "A", "behavior": "sign", "entry": "home_direct",
                "text": "点底部福利tab，点连续签到卡",
                "anchor_text": "福利", "anchor_xy": "0.5,0.927", "xy": "0.398,0.372",
            })
            assert code == 200, body
            assert body["entry"] == "home_direct", body["entry"]
            acts = body["actions"]
            assert acts and acts[0]["type"] == "any_of"
            pts = _points(acts)
            assert any(abs(p["x"] - 0.5) < 1e-6 and abs(p["y"] - 0.927) < 1e-6
                       for p in pts), pts
            assert any(abs(p["x"] - 0.398) < 1e-6 for p in pts), pts
            assert "candidates_used" in body and body["fragment"]
        finally:
            _restore(oa, ob)
    print("OK  generate：坐标字段映射进 actions，返回 fragment/candidates_used")


def test_generate_money_redline():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        _apps, oa, ob = _mk(tmp)
        try:
            r = routes_yaml.YamlRoutes(_Handler())
            code, body = r.handle_post("/api/yaml/generate", {
                "name": "A", "behavior": "withdraw", "text": "去提现"})
            assert code == 400 and "资金类" in body["error"], (code, body)
            code, body = r.handle_post("/api/yaml/generate", {
                "name": "A", "behavior": "withdraw", "text": "去提现",
                "allow_money": True})
            assert code == 200, body
        finally:
            _restore(oa, ob)
    print("OK  资金红线：withdraw 默认拒绝，allow_money 放行")


def test_apply_insert_then_skip_then_force():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        apps, oa, ob = _mk(tmp)
        try:
            r = routes_yaml.YamlRoutes(_Handler())
            code, gen = r.handle_post("/api/yaml/generate", {
                "name": "A", "behavior": "sign", "entry": "home_direct",
                "text": "点福利tab，点签到", "anchor_text": "福利",
                "anchor_xy": "0.5,0.927", "xy": "0.398,0.372"})
            actions = gen["actions"]
            code, body = r.handle_post("/api/yaml/apply",
                                       {"name": "A", "behavior": "sign", "actions": actions})
            assert body["action"] == "inserted", body
            data = yaml_writer.load_app(os.path.join(apps, "01_A.yaml"))
            assert data["behaviors"]["sign"] == actions
            code, body = r.handle_post("/api/yaml/apply",
                                       {"name": "A", "behavior": "sign", "actions": actions})
            assert body["action"] == "skipped", body
            # 换一组坐标再 force，才是真正的 replaced（相同内容按设计幂等 skip）
            code, gen2 = r.handle_post("/api/yaml/generate", {
                "name": "A", "behavior": "sign", "entry": "home_direct",
                "text": "点福利tab，点签到", "anchor_text": "福利",
                "anchor_xy": "0.5,0.927", "xy": "0.4,0.38"})
            code, body = r.handle_post("/api/yaml/apply",
                                       {"name": "A", "behavior": "sign",
                                        "actions": gen2["actions"], "force": True})
            assert body["action"] == "replaced", body
            data = yaml_writer.load_app(os.path.join(apps, "01_A.yaml"))
            assert data["behaviors"]["sign"] == gen2["actions"]
        finally:
            _restore(oa, ob)
    print("OK  apply：inserted → skipped → force 后 replaced")


def test_apply_by_text_only():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        apps, oa, ob = _mk(tmp)
        try:
            r = routes_yaml.YamlRoutes(_Handler())
            code, body = r.handle_post("/api/yaml/apply", {
                "name": "A", "behavior": "sign", "entry": "home_direct",
                "text": "点福利tab，点签到", "anchor_text": "福利",
                "anchor_xy": "0.5,0.927", "xy": "0.398,0.372"})

            assert "sign" in (yaml_writer.load_app(
                os.path.join(apps, "01_A.yaml")).get("behaviors") or {})
        finally:

    print("OK  apply 只传 text 也能生成并写入")


def test_non_owned_paths_return_none():
    r = routes_yaml.YamlRoutes(_Handler())
    assert r.handle_get("/api/health", {}) is None
    assert r.handle_get("/api/yaml/unknown", {}) is None
    assert r.handle_post("/api/jobs", {}) is None
    print("OK  非本模块路径返回 None（不影响原有分派）")


def test_vocab_gate_ast():
    src = open(os.path.join(ROOT, "webui", "routes_yaml.py"), encoding="utf-8").read()
    tree = ast.parse(src)
    lits = [n.value for n in ast.walk(tree)
            if isinstance(n, ast.Constant) and isinstance(n.value, str)]
    hits = [(i, w) for i, s in enumerate(lits) for w in
            ("今日签到", "立即签到", "任务中心", "连续签到", "签到领金币") if w in s]
    assert not hits, f"词表被复制进代码字面量: {hits[:3]}"
    print("OK  词表门禁：routes_yaml.py 字符串字面量无签到词表")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    fns = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("test_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in fns:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(fns) - failed}/{len(fns)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_window_gate.py`（173 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_window_gate.py - 第4步：behavior 时间窗（AUTOP-SELFSEARCH-2026-10-20 第4步）

用户原话：「常用步骤的共性」——签到/领现金类行为常有时效窗口（凌晨福利、
每日首签等）。支持 behaviors[bid] 新形态：

    behaviors:
      sign:
        window: {start: "00:00", end: "06:00"}   # 可选，24 小时制
        actions: [...]                            # 动作列表（旧 list 形态兼容）

规则：
  * window 缺失 / start/end 解析失败 -> 放行（不因配置问题堵死行为）；
  * start <= end   -> 窗口内 = start <= now <= end；
  * start >  end   -> 跨午夜窗口：now >= start 或 now <= end 均算窗口内；
  * 窗口外：记 WARN「跳过(BLOCKED)」，跳过该意图，不报错、不打断流程；
  * _in_window 支持注入 now="HH:MM" 便于测试；None 取当前时间。

运行：
    python -m pytest tests/test_window_gate.py -q
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from executor import Executor  # noqa: E402


class _Rec:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = []

    def log(self, event, app="", level="INFO"):
        self.lines.append(f"[{level}] {event}")


class _Human:
    def delay(self, *a):
        pass

    def maybe_wander(self, *a):
        pass

    def think(self):
        pass


def _mk():
    ex = Executor("fake-serial", _Human(), _Rec(), dry_run=True, config={})
    return ex


def t_parse_hhmm_valid_and_invalid():
    ex = _mk()
    assert ex._parse_hhmm("00:00") == 0
    assert ex._parse_hhmm("06:30") == 390
    assert ex._parse_hhmm("23:59") == 1439
    assert ex._parse_hhmm("23:59:59") == 1439
    assert ex._parse_hhmm("24:00") is None
    assert ex._parse_hhmm("6:5") is None
    assert ex._parse_hhmm("abc") is None
    assert ex._parse_hhmm(None) is None
    print("OK  _parse_hhmm 合法/非法")


def t_in_window_simple():
    ex = _mk()
    win = {"start": "00:00", "end": "06:00"}
    assert ex._in_window(win, now="00:00") is True
    assert ex._in_window(win, now="03:15") is True
    assert ex._in_window(win, now="06:00") is True
    assert ex._in_window(win, now="06:01") is False
    assert ex._in_window(win, now="12:00") is False
    assert ex._in_window(win, now="23:59") is False
    print("OK  普通窗口边界")


def t_cross_midnight_window():
    ex = _mk()
    win = {"start": "20:00", "end": "06:00"}
    assert ex._in_window(win, now="23:00") is True
    assert ex._in_window(win, now="02:00") is True
    assert ex._in_window(win, now="12:00") is False
    assert ex._in_window(win, now="19:59") is False
    print("OK  跨午夜窗口")


def t_no_window_or_bad_format_open():
    ex = _mk()
    assert ex._in_window(None, now="12:00") is True
    assert ex._in_window({}, now="12:00") is True
    assert ex._in_window({"start": "abc", "end": "xyz"}, now="12:00") is True
    assert ex._in_window({"start": "00:00", "end": "xyz"}, now="12:00") is True
    print("OK  window 缺失/解析失败均放行")


def t_behavior_actions_compat():
    ex = _mk()
    lst = [{"type": "think"}]
    assert ex._behavior_actions(lst) is lst, "list 形态原样返回"
    dct = {"window": {"start": "00:00", "end": "06:00"}, "actions": lst}
    assert ex._behavior_actions(dct) is lst, "dict 形态取 actions"
    assert ex._behavior_window(dct) == {"start": "00:00", "end": "06:00"}
    assert ex._behavior_window(lst) is None, "list 形态无 window"
    print("OK  behaviors[bid] 双形态兼容")


def t_run_app_flow_skips_blocked_dryrun():
    """dry-run：行为超窗 -> 记 BLOCKED 跳过，动作不打印，不报错。"""
    ex = _mk()
    cfg = {
        "name": "T", "package": "com.t",
        "intent_map": {"sign": "sign"},
        "behaviors": {"sign": {
            "window": {"start": "23:59", "end": "23:59"},   # 未来1分钟窗，当前必不在
            "actions": [{"type": "click_text", "text": "签到"}]}},
    }
    ex.run_app_flow(cfg, [("sign", "签到")])
    joined = "\n".join(ex.recorder.lines)
    assert "BLOCKED" in joined, joined
    # 动作行不应出现
    assert "- {\"type\": \"click_text\"" not in joined, joined
    print("OK  超窗行为被跳过（不报错、动作不打印）")


def t_run_app_flow_executes_in_window_dryrun():
    """dry-run：窗口内 -> 正常打印动作。"""
    ex = _mk()
    cfg = {
        "name": "T", "package": "com.t",
        "intent_map": {"sign": "sign"},
        "behaviors": {"sign": {
            "window": {"start": "00:00", "end": "23:59"},
            "actions": [{"type": "click_text", "text": "签到"}]}},
    }
    ex.run_app_flow(cfg, [("sign", "签到")])
    joined = "\n".join(ex.recorder.lines)
    assert "-> 行为[sign]" in joined, joined
    assert "BLOCKED" not in joined, joined
    print("OK  窗口内行为正常执行")


def t_run_app_flow_old_list_compat_dryrun():
    """dry-run：旧 list 形态（无 window）不受影响。"""
    ex = _mk()
    cfg = {
        "name": "T", "package": "com.t",
        "intent_map": {"sign": "sign"},
        "behaviors": {"sign": [{"type": "click_text", "text": "签到"}]},
    }
    ex.run_app_flow(cfg, [("sign", "签到")])
    joined = "\n".join(ex.recorder.lines)
    assert "-> 行为[sign]" in joined, joined
    assert "BLOCKED" not in joined, joined
    print("OK  旧 list 形态兼容")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_yaml_editor_static.py`（112 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_yaml_editor_static.py - 前端静态契约（AUTOP-WEBUI-YAML-2026-10-22）

marvis B 卡会话 failed 零产物，由 Host 接手实现并补测试。
不起服务，只做静态检查：文件存在、元素 ID 齐全、token 用法正确、端点齐全、无 CRLF 转换。

运行：
    ./.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest tests/test_yaml_editor_static.py -q
"""
import os
import re
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
STATIC = os.path.join(ROOT, "webui", "static")

REQUIRED_IDS = ["yStatus", "yAppFilter", "yAppList", "yBehavior", "ySave",
                "yReload", "yEditor", "yMsg", "yNlText", "yGen", "yApply",
                "yNlOut", "yForce"]
# 前端实际调用的 4 个；/api/yaml/fragment 由后端提供（按需查看单行为块），
# 编辑器走「整文件载入」所以前端不调它。
ENDPOINTS = ["/api/yaml/apps", "/api/yaml/app", "/api/yaml/generate",
             "/api/yaml/apply"]
BACKEND_ONLY = ["/api/yaml/fragment"]


def _read(name):
    with open(os.path.join(STATIC, name), encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return f.read()


def test_files_exist_and_nonempty():
    for name in ("yaml.html", "yaml_editor.js", "yaml_editor.css"):
        p = os.path.join(STATIC, name)
        assert os.path.isfile(p), f"缺少 {name}"
        assert os.path.getsize(p) > 200, f"{name} 内容过少（疑似占位）"
    print("OK  三个前端文件存在且非占位")


def test_html_has_required_ids():
    html = _read("yaml.html")
    missing = [i for i in REQUIRED_IDS if f'id="{i}"' not in html]
    assert not missing, f"yaml.html 缺少元素 ID: {missing}"
    assert 'href="/static/yaml_editor.css"' in html
    assert 'src="/static/yaml_editor.js"' in html
    assert "__CONSOLE_TOKEN__" in html, "yaml.html 必须保留 token 占位"
    print("OK  yaml.html 含全部元素 ID / 引入 css+js / 保留 token 占位")


def test_js_reads_token_from_meta():
    js = _read("yaml_editor.js")
    assert 'meta[name="console-token"]' in js, "必须从 meta 读 token"
    assert "X-Auth-Token" in js, "POST 必须带 X-Auth-Token"
    # 每个 POST fetch 都要经过 api()，而 api() 统一带 token 头
    posts = re.findall(r"method:\s*'POST'", js)
    assert len(posts) >= 3, f"POST 调用点过少: {len(posts)}"
    assert "const TOKEN =" in js, "token 需为模块级常量"
    print("OK  token 从 meta 读取，POST 统一带 X-Auth-Token")


def test_js_covers_all_endpoints():
    js = _read("yaml_editor.js")
    missing = [e for e in ENDPOINTS if e not in js]
    assert not missing, f"yaml_editor.js 未覆盖端点: {missing}"
    assert "/api/yaml/fragment" in open(
        os.path.join(ROOT, "webui", "routes_yaml.py"), encoding="utf-8").read(), \
        "后端应保留 fragment 端点"
    print("OK  前端覆盖 4 个端点；fragment 由后端保留")


def test_js_has_no_hardcoded_token():
    js = _read("yaml_editor.js")
    assert not re.search(r"TOKEN\s*=\s*['\"][0-9a-zA-Z]{8,}['\"]", js), "疑似写死 token"
    print("OK  无写死 token")


def test_js_does_not_convert_newlines():
    js = _read("yaml_editor.js")
    assert not re.search(r"replace\(/\\r\\n/g", js), "前端不应做 CRLF 转换（服务端负责 eol 保真）"
    assert not re.search(r"split\(['\"]\\\\n", js), "前端不应自行拆行"
    print("OK  前端不做换行转换")


def test_css_uses_variables_and_media_query():
    css = _read("yaml_editor.css")
    assert len(re.findall(r"var\(--", css)) >= 3, "应引用 app.css 的 CSS 变量"
    assert "@media" in css, "应有窄屏适配"
    assert "grid-template-columns" in css, "应为栅格布局"
    print("OK  css 引用变量 + 栅格 + 窄屏媒体查询")


def test_js_wires_all_controls():
    js = _read("yaml_editor.js")
    for cid in ("yAppFilter", "ySave", "yReload", "yEditor", "yGen", "yApply"):
        assert f"#{cid}" in js, f"未绑定 #{cid}"
    print("OK  6 个控件均已绑定事件")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    fns = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("test_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in fns:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(fns) - failed}/{len(fns)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_yaml_writer.py`（251 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_yaml_writer.py - 统一写回层（AUTOP-NL-2026-10-22）

锁定 7 条契约，重点是三条容易静默出错的：
  * 换行保真：CRLF 文件写回后仍是 CRLF，未修改行字节不变；
  * 幂等 + 不覆盖：已存在行为一律 skip，replace 需 force；
  * 写后复验与自动回滚。

全部用 tempfile 造 apps_dir，不碰真实 config/apps/。

运行：
    ./.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest tests/test_yaml_writer.py -q
"""
import os
import sys
import tempfile

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

import yaml  # noqa: E402

import devtools.yaml_writer as yw  # noqa: E402

LF_BODY = """name: A
package: com.example.a
behaviors:
  collect:
  - type: click_point
    x: 0.5
    y: 0.5
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    desc: 图像-收集
"""

CRLF_BODY = LF_BODY.replace("\n", "\r\n")


def _mkroot(body: str = LF_BODY, extra_app: str | None = None) -> str:
    root = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="yw_")
    apps = os.path.join(root, "config", "apps")
    os.makedirs(apps)
    with open(os.path.join(root, "config.yaml"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("apps: []\napps_dir: config/apps\n")
    with open(os.path.join(apps, "01_A.yaml"), "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
        f.write(body)
    if extra_app:
        with open(os.path.join(apps, "02_B.yaml"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(extra_app)
    return root


ACTIONS = [
    {"type": "click_first", "candidates_from": "sign", "timeout": 8, "optional": True},
    {"type": "think"},
]


def t_read_text_detects_eol():
    root = _mkroot(CRLF_BODY)
    lines, eol, ends_nl = yw.read_text(os.path.join(root, "config", "apps", "01_A.yaml"))
    assert eol == "\r\n", repr(eol)
    assert ends_nl is True
    assert lines[0] == "name: A"
    print("OK  读文件能识别 CRLF")


def t_insert_preserves_crlf_and_comments():
    root = _mkroot(CRLF_BODY)
    path = os.path.join(root, "config", "apps", "01_A.yaml")
    before = open(path, "rb").read()
    res = yw.insert_behavior(path, "sign", ACTIONS, comments=["测试注释"], apply=True,
                             base_dir=root)
    assert res.action == "inserted", (res.action, res.warnings)
    after = open(path, "rb").read()
    assert b"\r\n" in after and after.count(b"\n") == after.count(b"\r\n"), "写回把 CRLF 改成了 LF"
    assert "测试注释".encode("utf-8") in after
    # 未修改行的字节序列必须原样保留
    for ln in before.split(b"\r\n"):
        if ln.strip():
            assert ln in after, f"原行丢失或被改写: {ln!r}"
    print("OK  插入后 CRLF 保持、注释保留、原行字节不变")


def t_insert_is_idempotent():
    root = _mkroot()
    path = os.path.join(root, "config", "apps", "01_A.yaml")
    r1 = yw.insert_behavior(path, "sign", ACTIONS, apply=True, base_dir=root)
    assert r1.action == "inserted", r1.warnings
    r2 = yw.insert_behavior(path, "sign", ACTIONS, apply=True, base_dir=root)
    assert r2.action == "skipped", r2.action
    print("OK  重复插入 -> skipped（幂等）")


def t_insert_skips_existing_behavior():
    root = _mkroot()
    path = os.path.join(root, "config", "apps", "01_A.yaml")
    res = yw.insert_behavior(path, "collect", ACTIONS, apply=True, base_dir=root)
    assert res.action == "skipped", res.action
    print("OK  已存在行为不覆盖，提示改用 replace")


def t_replace_requires_force():
    root = _mkroot()
    path = os.path.join(root, "config", "apps", "01_A.yaml")
    res = yw.replace_behavior(path, "collect", ACTIONS, apply=True, base_dir=root)
    assert res.action == "skipped" and "force" in res.warnings[0], res.warnings
    res2 = yw.replace_behavior(path, "collect", ACTIONS, apply=True, force=True, base_dir=root)
    assert res2.action == "replaced", (res2.action, res2.warnings)
    print("OK  replace 需 force；force 后替换成功")


def t_replace_span_does_not_swallow_next_behavior():
    body = LF_BODY + "  watch:\n  - type: think\n"
    root = _mkroot(body)
    path = os.path.join(root, "config", "apps", "01_A.yaml")
    yw.replace_behavior(path, "collect", [{"type": "think"}], apply=True, force=True, base_dir=root)
    app = yaml.safe_load(open(path, encoding="utf-8"))
    assert app["behaviors"]["collect"] == [{"type": "think"}], app["behaviors"]["collect"]
    assert app["behaviors"]["watch"] == [{"type": "think"}], "替换吞掉了下一个行为"
    print("OK  替换不越界，后续行为完好")


def t_backup_naming_and_location():
    root = _mkroot()
    path = os.path.join(root, "config", "apps", "01_A.yaml")
    res = yw.insert_behavior(path, "sign", ACTIONS, apply=True, base_dir=root)
    assert res.backup and os.path.exists(res.backup)
    name = os.path.basename(res.backup)
    assert name.startswith("01_A.pre_NL_") and name.endswith(".yaml"), name
    assert os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(res.backup)) == "_backup_pre_fix"
    print("OK  备份落在 _backup_pre_fix/，命名含 pre_NL_<ts>")


def t_preview_does_not_touch_disk():
    root = _mkroot()
    path = os.path.join(root, "config", "apps", "01_A.yaml")
    before = open(path, "rb").read()
    res = yw.insert_behavior(path, "sign", ACTIONS, apply=False, base_dir=root)
    assert res.action == "preview"
    assert open(path, "rb").read() == before, "预览模式不应写盘"
    assert not os.path.exists(os.path.join(root, "_backup_pre_fix"))
    assert "- type: click_first" in res.diff
    print("OK  缺省预览：不写盘、不产生备份、返回 diff")


def t_verify_detects_missing_bid():
    root = _mkroot()
    path = os.path.join(root, "config", "apps", "01_A.yaml")
    ok, problems = yw.verify(path, {}, "sign", ACTIONS, base_dir=root)
    assert not ok and any("缺失" in p for p in problems), problems
    print("OK  verify 检出目标行为缺失")


def t_verify_detects_collateral_damage():
    root = _mkroot(extra_app="name: B\npackage: com.example.b\nbehaviors:\n  sign:\n  - type: think\n")
    path = os.path.join(root, "config", "apps", "01_A.yaml")
    before = yw.snapshot(os.path.join(root, "config.yaml"), base_dir=root)
    assert "A" in before and "B" in before, before.keys()
    # 人为改动 B，模拟误伤
    pb = os.path.join(root, "config", "apps", "02_B.yaml")
    with open(pb, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("name: B\npackage: com.example.b\nbehaviors:\n  sign:\n  - type: wait\n")
    ok, problems = yw.verify(path, before, "collect", None,
                             config_path=os.path.join(root, "config.yaml"), base_dir=root)
    assert not ok and any("误伤" in p for p in problems), problems
    print("OK  verify 检出误伤其他 App")


def t_rollback_when_verify_fails():
    root = _mkroot()
    path = os.path.join(root, "config", "apps", "01_A.yaml")
    original = open(path, "rb").read()
    real_verify = yw.verify
    yw.verify = lambda *a, **k: (False, ["模拟复验失败"])
    try:
        res = yw.insert_behavior(path, "sign", ACTIONS, apply=True, base_dir=root)
    finally:
        yw.verify = real_verify
    assert res.action == "rolled_back", res.action
    assert open(path, "rb").read() == original, "复验失败后未回滚"
    print("OK  复验失败自动回滚，文件恢复原字节")


def t_rejects_config_yaml_and_outside_paths():
    root = _mkroot()
    try:
        yw.insert_behavior(os.path.join(root, "config.yaml"), "sign", ACTIONS, base_dir=root)
    except yw.WriterError as e:
        assert "config.yaml" in str(e)
    else:
        raise AssertionError("应拒绝写 config.yaml")
    try:
        yw.insert_behavior(os.path.join(root, "elsewhere.yaml"), "sign", ACTIONS, base_dir=root)
    except yw.WriterError:
        pass
    else:
        raise AssertionError("应拒绝写 config/apps 之外的路径")
    print("OK  路径白名单生效")


def t_render_roundtrip_with_tricky_chars():
    actions = [
        {"type": "click_first",
         "candidates": [{"type": "text", "value": "连续签到：领+50%"}, {"type": "text", "value": "签到"}],
         "timeout": 8, "optional": False},
        {"type": "any_of", "optional": True,
         "choices": [[{"type": "click_point", "x": 0.5, "y": 0.641, "vision": False}]]},
    ]
    lines = yw.render_actions(actions, indent=2)
    text = "\n".join(ln[2:] if ln.startswith("  ") else ln for ln in lines)
    back = yaml.safe_load(text)
    assert back == actions, back
    assert any(ln.strip().startswith("- - type:") for ln in lines), "any_of 嵌套应为 `- - type:`"
    print("OK  渲染 round-trip 一致，全角冒号/+/% 不炸")


def t_indent4_old_single_file():
    body = ("- name: C\n  package: com.example.c\n  behaviors:\n"
            "    collect:\n    - type: think\n")
    root = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="yw4_")
    apps = os.path.join(root, "config", "apps")
    os.makedirs(apps)
    with open(os.path.join(root, "config.yaml"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("apps: []\napps_dir: config/apps\n")
    path = os.path.join(apps, "03_C.yaml")
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(body)
    res = yw.insert_behavior(path, "sign", ACTIONS, apply=True, base_dir=root)
    assert res.action == "inserted", (res.action, res.warnings)
    app = yw.load_app(path)  # 旧式 `- name:` 会加载成 list，用 load_app 归一化
    assert app["behaviors"]["sign"] == ACTIONS
    assert app["behaviors"]["collect"] == [{"type": "think"}]
    print("OK  旧单文件 ind=4 结构插入正确")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    fns = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in fns:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(fns) - failed}/{len(fns)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `tests/test_zone_pick.py`（205 行）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""test_zone_pick.py - 第3步：位置先验 zone 权重（AUTOP-SELFSEARCH-2026-10-20 第3步）

用户原话：「按钮名称、按钮位置、常用步骤的共性」——候选文案在多处匹配时，
位置本身就是先验：底部导航带的 tab 类按钮、右上角/右缘的浮球类按钮都应加分。

三区（常量在 pick_locate.py）：
  * tab_zone   ：bottom band（top >= ZONE_BOTTOM_BAND*screen_h），+150/-150；
  * top_right  ：顶部右上（top <= ZONE_TOP_BAND*screen_h 且 x 中心靠右），+150/-150；
  * right_edge ：右缘（x 中心 >= ZONE_RIGHT_RATIO*screen_w），+100/-100。

规则：
  * 未知屏高/屏宽（0）→ 该 zone 不做任何加分（不猜位置）；
  * 条目缺坐标（left/right 或 top 缺失）→ 该条目 zone 加分跳过（中性，不惩罚）；
  * 未知 zone 值 → 忽略（不影响既有 nav 带加分与拒配）；
  * 纯函数旧签名兼容：pick_best(entries, target, kind, screen_h, log) 仍可用。

运行：
    python -m pytest tests/test_zone_pick.py -q
"""
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from pick_locate import (pick_best, ZONE_BOTTOM_BAND, ZONE_TOP_BAND,  # noqa: E402
                         ZONE_RIGHT_RATIO)
from executor import Executor  # noqa: E402


class _Info(dict):
    pass


class _Obj:
    def __init__(self, info):
        self.info = _Info(info)


class _Objs:
    def __init__(self, infos):
        self._infos = [_Info(i) for i in infos]

    @property
    def count(self):
        return len(self._infos)

    def __getitem__(self, i):
        return _Obj(self._infos[i])

    @property
    def info(self):
        return self._infos[0]


class _Rec:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = []

    def log(self, event, app="", level="INFO"):
        self.lines.append(f"[{level}] {event}")


SCREEN_H, SCREEN_W = 2340, 1080


def t_tab_zone_prefers_bottom():
    """tab_zone：底部带条目（含 +150）胜过中部可点击条目（-150）。"""
    entries = [
        {"label": "任务", "clickable": True, "top": 600,
         "left": 0, "right": 300},
        {"label": "任务", "clickable": False, "top": 2300,
         "left": 0, "right": 300},
    ]
    idx = pick_best(entries, "任务", "text", SCREEN_H, zone="tab_zone")
    assert idx == 1, f"应选底部带条目，实际 {idx}"
    assert int(ZONE_BOTTOM_BAND * SCREEN_H) <= 2300, "fixture 应落在底部带内"
    print("OK  tab_zone 底部带条目胜出")


def t_tab_zone_no_zone_tie_keeps_first():
    """无 zone：两完全相等可点击节点平局取先（旧行为回归保护）。"""
    entries = [
        {"label": "任务", "clickable": True, "top": 600},
        {"label": "任务", "clickable": True, "top": 2260},
    ]
    assert pick_best(entries, "任务", "text", SCREEN_H) == 0
    print("OK  无 zone 时平局取先（不引入位置偏见）")


def t_top_right_prefers_corner():
    """top_right：右上角条目胜过左上角同文案。"""
    entries = [
        {"label": "领现金", "clickable": True, "top": 300,
         "left": 900, "right": 1060},
        {"label": "领现金", "clickable": True, "top": 300,
         "left": 100, "right": 260},
    ]
    idx = pick_best(entries, "领现金", "text", SCREEN_H,
                    screen_w=SCREEN_W, zone="top_right")
    assert idx == 0, f"应选右上角条目，实际 {idx}"
    print("OK  top_right 右上角条目胜出")


def t_right_edge_prefers_right():
    """right_edge：右缘条目胜过左侧同文案。"""
    entries = [
        {"label": "红包", "clickable": True, "top": 1200,
         "left": 900, "right": 1060},
        {"label": "红包", "clickable": True, "top": 1200,
         "left": 100, "right": 260},
    ]
    idx = pick_best(entries, "红包", "text", SCREEN_H,
                    screen_w=SCREEN_W, zone="right_edge")
    assert idx == 0, f"应选右缘条目，实际 {idx}"
    print("OK  right_edge 右缘条目胜出")


def t_zone_ignored_when_screen_unknown():
    """未知屏高/屏宽：zone 不做加分，平局取先。"""
    entries = [
        {"label": "任务", "clickable": True, "top": 600,
         "left": 0, "right": 300},
        {"label": "任务", "clickable": True, "top": 2260,
         "left": 0, "right": 300},
    ]
    assert pick_best(entries, "任务", "text", 0,
                     screen_w=0, zone="tab_zone") == 0
    assert pick_best(entries, "任务", "text", SCREEN_H,
                     screen_w=0, zone="right_edge") == 0
    print("OK  未知屏高/屏宽时 zone 无加分")


def t_zone_missing_coords_no_crash():
    """条目缺坐标：该条目 zone 加分跳过，不崩、不误惩罚。"""
    entries = [
        {"label": "任务", "clickable": True, "top": 600},
        {"label": "任务", "clickable": True, "top": 2260},
    ]
    assert pick_best(entries, "任务", "text", SCREEN_H, screen_w=SCREEN_W,
                     zone="right_edge") == 0
    assert pick_best(entries, "任务", "text", SCREEN_H, screen_w=SCREEN_W,
                     zone="top_right") == 0
    print("OK  缺坐标条目 zone 加分中性化")


def t_unknown_zone_ignored():
    """未知 zone 值：忽略，不影响既有择优。"""
    entries = [
        {"label": "任务", "clickable": True, "top": 600},
        {"label": "任务", "clickable": True, "top": 2260},
    ]
    assert pick_best(entries, "任务", "text", SCREEN_H, screen_w=SCREEN_W,
                     zone="bogus") == 0
    print("OK  未知 zone 值忽略")


def t_legacy_signature_log_positional():
    """旧签名兼容：pick_best(entries, target, kind, screen_h, log) 仍可用。"""
    lines = []
    entries = [
        {"label": "签到", "clickable": False, "top": 100},
        {"label": "签到", "clickable": True, "top": 100},
    ]
    idx = pick_best(entries, "签到", "text", SCREEN_H,
                    lambda e, app="", level="INFO": lines.append((level, e)))
    assert idx == 1, "可点击条目应胜出（旧签名下位置参数仍解析为 log）"
    assert any("择优" in m for _l, m in lines), lines
    print("OK  旧签名兼容（第5个位置参数仍是 log）")


def t_executor_pick_best_passes_zone_and_screen_w():
    """executor._pick_best 透传 zone/screen_w：tab_zone 选中底部条目。"""
    ex = Executor.__new__(Executor)
    ex.recorder = _Rec()

    class _D:
        info = {"displayHeight": 2340, "displayWidth": 1080}
    ex.d = _D()
    obj = _Objs([
        {"text": "任务", "clickable": True,
         "bounds": {"left": 0, "top": 600, "right": 300, "bottom": 700}},
        {"text": "任务", "clickable": True,
         "bounds": {"left": 0, "top": 2300, "right": 300, "bottom": 2400}},
    ])
    best = ex._pick_best(obj, "任务", "text", zone="tab_zone")
    assert best.info["bounds"]["top"] == 2300, best.info
    print("OK  executor._pick_best 透传 zone/screen_w")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tests = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith("t_")]
    failed = 0
    for fn in tests:
        try:
            fn()
        except AssertionError as e:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {fn.__name__}: {e}")
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed += 1
            print(f"ERROR {fn.__name__}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    print(f"\n{len(tests) - failed}/{len(tests)} 通过")
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)
```

### `config/apps/01_趣头条.yaml`（141 行）

```yaml
name: 趣头条
package: com.jifen.qukan
aliases:
- 趣头条
- 趣看天下
- 趣闻
- 新闻赚金币
intent_map:
  sign: sign
  watch: watch
  withdraw: withdraw
behaviors:
  sign:
  # 【用户人工描述 2026-10-20】需关好几个弹窗才进正式界面；底部5按钮右下=我的、右二=任务；任务界面中部最左=今日签到
  # 多轮弹窗清扫（F1 dismiss_popups 上线后由全局清扫接管，此处为过渡手写版）
  - type: any_of
    optional: true
    choices:
    - - type: click_first
        candidates:
        - type: text
          value: 暂不
        - type: text
          value: 关闭
        - type: text
          value: 以后再说
        - type: text
          value: 稍后再说
        - type: text
          value: 我知道了
        - type: text
          value: 知道了
        - type: text
          value: 不再提醒
        - type: text
          value: 取消
        timeout: 3
  - type: any_of
    optional: true
    choices:
    - - type: click_first
        candidates:
        - type: text
          value: 暂不
        - type: text
          value: 关闭
        - type: text
          value: 以后再说
        - type: text
          value: 我知道了
        - type: text
          value: 知道了
        timeout: 3
  # 底部右二 tab「任务」
  - type: any_of
    optional: true
    choices:
    - - type: click_first
        candidates:
        - type: text
          value: 任务
        candidates_from: nav_tab
        zone: tab_zone
        timeout: 8
        optional: false
    - - type: click_point
        x: 0.7
        y: 0.914
        timeout: 8
        optional: false
        vision: false
        desc: 底部右二tab「任务」坐标兜底（待真机校准）
  - type: think
  # 任务界面中部最左「今日签到」——纯词表自搜寻（全局 SIGN 前缀同序）
  - type: any_of
    optional: true
    choices:
    - - type: click_first
        candidates_from: sign
        timeout: 8
        optional: false
    - - type: click_point
        x: 0.16
        y: 0.45
        timeout: 8
        optional: false
        vision: false
        desc: 任务界面中部最左「今日签到」坐标兜底（待真机校准）
  watch:
  - type: click_first
    candidates:
    - type: text
      value: 看视频领金币
    - type: text
      value: 体验领金币
    - type: text
      value: 领50金币
    - type: text
      value: 视频
    timeout: 8
    optional: true
  - type: think
  - type: reward
    pattern: 看视频[^0-9]*(?P<amount>[0-9]+)\s*(?P<unit>金币)
    unit: 金币
    name: 看视频
  - type: reward
    pattern: \+\s*(?P<amount>[0-9]+)\s*(?P<unit>金币)
    unit: 金币
    name: 看视频加奖
  - type: swipe
    from:
    - 0.5
    - 0.7
    to:
    - 0.5
    - 0.3
    duration: 0.8
    natural: true
  - type: wait
    seconds: 3
  withdraw:
  - type: click_text
    text: 我的
    timeout: 8
  - type: think
  - type: click_text
    text: 提现兑换
    timeout: 6
  - type: ensure
    text: 我要提现
    exists: 'yes'
    timeout: 5
  - type: reward
    pattern: 约(?P<amount>[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*(?P<unit>元)
    unit: 元
    name: 可提现余额
  - type: click_text
    text: 立即提现
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
```

### `config/apps/02_拼多多.yaml`（98 行）

```yaml
name: 拼多多
package: com.xunmeng.pinduoduo
aliases:
- 拼多多
behaviors:
  watch:
  - type: click_point
    images:
    - images/拼多多/e4417c6d-5e2a-4a24-a172-589357b264c5.png
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    desc: 图像-拼多多-任务界面-多多视频
  collect:
  - type: click_point
    images:
    - images/拼多多/5c996370-94cc-4137-97f9-da361fd51e9b.png
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    desc: 图像-拼多多-任务界面-余额
  withdraw:
  - type: click_point
    images:
    - images/拼多多/526d1934-9de7-4322-9f64-0834118bc519.png
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    desc: 图像-拼多多-提现-立即提现
  sign:
  # 【用户人工描述 2026-10-20】左下数第2 tab=多多视频；其上部最右边缘圆形=签到/提现共用入口；入口任务列表没有签到就滑动；入口上部右侧=提现（暂略）
  # 注意：签到入口禁止截图 → 只用文案+归一化坐标，不用图像模板
  - type: any_of
    optional: true
    choices:
    - - type: click_first
        candidates:
        - type: text
          value: 多多视频
        candidates_from: nav_tab
        zone: tab_zone
        timeout: 8
        optional: false
    - - type: click_point
        x: 0.3
        y: 0.914
        timeout: 8
        optional: false
        vision: false
        desc: 底部左二tab「多多视频」坐标兜底
        expect:
          phash: images/拼多多/landing/ref_video.png
          phash_max_dist: 8
          retry: 1
  - type: think
  # 2026-10-23 真机重校准：圆形金币入口实测在 (0.856,0.167)。
  # 旧值 (0.93,0.12) 偏 80×110px，会静默走到错误页面（整条链后续全错）。
  # 该页无「签到」二字、可点节点仅 22 个（纯自绘），文案/节点信号全部失效，
  # 靠 dHash 落地校验兜底（同页距离 0，跨页 12~33，阈值 8 有干净间隔）。
  - type: click_point
    x: 0.856
    y: 0.167
    timeout: 6
    optional: false
    vision: false
    desc: 多多视频页右上缘圆形金币入口（2026-10-23 真机校准）
    expect:
      phash: images/拼多多/landing/ref_coin.png
      phash_max_dist: 8
      retry: 1
  # 金币中心任务列表里没有「签到」二字：签到是顶部 7 天连签条上的「今日可领」卡片，
  # 需要上滑才会露出来（旧配置 scroll_to text=签到 永远找不到，实测滚动 4 页仍无该词）。
  - type: swipe
    from: [0.5, 0.78]
    to: [0.5, 0.35]
    duration: 0.4
  - type: swipe
    from: [0.5, 0.78]
    to: [0.5, 0.35]
    duration: 0.4
  - type: click_point
    x: 0.144
    y: 0.505
    timeout: 6
    optional: false
    vision: false
    desc: 金币中心7天连签条「今日可领」卡片（2026-10-23 真机校准）
    expect:
      phash: images/拼多多/landing/ref_sign.png
      phash_max_dist: 8
      retry: 1
  - type: click_point
    x: 0.5
    y: 0.628
    timeout: 6
    optional: false
    vision: false
    desc: 每日签到弹窗「领取今日现金」按钮（2026-10-23 真机校准）
    expect:
      phash: images/拼多多/landing/ref_done.png
      phash_max_dist: 8
```

```yaml
name: 京东

  # 旧配置两个错：
  #   1) (0.3,0.914)=(324,2139) 落在内容区——真 tab 栏在 y≈2100，逛 的真实中心是 **(330,2105)**；
  #   2) 假设「逛页右侧浮动金币胶囊 (990,559)」——该胶囊不存在，进逛页会自动弹金币面板。
  # 真实链路：逛 tab → 自动弹「金币面板」→「连续签到7天」第 N 天 [签到领] (259,1008)。
  # 实测 2026-10-24：现金 20.08 → 20.12 元，第 2 天打勾、第 3 天转「明日可领」。
  # 落地校验用文案（本页暴露「赚现金 / 领金币」两个胶囊标签）。

    expect:

    x: 0.24
    y: 0.431

    timeout: 8
    optional: true

    timeout: 6
    optional: true
```

### `config/apps/04_美团.yaml`（95 行）

```yaml
name: 美团
package: com.sankuai.meituan
aliases:
- 美团
- 美团外卖
- 美图
launch_reset: true   # 视频 tab 入口需从首页冷启动
behaviors:
  sign:
  # 2026-10-24 真机重校准（用户指正：入口是底部【视频】tab，和拼多多同构）。
  # 旧配置第 3 步「视频页右上缘圆形共用入口」坐标写成了 (0.3,0.914)——与底部 tab 完全相同，
  # 属复制粘贴错误，等于原地再点一次 tab，所以永远「动作未命中」。
  # 真实链路：底部【视频】tab (324,2105) → 视频页右上角圆形徽标 **(994,477)**
  #      → 「赚钱中心」→「今日签到 [去签到]」(231,650)。
  # 徽标内容会在「71488金币」和「495元」之间切换，但位置固定。
  # 🔴 阻塞（2026-10-24 实测）：点[去签到]后弹「连签7天必得55.4万金币」，
  #   选[放弃赚更多 领48988] 会再弹「仅差1步得组件专属权益」，选[放弃权益] 反而
  #   拉起**系统桌面小组件安装器**，返回键退不出，只能 force-stop。
  #   金币 71488 未变 → 签到是否入账无法确认。故最后一步 optional，留人工。
  - type: click_point
    x: 0.3
    y: 0.900
    timeout: 8
    optional: false
    vision: false
    desc: 底部【视频】tab（真机中心 (324,2105)）
    expect:
      # 视频页内容是随机视频，整页 phash 必不匹配（实测同页 33）。
      # 改用文案：视频页独有的「倍速 / 上滑继续看视频」，与首页可区分。
      text_any: [倍速, 上滑继续看视频]
      settle: 3.0
      retry: 1
  - type: think
  # 视频页右上角圆形金币徽标（内容会变：金币/现金）
  - type: click_point
    x: 0.92
    y: 0.204
    timeout: 8
    optional: false
    vision: false
    desc: 视频页右上角圆形金币徽标（真机中心 (994,477)）
    expect:
      phash: images/美团/landing/ref_center.png
      phash_max_dist: 12
      settle: 3.5
      retry: 1
  - type: think
  # 赚钱中心「今日签到」[去签到]
  - type: click_point
    x: 0.214
    y: 0.278
    images:
    - images/美团/btn_qiandao.png
    timeout: 8
    optional: true
    desc: 赚钱中心[去签到]（图像锚点+坐标兜底）；后续弹窗会推桌面组件，见上方阻塞说明
  withdraw:
  - type: click_point
    images:
    - images/美团/7aeb4e5b-dbae-4a18-8dde-6ba9ea09f126.png
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    desc: 图像-美团-提现-活动钱包
  - type: click_point
    images:
    - images/美团/eb3599b0-882d-4c1e-907d-6b498576c8a1.png
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    desc: 图像-美团-提现-提现到美团钱包
  - type: click_point
    images:
    - images/美团/1ca55c04-27a2-4bda-bbc6-1f58970ffc9f.png
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    desc: 图像-美团-提现-提现
  collect:
  - type: click_point
    images:
    - images/美团/9c28a301-b81e-4b27-8795-e25d3d21e9be.png
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    desc: 图像-美团-任务界面-换现金
  - type: click_point
    images:
    - images/美团/62a94570-7063-4599-9739-2d707b85eb66.png
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    desc: 图像-美团-任务列表-关闭
  watch:
  - type: click_point
    images:
    - images/美团/32c261fe-128b-4dd9-b15c-c56a71478108.png
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    desc: 图像-美团-任务界面-视频
```

```yaml
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    desc: 图像-快手-点宝箱-关闭
  sign:
  # 2026-10-24 真机重校准。旧链路：☰侧边栏 → 任务中心 → scroll_to「签到」。
  # 但任务中心是 H5 页面（dump 仅 17 节点、无任何文案），scroll_to text=签到 永远失败。
  # 真实链路：☰(76,140) → 侧边栏右侧「任务中心」磁贴(689,376)
  #      → 「连续签到14天得0.4元」行 [立即签到](924,793) → 弹窗 [立即签到](540,1480)。
  # 实测 2026-10-24 已连签 5/14 → 6/14 成功，明日可领 688 金币。
  - type: click_point
    x: 0.07
    y: 0.06
    timeout: 6
    optional: false
    vision: false
    desc: 首页左上角☰打开侧边栏
  - type: think
  - type: any_of
    optional: false
    choices:
    - - type: click_first
        candidates:
        - type: desc
          value: 任务中心
        - type: text
          value: 任务中心
        timeout: 8
        optional: false
    - - type: click_point
        x: 0.638
        y: 0.161
        timeout: 8
        optional: false
        vision: false
        desc: 侧边栏「任务中心」磁贴（真机中心 (689,376)，旧值 0.5/0.18 偏左错过）
        expect:
          phash: images/快手/landing/ref_task.png
          phash_max_dist: 8
          settle: 3.0
          retry: 1
  - type: think
  # 任务中心「连续签到14天得0.4元」行的 [立即签到]
  - type: click_point
    x: 0.856
    y: 0.339
    timeout: 6
    optional: false
    vision: false
    desc: 任务中心[立即签到]（真机中心 (924,793)）
    expect:
      # 弹窗内容会变（288→688、今日→明日），整页前后差 7，故阈值放宽到 10；
      # 弹窗 vs 任务页 30，仍有干净间隔。
      phash: images/快手/landing/ref_dialog.png
      phash_max_dist: 10
      settle: 3.0
      retry: 1
  # 弹窗内 [立即签到]：点完 288 金币入账、连签 5/14 → 6/14
  - type: click_point
    x: 0.5
    y: 0.632
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    vision: false
    desc: 签到弹窗[立即签到]（真机中心 (540,1480)）
  - type: reward
    pattern: 今日签到可领\s*(?P<amount>[0-9]+)\s*(?P<unit>金币)
    unit: 金币
    name: 签到金币
  withdraw:
  - type: click_point
    images:
    - images/快手/f1ab7643-c156-4612-ae98-8fb425475b29.png
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    desc: 图像-快手-提现-领现金
  - type: click_point
    images:
    - images/快手/9e4c2242-f684-4574-afdc-e7df073e5363.png
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    desc: 图像-快手-提现-立即兑换
  - type: click_point
    images:
    - images/快手/77b164cc-12ca-410f-a0e4-ae9f48460d91.png
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    desc: 图像-快手-提现-立即提现
  collect:
  - type: click_point
    images:
    - images/快手/0100ec21-8be7-4552-b456-e20e7f831f39.png
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    desc: 图像-快手-任务列表-关闭
  # 2026-10-25 整理：原中文 behavior 名「白拿礼物」收编为标准意图名 gift
  # （NLU 侧已注册 gift 关键词：白拿礼物/白拿/免费领/领礼物）。
  # 中文 behavior 名无法被任何标准意图命中，只能靠 intent_map 手写映射。
  gift:
  - type: click_point
    images:
    - images/快手/dc7e72b7-ba53-4572-aafa-052262c440d3.png
    timeout: 6
    optional: true
    desc: 图像-快手-白拿礼物-去领取
```
